585 |             _fmt(s.mean_disclosure_delay),
586 |             _fmt(s.mean_fire_delay),
587 |             _fmt(s.mean_gap),
588 |             _level_name(s.finest_disclosed_level),
589 |         ]
590 |         print("  ".join(f"{value:>{width}}" for value, (_, width) in zip(row, columns)))
591 | 
592 |     dupes = adjacent_duplicate_rows(summaries)
593 |     if dupes:
594 |         pairs = ", ".join(f"{a:g}<->{b:g}" for a, b in dupes)
595 |         print(
596 |             f"    NOTE: adjacent settings produced identical rows ({pairs}). "
597 |             f"{sweep.param_name} acts on integer counts; these values did not "
598 |             f"cross a discrete boundary between them."
599 |         )
600 |     print()
601 | 
602 | 
603 | def main() -> None:
604 |     results = run_disclosure_sweeps()
605 |     n_seeds = next(iter(results.sweeps.values()))[0].n_runs
606 |     bar = "=" * 78
607 | 
608 |     print(bar)
609 |     print(f"{n_seeds} seeds, {DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS}-day runs, default structure, DETECTION FIXED AT DEFAULTS")
610 |     print(f"established = cumulative infections >= {ESTABLISHED_MIN_INFECTIONS} (ESTABLISHED_MIN_INFECTIONS)")
611 |     print(bar)
612 |     print()
613 |     print(f"  FIZZLE RATE (outbreak never established) :  {results.fizzle_rate * 100:.1f}%   "
614 |           f"({results.non_established}/{n_seeds} runs)")
615 |     print()
616 |     fa = "n/a" if results.false_alarm_rate is None else f"{results.false_alarm_rate * 100:.1f}%"
617 |     print(f"  >>> FALSE-ALARM RATE at shipped defaults :  {fa}   "
618 |           f"({results.false_alarms}/{results.non_established} non-established runs fired anyway) <<<")
619 |     print(f"      This is a real weakness of the shipped detector, not a footnote.")
620 |     print(f"      With the default ABSOLUTE_EXCESS_RATIO, three in five outbreaks that")
621 |     print(f"      never established still trip the detector. Sweeping the DISCLOSURE")
622 |     print(f"      gates below cannot touch it -- it is a detection-side cost, and it is")
623 |     print(f"      the same number under every disclosure setting.")
624 |     print()
625 |     print(f"  established runs                  : {results.n_established} of {n_seeds}")
626 |     print(f"  ...of which the detector fired    : {results.n_fired}")
627 |     print(f"  mean day-30 cumulative infections : {_fmt(results.mean_total_infections)}  "
628 |           f"(disclosure-independent by construction -- this is why day-30")
629 |     print(f"                                      totals cannot be the outcome axis)")
630 |     print(f"  CONTROL mean fire delay          :  {_fmt(results.control_fire_delay)} days  "
631 |           f"(all established runs that fired)")
632 |     print()
633 |     print("  Column key for the sweep tables:")
634 |     print("    disc_frac  established runs that ever disclosed / all established runs")
635 |     print("               (the selection: shrinks as the gate tightens)")
636 |     print("    inf<disc   UNBIASED. Mean, over ALL established runs, of infections never")
637 |     print("               disclosed before the run ended: a run that disclosed contributes")
638 |     print("               its count at first disclosure, a run that never disclosed")
639 |     print("               contributes its full day-30 total.")
640 |     print("    inf@disc   CONDITIONAL on disclosure. Mean infections at first disclosure")
641 |     print("               over only the runs that disclosed -- under a tight gate those are")
642 |     print("               systematically the largest outbreaks, so this understates the cost.")
643 |     print("    disc_delay mean days to first disclosure   } over the same subset:")
644 |     print("    fire_delay mean days to first fire          } established runs that disclosed")
645 |     print("    gap        disc_delay - fire_delay, the privacy-policy window (== mean policy cost)")
646 |     print("    finest_lvl finest scope level ever disclosed at this setting")
647 |     print("    a row marked IMPOSSIBLE is a structurally degenerate configuration, not data.")
648 |     print()
649 |     print("  gap alone is ambiguous (it shrinks if disclosure speeds up OR detection slows)")
650 |     print("  -- but detection is fixed here, so fire_delay is flat and every move in gap is")
651 |     print("  disclosure timing. Read disc_delay and fire_delay together, never gap alone.")
652 |     print()
653 | 
654 |     sweeps_by_name = {s.param_name: s for s in DISCLOSURE_SWEEPS}
655 |     for param_name, summaries in results.sweeps.items():
656 |         _print_sweep(sweeps_by_name[param_name], summaries)
657 | 
658 | 
659 | if __name__ == "__main__":
660 |     main()

simulation/infection.py

 1 | """Infection state machine.
 2 | 
 3 |     SUSCEPTIBLE -> INCUBATING -> SYMPTOMATIC -> RECOVERED
 4 | 
 5 | States progress in that order, one step at a time, and never move
 6 | backwards. :func:`set_state` is the single choke point that enforces it.
 7 | """
 8 | 
 9 | from __future__ import annotations
10 | 
11 | import random
12 | from enum import Enum
13 | from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
14 | 
15 | from .config import SimulationConfig
16 | 
17 | if TYPE_CHECKING:  # avoid an import cycle; only needed for type checkers
18 |     from .agent import Agent
19 | 
20 | 
21 | class InfectionState(Enum):
22 |     SUSCEPTIBLE = "susceptible"
23 |     INCUBATING = "incubating"
24 |     SYMPTOMATIC = "symptomatic"
25 |     RECOVERED = "recovered"
26 | 
27 | 
28 | # The only permitted progression. Position in this tuple defines "forward".
29 | STATE_ORDER: tuple[InfectionState, ...] = (
30 |     InfectionState.SUSCEPTIBLE,
31 |     InfectionState.INCUBATING,
32 |     InfectionState.SYMPTOMATIC,
33 |     InfectionState.RECOVERED,
34 | )
35 | 
36 | # States in which an agent can transmit to a susceptible suite-mate.
37 | INFECTIOUS_STATES: frozenset[InfectionState] = frozenset(
38 |     {InfectionState.INCUBATING, InfectionState.SYMPTOMATIC}
39 | )
40 | 
41 | 
42 | def order_index(state: InfectionState) -> int:
43 |     """Position of ``state`` in :data:`STATE_ORDER`."""
44 |     return STATE_ORDER.index(state)
45 | 
46 | 
47 | def set_state(agent: "Agent", new_state: InfectionState, day: int) -> None:
48 |     """Advance ``agent`` to ``new_state`` and stamp the day of the change.
49 | 
50 |     Raises ``ValueError`` unless ``new_state`` is the state immediately
51 |     after the agent's current one in :data:`STATE_ORDER`: the machine only
52 |     ever steps forward, and only by one.
53 |     """
54 |     current = order_index(agent.state)
55 |     target = order_index(new_state)
56 |     if target != current + 1:
57 |         raise ValueError(
58 |             f"agent {agent.id}: illegal transition "
59 |             f"{agent.state.value} -> {new_state.value} "
60 |             f"(states move one step forward only, never back or skipping)"
61 |         )
62 |     agent.state = new_state
63 |     agent.state_changed_day = day
64 | 
65 | 
66 | def infect(
67 |     agent: "Agent", day: int, rng: random.Random, config: SimulationConfig
68 | ) -> None:
69 |     """SUSCEPTIBLE -> INCUBATING. Draws this agent's incubation and
70 |     symptomatic durations up front, from the configured ranges."""
71 |     set_state(agent, InfectionState.INCUBATING, day)
72 |     agent.incubation_days = rng.randint(
73 |         config.incubation_days_min, config.incubation_days_max
74 |     )
75 |     agent.symptomatic_days = rng.randint(
76 |         config.symptomatic_days_min, config.symptomatic_days_max
77 |     )
78 | 
79 | 
80 | def advance_time_based(agent: "Agent", day: int) -> bool:
81 |     """Apply the time-driven transitions:
82 | 
83 |     * INCUBATING -> SYMPTOMATIC once ``incubation_days`` have elapsed,
84 |     * SYMPTOMATIC -> RECOVERED once ``symptomatic_days`` have elapsed.
85 | 
86 |     At most one transition per call. Returns True if the agent changed
87 |     state. SUSCEPTIBLE and RECOVERED agents are untouched.
88 |     """
89 |     if agent.state is InfectionState.INCUBATING:
90 |         if day - agent.state_changed_day >= agent.incubation_days:
91 |             set_state(agent, InfectionState.SYMPTOMATIC, day)
92 |             return True
93 |     elif agent.state is InfectionState.SYMPTOMATIC:
94 |         if day - agent.state_changed_day >= agent.symptomatic_days:
95 |             set_state(agent, InfectionState.RECOVERED, day)
96 |             return True
97 |     return False

simulation/pipeline.py

  1 | """End-to-end wiring: simulated agents -> anonymous reports -> the
  2 | EXISTING, UNMODIFIED detection engine -> disclosure.
  3 | 
  4 | This module is the one place ground truth (``Simulation`` / ``Agent``
  5 | state) and the production engines (``microcluster.engine`` /
  6 | ``detection`` / ``disclosure``) meet. Only ``Report`` objects and the
  7 | ``ScopeRegistry`` cross that line -- see CLAUDE.md, "The ground-truth
  8 | firewall". ``DailyRecord.true_infected_count`` is the one deliberate
  9 | exception: it is ground truth kept for MEASURING the detector after the
 10 | fact, and is never part of what gets fed to it.
 11 | """
 12 | 
 13 | from __future__ import annotations
 14 | 
 15 | import random
 16 | from dataclasses import dataclass
 17 | from datetime import timedelta
 18 | 
 19 | from microcluster.config import DEFAULT_DETECTION_CONFIG, DEFAULT_DISCLOSURE_CONFIG
 20 | from microcluster.config import DetectionConfig, DisclosureConfig
 21 | from microcluster.detection import HysteresisState
 22 | from microcluster.disclosure import ScopeEvaluation
 23 | from microcluster.engine import analyze
 24 | from microcluster.models import Report
 25 | 
 26 | from . import reporting
 27 | from .config import DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG, REPORTING_MODEL_CLEAN, SimulationConfig
 28 | from .infection import InfectionState
 29 | from .population import StructureSpec
 30 | from .simulator import Simulation
 31 | 
 32 | __all__ = [
 33 |     "DailyRecord",
 34 |     "SimulationRun",
 35 |     "SimulatedReports",
 36 |     "simulate_and_report",
 37 |     "StreamAnalyzer",
 38 |     "analyze_report_stream",
 39 |     "run_with_detection",
 40 | ]
 41 | 
 42 | 
 43 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
 44 | class DailyRecord:
 45 |     """What happened on one simulated day, from both sides of the
 46 |     ground-truth firewall.
 47 | 
 48 |     ``first_fired_day`` / ``first_disclosed_day`` are running totals as of
 49 |     THIS day (``None`` until the first time each has happened), recorded
 50 |     here rather than left for a caller to re-derive later by scanning the
 51 |     record list. The gap between them -- time-to-first-DISCLOSURE minus
 52 |     time-to-first-FIRE -- is the measurable cost of the privacy policy:
 53 |     how long the system knew something before it was permitted to say
 54 |     where.
 55 |     """
 56 | 
 57 |     day: int
 58 |     true_infected_count: int  # ground truth, kept for measurement only
 59 |     reports_submitted_today: int
 60 |     reports_accumulated: int
 61 |     detection_fired: bool
 62 |     relative_fired: bool
 63 |     absolute_fired: bool
 64 |     disclosed_scope_id: str | None
 65 |     first_fired_day: int | None
 66 |     first_disclosed_day: int | None
 67 | 
 68 |     # The complete disclosure gate table for this day (rule 12): a
 69 |     # ScopeEvaluation for every candidate scope, winner and rejected
 70 |     # alike, exactly as disclosure.evaluate returned it. Empty on days the
 71 |     # detector did not fire (disclosure does not run). The presentation
 72 |     # layer renders these and never recomputes a gate.
 73 |     disclosure_evaluations: tuple[ScopeEvaluation, ...] = ()
 74 | 
 75 | 
 76 | @dataclass
 77 | class SimulationRun:
 78 |     simulation: Simulation
 79 |     reports: list[Report]
 80 |     daily_records: list[DailyRecord]
 81 | 
 82 |     def distinct_disclosed_scopes(self) -> list[str]:
 83 |         """Every distinct scope id ever disclosed over the run, in the
 84 |         order each was first named. Small and stable is the point of
 85 |         scope-stability disclosure (config.SIBLING_SWITCH_MARGIN); a long
 86 |         list here means disclosure wandered."""
 87 |         seen: list[str] = []
 88 |         for record in self.daily_records:
 89 |             scope_id = record.disclosed_scope_id
 90 |             if scope_id is not None and scope_id not in seen:
 91 |                 seen.append(scope_id)
 92 |         return seen
 93 | 
 94 | 
 95 | @dataclass
 96 | class SimulatedReports:
 97 |     """One simulated run's epidemic plus the anonymous reports it produced,
 98 |     day by day -- everything EXCEPT the detection/disclosure analysis.
 99 | 
100 |     Split out from :func:`run_with_detection` so an experiment can simulate
101 |     a seed once and then replay the analysis under many different engine
102 |     configs cheaply (see :func:`analyze_report_stream` and
103 |     ``simulation.experiments``). The simulated epidemic and the reports
104 |     depend on nothing in ``microcluster`` -- neither the detection nor the
105 |     disclosure config -- so it is always safe to reuse this across configs.
106 |     """
107 | 
108 |     simulation: Simulation
109 |     config: SimulationConfig
110 |     daily_reports: list[list[Report]]  # index d -> the reports generated on day d
111 | 
112 | 
113 | def simulate_and_report(
114 |     *,
115 |     seed: int,
116 |     days: int,
117 |     spec: StructureSpec | None = None,
118 |     config: SimulationConfig = DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG,
119 |     seed_infections: int | None = None,
120 | ) -> SimulatedReports:
121 |     """Run the simulator for ``days`` days and generate each day's
122 |     anonymous reports, WITHOUT analysing them.
123 | 
124 |     Only the CLEAN reporting model can be simulated ahead of analysis: the
125 |     realistic model's stigma suppression reacts to yesterday's disclosure,
126 |     so it needs the interleaved loop in ``simulation.realism`` (reached
127 |     through :func:`run_with_detection`).
128 | 
129 |     Report generation uses its own RNG stream (``f"reporting-{seed}"``),
130 |     independent of the simulation's own, and is interleaved with the daily
131 |     steps exactly as :func:`run_with_detection` does it -- so the two
132 |     produce byte-identical report streams for the same seed.
133 |     """
134 |     if config.reporting_model != REPORTING_MODEL_CLEAN:
135 |         raise ValueError(
136 |             "simulate_and_report only supports the clean reporting model; "
137 |             "use run_with_detection for the realistic model (it needs the "
138 |             "day-by-day disclosure feedback)"
139 |         )
140 |     sim = Simulation(seed=seed, spec=spec, config=config, seed_infections=seed_infections)
141 |     report_rng = random.Random(f"reporting-{seed}")
142 | 
143 |     daily_reports: list[list[Report]] = [
144 |         reporting.generate_daily_reports(sim.agents, 0, report_rng, config)
145 |     ]
146 |     for _ in range(days):
147 |         day_state = sim.step()
148 |         daily_reports.append(
149 |             reporting.generate_daily_reports(sim.agents, day_state.day, report_rng, config)
150 |         )
151 |     return SimulatedReports(simulation=sim, config=config, daily_reports=daily_reports)
152 | 
153 | 
154 | class StreamAnalyzer:
155 |     """One day at a time version of :func:`analyze_report_stream`.
156 | 
157 |     Holds the state the daily replay threads (accumulated reports,
158 |     hysteresis, disclosure scope-stability anchor, first-fired /
159 |     first-disclosed days) so a caller that must react to yesterday's
160 |     disclosure while generating today's reports (the stigma feedback in
161 |     ``simulation.realism``) can drive the very same analysis step. The
162 |     batch function below is now a loop over this class, so both paths
163 |     are byte-identical for the same report stream.
164 |     """
165 | 
166 |     def __init__(
167 |         self,
168 |         simulation: Simulation,
169 |         config: SimulationConfig,
170 |         *,
171 |         detection_config: DetectionConfig = DEFAULT_DETECTION_CONFIG,
172 |         disclosure_config: DisclosureConfig = DEFAULT_DISCLOSURE_CONFIG,
173 |         thread_scope_stability: bool = True,
174 |     ) -> None:
175 |         self.sim = simulation
176 |         self.config = config
177 |         self.detection_config = detection_config
178 |         self.disclosure_config = disclosure_config
179 |         self.thread_scope_stability = thread_scope_stability
180 |         self.all_reports: list[Report] = []
181 |         self.hysteresis_state: HysteresisState | None = None
182 |         self.prior_disclosed_scope_id: str | None = None
183 |         self.first_fired_day: int | None = None
184 |         self.first_disclosed_day: int | None = None
185 | 
186 |     def step(self, day: int, new_reports: list[Report]) -> DailyRecord:
187 |         self.all_reports.extend(new_reports)
188 |         now = self.config.simulation_start + timedelta(days=day)
189 |         analysis = analyze(
190 |             self.all_reports,
191 |             self.sim.registry,
192 |             now=now,
193 |             detection_config=self.detection_config,
194 |             disclosure_config=self.disclosure_config,
195 |             hysteresis_state=self.hysteresis_state,
196 |             prior_disclosed_scope_id=(
197 |                 self.prior_disclosed_scope_id if self.thread_scope_stability else None
198 |             ),
199 |         )
200 |         self.hysteresis_state = analysis.detection.hysteresis_state
201 |         # The stability "anchor" only moves when a scope is actually named
202 |         # today; a quiet day (nothing disclosed) does not reset it, so the
203 |         # NEXT disclosure still prefers the last-named scope's lineage.
204 |         if analysis.disclosed_scope_id is not None:
205 |             self.prior_disclosed_scope_id = analysis.disclosed_scope_id
206 | 
207 |         if self.first_fired_day is None and analysis.detection.fired:
208 |             self.first_fired_day = day
209 |         if self.first_disclosed_day is None and analysis.disclosed_scope_id is not None:
210 |             self.first_disclosed_day = day
211 | 
212 |         day_state = self.sim.history[day]
213 |         true_infected = day_state.count(InfectionState.INCUBATING) + day_state.count(
214 |             InfectionState.SYMPTOMATIC
215 |         )
216 |         return DailyRecord(
217 |             day=day,
218 |             true_infected_count=true_infected,
219 |             reports_submitted_today=len(new_reports),
220 |             reports_accumulated=len(self.all_reports),
221 |             detection_fired=analysis.detection.fired,
222 |             relative_fired=analysis.detection.relative_detector_fired,
223 |             absolute_fired=analysis.detection.absolute_detector_fired,
224 |             disclosed_scope_id=analysis.disclosed_scope_id,
225 |             first_fired_day=self.first_fired_day,
226 |             first_disclosed_day=self.first_disclosed_day,
227 |             disclosure_evaluations=analysis.disclosure.evaluations,
228 |         )
229 | 
230 | 
231 | def analyze_report_stream(
232 |     simulated: SimulatedReports,
233 |     *,
234 |     detection_config: DetectionConfig = DEFAULT_DETECTION_CONFIG,
235 |     disclosure_config: DisclosureConfig = DEFAULT_DISCLOSURE_CONFIG,
236 |     thread_scope_stability: bool = True,
237 | ) -> list[DailyRecord]:
238 |     """Replay ``microcluster.engine.analyze`` day by day over an
239 |     already-simulated report stream, feeding the ACCUMULATED reports so
240 |     far with ``now`` set to the simulated timestamp (never the wall
241 |     clock). Detection hysteresis and disclosure scope stability are
242 |     threaded day to day.
243 | 
244 |     Pure with respect to ``simulated`` -- safe to call repeatedly with
245 |     different ``detection_config`` / ``disclosure_config`` on the same
246 |     ``SimulatedReports``.
247 | 
248 |     ``thread_scope_stability=False`` stops threading
249 |     ``prior_disclosed_scope_id``, so each day's disclosure is the plain
250 |     finest-eligible pick with no continuity preference.
251 |     """
252 |     analyzer = StreamAnalyzer(
253 |         simulated.simulation,
254 |         simulated.config,
255 |         detection_config=detection_config,
256 |         disclosure_config=disclosure_config,
257 |         thread_scope_stability=thread_scope_stability,
258 |     )
259 |     return [
260 |         analyzer.step(day, new_reports)
261 |         for day, new_reports in enumerate(simulated.daily_reports)
262 |     ]
263 | 
264 | 
265 | def run_with_detection(
266 |     *,
267 |     seed: int,
268 |     days: int,
269 |     spec: StructureSpec | None = None,
270 |     config: SimulationConfig = DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG,
271 |     seed_infections: int | None = None,
272 |     detection_config: DetectionConfig = DEFAULT_DETECTION_CONFIG,
273 |     disclosure_config: DisclosureConfig = DEFAULT_DISCLOSURE_CONFIG,
274 | ) -> SimulationRun:
275 |     """Simulate ``days`` days for ``seed``, generate the anonymous report
276 |     stream, and analyse it day by day -- the full end-to-end pipeline.
277 | 
278 |     Equivalent to ``simulate_and_report`` followed by
279 |     ``analyze_report_stream``; kept as one call for the common case where
280 |     a caller wants a single run under a single set of configs.
281 |     """
282 |     if config.reporting_model != REPORTING_MODEL_CLEAN:
283 |         from .realism import run_realistic  # lazy: realism imports this module
284 | 
285 |         return run_realistic(
286 |             seed=seed,
287 |             days=days,
288 |             spec=spec,
289 |             config=config,
290 |             seed_infections=seed_infections,
291 |             detection_config=detection_config,
292 |             disclosure_config=disclosure_config,
293 |         )
294 |     simulated = simulate_and_report(
295 |         seed=seed, days=days, spec=spec, config=config, seed_infections=seed_infections
296 |     )
297 |     daily_records = analyze_report_stream(
298 |         simulated,
299 |         detection_config=detection_config,
300 |         disclosure_config=disclosure_config,
301 |     )
302 |     all_reports = [report for day_reports in simulated.daily_reports for report in day_reports]
303 |     return SimulationRun(
304 |         simulation=simulated.simulation,
305 |         reports=all_reports,
306 |         daily_records=daily_records,
307 |     )

simulation/population.py

  1 | """Population and scope-registry builder.
  2 | 
  3 | Given a structure spec, produce the agents together with a matching
  4 | ``microcluster.models.ScopeRegistry`` whose every declared population is
  5 | the *actual* number of agents beneath that scope (rule 7 in CLAUDE.md:
  6 | declared populations are set at group creation, not counted from reports --
  7 | here "group creation" is this function, and it counts agents, not
  8 | reports).
  9 | 
 10 | Binding constraint (CLAUDE.md SIMULATION section): agents live in suites.
 11 | Any Report generated later uses the agent's SUITE id as ``location_id``.
 12 | The coarser scopes (FLOOR, BUILDING, CAMPUS) exist here only so that
 13 | ``registry.chain_to_root`` can reach them.
 14 | """
 15 | 
 16 | from __future__ import annotations
 17 | 
 18 | import random
 19 | from collections import Counter
 20 | from dataclasses import dataclass
 21 | 
 22 | from microcluster.config import MIN_SCOPE_POPULATION
 23 | from microcluster.models import Scope, ScopeLevel, ScopeRegistry
 24 | 
 25 | from . import config as simconfig
 26 | from .agent import Agent
 27 | from .config import DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG, SimulationConfig
 28 | 
 29 | 
 30 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
 31 | class StructureSpec:
 32 |     """How many of each scope, and how many agents per suite. The defaults
 33 |     give the 150-agent smoke-test structure."""
 34 | 
 35 |     buildings: int = simconfig.DEFAULT_BUILDINGS
 36 |     floors_per_building: int = simconfig.DEFAULT_FLOORS_PER_BUILDING
 37 |     suites_per_floor: int = simconfig.DEFAULT_SUITES_PER_FLOOR
 38 |     agents_per_suite: int = simconfig.DEFAULT_AGENTS_PER_SUITE
 39 | 
 40 | 
 41 | def _validate_spec(spec: StructureSpec) -> None:
 42 |     for name, value in vars(spec).items():
 43 |         if value <= 0:
 44 |             raise ValueError(f"StructureSpec.{name} must be positive, got {value!r}")
 45 |     if spec.agents_per_suite <= MIN_SCOPE_POPULATION:
 46 |         raise ValueError(
 47 |             f"agents_per_suite ({spec.agents_per_suite}) must exceed "
 48 |             f"MIN_SCOPE_POPULATION ({MIN_SCOPE_POPULATION}); at or below it the "
 49 |             f"privacy gate can never pass at suite level, so a suite-level "
 50 |             f"disclosure would be structurally impossible."
 51 |         )
 52 | 
 53 | 
 54 | def build_population(
 55 |     spec: StructureSpec | None = None,
 56 |     rng: random.Random | None = None,
 57 |     config: SimulationConfig = DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG,
 58 | ) -> tuple[list[Agent], ScopeRegistry]:
 59 |     """Build the agent list and the matching ``ScopeRegistry``.
 60 | 
 61 |     ``rng`` seeds the per-agent reporting-probability draws; pass an
 62 |     explicit ``random.Random`` for reproducibility.
 63 |     """
 64 |     spec = spec or StructureSpec()
 65 |     _validate_spec(spec)
 66 |     rng = rng or random.Random()
 67 | 
 68 |     campus_id = config.campus_scope_id
 69 |     parent_of: dict[str, str] = {}
 70 |     building_ids: list[str] = []
 71 |     floor_ids: list[str] = []
 72 |     suite_ids: list[str] = []
 73 |     agents: list[Agent] = []
 74 | 
 75 |     for b in range(1, spec.buildings + 1):
 76 |         building_id = f"{campus_id}-B{b}"
 77 |         building_ids.append(building_id)
 78 |         parent_of[building_id] = campus_id
 79 |         for f in range(1, spec.floors_per_building + 1):
 80 |             floor_id = f"{building_id}-F{f}"
 81 |             floor_ids.append(floor_id)
 82 |             parent_of[floor_id] = building_id
 83 |             for s in range(1, spec.suites_per_floor + 1):
 84 |                 suite_id = f"{floor_id}-S{s}"
 85 |                 suite_ids.append(suite_id)
 86 |                 parent_of[suite_id] = floor_id
 87 |                 for a in range(1, spec.agents_per_suite + 1):
 88 |                     agents.append(
 89 |                         Agent(
 90 |                             id=f"{suite_id}-A{a}",
 91 |                             suite_id=suite_id,
 92 |                             reporting_probability=rng.uniform(
 93 |                                 config.reporting_probability_min,
 94 |                                 config.reporting_probability_max,
 95 |                             ),
 96 |                         )
 97 |                     )
 98 | 
 99 |     # Declared population of a scope == number of agents beneath it. Walk
100 |     # each agent's suite up to the campus root, counting as we go.
101 |     population: Counter[str] = Counter()
102 |     for agent in agents:
103 |         node = agent.suite_id
104 |         while True:
105 |             population[node] += 1
106 |             if node == campus_id:
107 |                 break
108 |             node = parent_of[node]
109 | 
110 |     scopes: dict[str, Scope] = {
111 |         campus_id: Scope(
112 |             id=campus_id,
113 |             label=config.campus_label,
114 |             level=ScopeLevel.CAMPUS,
115 |             population=population[campus_id],
116 |         )
117 |     }
118 |     for building_id in building_ids:
119 |         scopes[building_id] = Scope(
120 |             id=building_id,
121 |             label=building_id,
122 |             level=ScopeLevel.BUILDING,
123 |             population=population[building_id],
124 |             parent_id=parent_of[building_id],
125 |         )
126 |     for floor_id in floor_ids:
127 |         scopes[floor_id] = Scope(
128 |             id=floor_id,
129 |             label=floor_id,
130 |             level=ScopeLevel.FLOOR,
131 |             population=population[floor_id],
132 |             parent_id=parent_of[floor_id],
133 |         )
134 |     for suite_id in suite_ids:
135 |         scopes[suite_id] = Scope(
136 |             id=suite_id,
137 |             label=suite_id,
138 |             level=ScopeLevel.SUITE,
139 |             population=population[suite_id],
140 |             parent_id=parent_of[suite_id],
141 |         )
142 | 
143 |     registry = ScopeRegistry(scopes)  # runs ScopeRegistry's own validation
144 |     return agents, registry

simulation/realism.py

  1 | """Reporting-realism stress test.
  2 | 
  3 | The shipped reporting model is deliberately clean: each agent gets one fixed
  4 | probability (uniform 0.3 to 0.7), applied independently each symptomatic
  5 | day, with no memory and no feedback. Every committed result depends on it.
  6 | This module is an ALTERNATIVE, selected with
  7 | ``SimulationConfig(reporting_model="realistic")``, that breaks those three
  8 | assumptions on purpose:
  9 | 
 10 |   * HETEROGENEITY. Baselines come from Beta(0.5, 0.5) instead of
 11 |     uniform(0.3, 0.7). Same mean (0.5), very different spread: about a third
 12 |     of agents almost never report and about a third almost always do.
 13 |   * CORRELATION. A person's probability rises after suite-mates report (the
 14 |     "everyone else is filing, so will I" effect, and worried-well filers).
 15 |     p' = 1 - (1 - p) ** (1 + gain * r), r = reports from the agent's own
 16 |     suite in the previous few days. It applies to background reports too.
 17 |   * STIGMA. Once a scope has been NAMED in a disclosure, its members report
 18 |     less (p' = p * stigma_factor) for as long as it stays named plus a
 19 |     tail. This needs a feedback loop the clean model does not have: today's
 20 |     reports depend on yesterday's disclosure, so this runner interleaves
 21 |     generation with the same daily analysis step the batch pipeline uses
 22 |     (``pipeline.StreamAnalyzer``).
 23 | 
 24 | The epidemic itself is untouched and, for a given seed, IDENTICAL under both
 25 | models (the simulation RNG stream never sees reporting), so every
 26 | comparison below is paired: same outbreak, different reporting behaviour.
 27 | 
 28 | Every constant is CHOSEN, not measured -- like the clean model it stands in
 29 | for. This tests how much the detector depends on the clean assumption; it
 30 | does not claim to describe how real residents behave.
 31 | 
 32 |     python -m simulation.realism
 33 | """
 34 | 
 35 | from __future__ import annotations
 36 | 
 37 | import random
 38 | from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
 39 | from datetime import timedelta
 40 | from statistics import mean
 41 | from typing import Sequence
 42 | 
 43 | from microcluster.config import (
 44 |     DEFAULT_DETECTION_CONFIG,
 45 |     DEFAULT_DISCLOSURE_CONFIG,
 46 |     DetectionConfig,
 47 |     DisclosureConfig,
 48 | )
 49 | from microcluster.models import CHECKLIST, Onset, Report, ScopeRegistry
 50 | 
 51 | from .agent import Agent
 52 | from .config import (
 53 |     DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS,
 54 |     DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG,
 55 |     REPORTING_MODEL_REALISTIC,
 56 |     SimulationConfig,
 57 | )
 58 | from .experiments import RunOutcome, reduce_run, summarize_setting
 59 | from .infection import InfectionState
 60 | from .pipeline import SimulationRun, StreamAnalyzer, run_with_detection
 61 | from .population import StructureSpec
 62 | from .reporting import _non_outbreak_categories, _onset_for
 63 | from .simulator import Simulation
 64 | 
 65 | __all__ = [
 66 |     "RealismConfig",
 67 |     "VARIANTS",
 68 |     "run_realistic",
 69 |     "run_realism_sweep",
 70 | ]
 71 | 
 72 | 
 73 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
 74 | class RealismConfig:
 75 |     """Which realism effects are on, and how strong. All CHOSEN figures."""
 76 | 
 77 |     heterogeneity: bool = True
 78 |     correlation: bool = True
 79 |     stigma: bool = True
 80 | 
 81 |     # Beta(a, b) per-agent baseline. a = b = 0.5 keeps the clean model's
 82 |     # mean of 0.5 but makes it bimodal (U-shaped).
 83 |     baseline_beta_a: float = 0.5
 84 |     baseline_beta_b: float = 0.5
 85 | 
 86 |     # p' = 1 - (1 - p) ** (1 + gain * r); r counts reports from the agent's
 87 |     # own suite in the previous ``correlation_window_days`` days, capped.
 88 |     correlation_gain: float = 0.5
 89 |     correlation_window_days: int = 3
 90 |     correlation_max_reports: int = 4
 91 | 
 92 |     # Inside a scope that has been named: p' = p * stigma_factor, for as
 93 |     # long as it stays named and ``stigma_days`` after it was last named.
 94 |     stigma_factor: float = 0.5
 95 |     stigma_days: int = 7
 96 | 
 97 | 
 98 | DEFAULT_REALISM_CONFIG = RealismConfig()
 99 | 
100 | # The full model and each effect alone, for attribution.
101 | VARIANTS: dict[str, RealismConfig | None] = {
102 |     "clean (shipped)": None,
103 |     "realistic (all three)": DEFAULT_REALISM_CONFIG,
104 |     "heterogeneity only": RealismConfig(heterogeneity=True, correlation=False, stigma=False),
105 |     "correlation only": RealismConfig(heterogeneity=False, correlation=True, stigma=False),
106 |     "stigma only": RealismConfig(heterogeneity=False, correlation=False, stigma=True),
107 | }
108 | 
109 | 
110 | class _State:
111 |     """What the generator remembers between days."""
112 | 
113 |     def __init__(self, registry: ScopeRegistry, realism: RealismConfig) -> None:
114 |         self.realism = realism
115 |         self._lineage: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
116 |         self._registry = registry
117 |         self.suite_reports_by_day: dict[str, dict[int, int]] = {}
118 |         self.named_until: dict[str, int] = {}
119 | 
120 |     def lineage(self, suite_id: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
121 |         got = self._lineage.get(suite_id)
122 |         if got is None:
123 |             got = tuple(s.id for s in self._registry.chain_to_root(suite_id))
124 |             self._lineage[suite_id] = got
125 |         return got
126 | 
127 |     def recent_suite_reports(self, suite_id: str, day: int) -> int:
128 |         by_day = self.suite_reports_by_day.get(suite_id)
129 |         if not by_day:
130 |             return 0
131 |         window = self.realism.correlation_window_days
132 |         total = sum(by_day.get(d, 0) for d in range(day - window, day))
133 |         return min(total, self.realism.correlation_max_reports)
134 | 
135 |     def note_reports(self, day: int, reports: Sequence[Report]) -> None:
136 |         for r in reports:
137 |             d = self.suite_reports_by_day.setdefault(r.location_id, {})
138 |             d[day] = d.get(day, 0) + 1
139 | 
140 |     def note_disclosed(self, day: int, scope_id: str | None) -> None:
141 |         if scope_id is not None:
142 |             self.named_until[scope_id] = day + self.realism.stigma_days
143 | 
144 |     def suppressed(self, suite_id: str, day: int) -> bool:
145 |         return any(self.named_until.get(s, -1) >= day for s in self.lineage(suite_id))
146 | 
147 | 
148 | def _effective(p: float, suite_id: str, day: int, state: _State) -> float:
149 |     rc = state.realism
150 |     if rc.correlation:
151 |         r = state.recent_suite_reports(suite_id, day)
152 |         if r:
153 |             p = 1.0 - (1.0 - p) ** (1.0 + rc.correlation_gain * r)
154 |     if rc.stigma and state.suppressed(suite_id, day):
155 |         p *= rc.stigma_factor
156 |     return p
157 | 
158 | 
159 | def _generate_daily_reports(
160 |     agents: list[Agent],
161 |     day: int,
162 |     rng: random.Random,
163 |     config: SimulationConfig,
164 |     state: _State,
165 | ) -> list[Report]:
166 |     """Same structure as ``reporting.generate_daily_reports`` (same
167 |     categories, same one-outbreak-report-per-episode rule, same suite-only
168 |     location); only the probabilities differ."""
169 |     submitted_at = config.simulation_start + timedelta(days=day)
170 |     noise_categories = _non_outbreak_categories(config.outbreak_category)
171 |     reports: list[Report] = []
172 |     for agent in agents:
173 |         if agent.state is InfectionState.SYMPTOMATIC:
174 |             if agent.has_reported:
175 |                 continue
176 |             p = _effective(agent.reporting_probability, agent.suite_id, day, state)
177 |             if rng.random() < p:
178 |                 symptom = rng.choice(CHECKLIST[config.outbreak_category])
179 |                 reports.append(
180 |                     Report(
181 |                         category=config.outbreak_category,
182 |                         symptom=symptom,
183 |                         onset=_onset_for(day - agent.state_changed_day),
184 |                         location_id=agent.suite_id,
185 |                         submitted_at=submitted_at,
186 |                     )
187 |                 )
188 |                 agent.has_reported = True
189 |             continue
190 |         noise = _effective(config.background_noise_daily_rate, agent.suite_id, day, state)
191 |         if rng.random() < noise:
192 |             category = rng.choice(noise_categories)
193 |             reports.append(
194 |                 Report(
195 |                     category=category,
196 |                     symptom=rng.choice(CHECKLIST[category]),
197 |                     onset=rng.choice(list(Onset)),
198 |                     location_id=agent.suite_id,
199 |                     submitted_at=submitted_at,
200 |                 )
201 |             )
202 |     return reports
203 | 
204 | 
205 | def run_realistic(
206 |     *,
207 |     seed: int,
208 |     days: int,
209 |     spec: StructureSpec | None = None,
210 |     config: SimulationConfig = DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG,
211 |     seed_infections: int | None = None,
212 |     detection_config: DetectionConfig = DEFAULT_DETECTION_CONFIG,
213 |     disclosure_config: DisclosureConfig = DEFAULT_DISCLOSURE_CONFIG,
214 |     realism: RealismConfig = DEFAULT_REALISM_CONFIG,
215 | ) -> SimulationRun:
216 |     """Simulate + report + analyse one run under the realistic model."""
217 |     sim = Simulation(seed=seed, spec=spec, config=config, seed_infections=seed_infections)
218 |     if realism.heterogeneity:
219 |         # own stream: never perturbs the epidemic's RNG
220 |         baseline_rng = random.Random(f"realism-baseline-{seed}")
221 |         for agent in sim.agents:
222 |             agent.reporting_probability = baseline_rng.betavariate(
223 |                 realism.baseline_beta_a, realism.baseline_beta_b
224 |             )
225 |     rng = random.Random(f"reporting-{seed}")
226 |     state = _State(sim.registry, realism)
227 |     analyzer = StreamAnalyzer(
228 |         sim, config, detection_config=detection_config, disclosure_config=disclosure_config
229 |     )
230 | 
231 |     all_reports: list[Report] = []
232 |     records = []
233 |     for day in range(days + 1):
234 |         if day > 0:
235 |             sim.step()
236 |         todays = _generate_daily_reports(sim.agents, day, rng, config, state)
237 |         state.note_reports(day, todays)
238 |         all_reports.extend(todays)
239 |         record = analyzer.step(day, todays)
240 |         state.note_disclosed(day, record.disclosed_scope_id)
241 |         records.append(record)
242 |     return SimulationRun(simulation=sim, reports=all_reports, daily_records=records)
243 | 
244 | 
245 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
246 | # The paired sweep
247 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
248 | 
249 | 
250 | def _continuity(records) -> float | None:
251 |     """Of the days from first disclosure to the end of the run, the share on
252 |     which something was still being disclosed."""
253 |     first = records[-1].first_disclosed_day
254 |     if first is None:
255 |         return None
256 |     tail = records[first:]
257 |     return sum(1 for r in tail if r.disclosed_scope_id is not None) / len(tail)
258 | 
259 | 
260 | def run_realism_sweep(
261 |     seeds: Sequence[int] | None = None,
262 |     *,
263 |     days: int = DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS,
264 |     spec: StructureSpec | None = None,
265 | ) -> dict[str, dict]:
266 |     seed_list = list(seeds) if seeds is not None else list(range(1, 501))
267 |     out: dict[str, dict] = {}
268 |     for name, realism in VARIANTS.items():
269 |         outcomes: list[RunOutcome] = []
270 |         continuity: list[float] = []
271 |         reports_total: list[int] = []
272 |         for seed in seed_list:
273 |             if realism is None:
274 |                 run = run_with_detection(seed=seed, days=days, spec=spec)
275 |             else:
276 |                 run = run_realistic(
277 |                     seed=seed,
278 |                     days=days,
279 |                     spec=spec,
280 |                     config=replace(DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG, reporting_model=REPORTING_MODEL_REALISTIC),
281 |                     realism=realism,
282 |                 )
283 |             outcome = reduce_run(seed, run.simulation, run.daily_records)
284 |             outcomes.append(outcome)
285 |             reports_total.append(len(run.reports))
286 |             if outcome.established:
287 |                 c = _continuity(run.daily_records)
288 |                 if c is not None:
289 |                     continuity.append(c)
290 |         s = summarize_setting(outcomes, param_name="reporting_model", value=0)
291 |         non_est = [o for o in outcomes if not o.established]
292 |         false_disclosures = [o for o in non_est if o.first_disclosed_day is not None]
293 |         out[name] = {
294 |             "n_runs": s.n_runs,
295 |             "n_established": s.n_established,
296 |             "n_disclosed": s.n_disclosed,
297 |             "disclosed_fraction": s.disclosed_fraction,
298 |             "fire_delay": s.mean_fire_delay,
299 |             "disclosure_delay": s.mean_disclosure_delay,
300 |             "gap": s.mean_gap,
301 |             "infections_before_disclosure": s.mean_infections_before_disclosure_unbiased,
302 |             "false_alarm_rate": s.false_alarm_rate,
303 |             "false_disclosure_rate": (len(false_disclosures) / len(non_est)) if non_est else None,
304 |             "disclosure_continuity": mean(continuity) if continuity else None,
305 |             "mean_reports_per_run": mean(reports_total),
306 |             "finest_level": s.finest_disclosed_level.name if s.finest_disclosed_level else None,
307 |         }
308 |     return out
309 | 
310 | 
311 | GRID_BETA = (None, 2.0, 0.5, 0.25)        # None = the clean uniform(0.3, 0.7) baseline
312 | GRID_GAIN = (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0)         # correlation strength; 0 = none
313 | 
314 | 
315 | def run_sensitivity_grid(
316 |     seeds: Sequence[int] | None = None, *, days: int = DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS
317 | ) -> list[dict]:
318 |     """Heterogeneity x correlation grid (stigma on throughout). The full
319 |     model above is one cell. Its point: the timing metrics can look robust
320 |     only because opposing effects cancel, so show how far they move when the
321 |     strengths are changed."""
322 |     seed_list = list(seeds) if seeds is not None else list(range(1, 501))
323 |     cfg = replace(DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG, reporting_model=REPORTING_MODEL_REALISTIC)
324 |     rows: list[dict] = []
325 |     for beta in GRID_BETA:
326 |         for gain in GRID_GAIN:
327 |             realism = RealismConfig(
328 |                 heterogeneity=beta is not None,
329 |                 correlation=gain > 0,
330 |                 stigma=True,
331 |                 baseline_beta_a=beta or 0.5,
332 |                 baseline_beta_b=beta or 0.5,
333 |                 correlation_gain=gain,
334 |             )
335 |             outcomes = []
336 |             for seed in seed_list:
337 |                 run = run_realistic(seed=seed, days=days, config=cfg, realism=realism)
338 |                 outcomes.append(reduce_run(seed, run.simulation, run.daily_records))
339 |             s = summarize_setting(outcomes, param_name="grid", value=0)
340 |             non_est = [o for o in outcomes if not o.established]
341 |             rows.append(
342 |                 {
343 |                     "beta": beta,
344 |                     "correlation_gain": gain,
345 |                     "disclosure_delay": s.mean_disclosure_delay,
346 |                     "infections_before_disclosure": s.mean_infections_before_disclosure_unbiased,
347 |                     "false_alarm_rate": s.false_alarm_rate,
348 |                     "false_disclosure_rate": (
349 |                         sum(1 for o in non_est if o.first_disclosed_day is not None) / len(non_est)
350 |                         if non_est else None
351 |                     ),
352 |                 }
353 |             )
354 |     return rows
355 | 
356 | 
357 | def _f(v, d=2, pct=False):
358 |     if v is None:
359 |         return "n/a"
360 |     return f"{v * 100:.0f}%" if pct else f"{v:.{d}f}"
361 | 
362 | 
363 | def format_sweep(results: dict[str, dict]) -> str:
364 |     head = (
365 |         f"{'model':<24}{'fire d':>8}{'disc d':>8}{'gap':>7}{'inf<disc':>10}"
366 |         f"{'discl%':>8}{'false-alarm':>13}{'false-disc':>12}{'continuity':>12}{'reports':>9}"
367 |     )
368 |     lines = ["REPORTING-REALISM STRESS TEST (paired: same outbreaks, different reporting)", "", head]
369 |     for name, r in results.items():
370 |         lines.append(
371 |             f"{name:<24}{_f(r['fire_delay'],1):>8}{_f(r['disclosure_delay'],1):>8}{_f(r['gap'],1):>7}"
372 |             f"{_f(r['infections_before_disclosure'],1):>10}{_f(r['disclosed_fraction'],pct=True):>8}"
373 |             f"{_f(r['false_alarm_rate'],pct=True):>13}{_f(r['false_disclosure_rate'],pct=True):>12}"
374 |             f"{_f(r['disclosure_continuity'],pct=True):>12}{_f(r['mean_reports_per_run'],0):>9}"
375 |         )
376 |     return "\n".join(lines)
377 | 
378 | 
379 | def main() -> None:
380 |     print(format_sweep(run_realism_sweep()))
381 | 
382 | 
383 | if __name__ == "__main__":
384 |     main()

simulation/reporting.py

  1 | """Turns simulated infection state into anonymous Reports.
  2 | 
  3 | This is the ONLY bridge between the simulator's ground truth and the
  4 | production ``microcluster`` engines. Only two facts survive the crossing:
  5 | which SUITE an agent is in, and whether they feel like filing a report
  6 | today. Everything else the simulator knows -- who is actually infected,
  7 | since when, who infected whom -- stays on this side of the line (see
  8 | CLAUDE.md, "The ground-truth firewall").
  9 | 
 10 | Two kinds of reports are generated each day:
 11 | 
 12 |   * OUTBREAK reports -- from agents SYMPTOMATIC with the real illness, at
 13 |     most once per episode, in the single configured outbreak category.
 14 |   * BACKGROUND NOISE -- from agents who are NOT currently symptomatic,
 15 |     independently each day, in a category other than the outbreak's. This
 16 |     is what makes the detection problem real: without it, every report in
 17 |     the stream would be outbreak signal.
 18 | """
 19 | 
 20 | from __future__ import annotations
 21 | 
 22 | import random
 23 | from datetime import timedelta
 24 | 
 25 | from microcluster.models import CHECKLIST, Category, Onset, Report
 26 | 
 27 | from .agent import Agent
 28 | from .config import SimulationConfig
 29 | from .infection import InfectionState
 30 | 
 31 | # Every fixed-checklist category except whichever one the outbreak is
 32 | # using, for drawing a background-noise report's category.
 33 | _ALL_CATEGORIES: tuple[Category, ...] = tuple(Category)
 34 | 
 35 | 
 36 | def _non_outbreak_categories(outbreak_category: Category) -> tuple[Category, ...]:
 37 |     return tuple(c for c in _ALL_CATEGORIES if c != outbreak_category)
 38 | 
 39 | 
 40 | def _onset_for(days_symptomatic: int) -> Onset:
 41 |     """Coarse onset bucket (rule 1), computed from how many days an agent
 42 |     has been symptomatic -- what a real reporter would say if asked."""
 43 |     if days_symptomatic <= 0:
 44 |         return Onset.TODAY
 45 |     if days_symptomatic <= 2:
 46 |         return Onset.ONE_TO_TWO_DAYS
 47 |     if days_symptomatic <= 7:
 48 |         return Onset.THREE_TO_SEVEN_DAYS
 49 |     return Onset.ONE_WEEK_PLUS
 50 | 
 51 | 
 52 | def generate_daily_reports(
 53 |     agents: list[Agent],
 54 |     day: int,
 55 |     rng: random.Random,
 56 |     config: SimulationConfig,
 57 | ) -> list[Report]:
 58 |     """Every report filed on simulated ``day``.
 59 | 
 60 |     Mutates ``agent.has_reported`` in place when an outbreak report is
 61 |     filed, so the same episode is never reported twice. ``rng`` should be
 62 |     a stream dedicated to reporting, kept separate from the simulation's
 63 |     own transmission/progression stream so that adding report generation
 64 |     never perturbs the epidemic trajectory itself.
 65 |     """
 66 |     submitted_at = config.simulation_start + timedelta(days=day)
 67 |     noise_categories = _non_outbreak_categories(config.outbreak_category)
 68 |     reports: list[Report] = []
 69 | 
 70 |     for agent in agents:
 71 |         if agent.state is InfectionState.SYMPTOMATIC:
 72 |             if agent.has_reported:
 73 |                 continue
 74 |             if rng.random() < agent.reporting_probability:
 75 |                 days_symptomatic = day - agent.state_changed_day
 76 |                 symptom = rng.choice(CHECKLIST[config.outbreak_category])
 77 |                 reports.append(
 78 |                     Report(
 79 |                         category=config.outbreak_category,
 80 |                         symptom=symptom,
 81 |                         onset=_onset_for(days_symptomatic),
 82 |                         location_id=agent.suite_id,
 83 |                         submitted_at=submitted_at,
 84 |                     )
 85 |                 )
 86 |                 agent.has_reported = True
 87 |             continue
 88 | 
 89 |         # Not currently symptomatic with the real illness: a chance of an
 90 |         # ordinary, unrelated report, independent of any episode.
 91 |         if rng.random() < config.background_noise_daily_rate:
 92 |             category = rng.choice(noise_categories)
 93 |             reports.append(
 94 |                 Report(
 95 |                     category=category,
 96 |                     symptom=rng.choice(CHECKLIST[category]),
 97 |                     onset=rng.choice(list(Onset)),
 98 |                     location_id=agent.suite_id,
 99 |                     submitted_at=submitted_at,
100 |                 )
101 |             )
102 | 
103 |     return reports

simulation/scale.py

  1 | """Scale validation: does the pipeline still behave at 500 / 1,000 / 2,000
  2 | people with four scope levels (campus > building > floor > suite)?
  3 | 
  4 | The population builder was already parametric; what was never exercised was
  5 | a structure with more than one building, so the BUILDING scope always
  6 | equalled the CAMPUS scope and the hierarchy had three DISTINCT levels. The
  7 | specs below give four distinct levels at every size. No engine, gate or
  8 | simulator logic is touched -- this module only builds bigger structures,
  9 | runs the existing pipeline over a seeded sweep, and CHECKS the results
 10 | against the gate formulas independently of the engine that produced them.
 11 | 
 12 |     python -m simulation.scale            # prints the table
 13 |     python -m scripts.stress_tests        # writes results/scale.json too
 14 | 
 15 | Every number here is a property of this simulator with CHOSEN parameters
 16 | (see config.py); it says whether the machinery scales, not what a real
 17 | building would do.
 18 | """
 19 | 
 20 | from __future__ import annotations
 21 | 
 22 | import math
 23 | import time
 24 | from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 25 | from statistics import mean, median
 26 | 
 27 | from microcluster.config import DEFAULT_DISCLOSURE_CONFIG
 28 | from microcluster.models import ScopeLevel
 29 | 
 30 | from .config import DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS
 31 | from .experiments import reduce_run, summarize_setting
 32 | from .infection import InfectionState
 33 | from .pipeline import run_with_detection
 34 | from .population import StructureSpec
 35 | 
 36 | __all__ = ["SCALE_SPECS", "SizeReport", "measure_size", "run_scale_validation"]
 37 | 
 38 | # (buildings, floors per building, suites per floor, agents per suite).
 39 | # Suite size stays 25 so the suite-level gates are identical at every size
 40 | # and any change is attributable to the HIERARCHY growing, not the suite.
 41 | SCALE_SPECS: dict[int, StructureSpec] = {
 42 |     150: StructureSpec(1, 2, 3, 25),    # the shipped baseline (3 distinct levels)
 43 |     500: StructureSpec(2, 2, 5, 25),
 44 |     1000: StructureSpec(2, 4, 5, 25),
 45 |     2000: StructureSpec(4, 4, 5, 25),
 46 | }
 47 | 
 48 | 
 49 | @dataclass
 50 | class SizeReport:
 51 |     n_agents: int
 52 |     n_suites: int
 53 |     n_distinct_populations: int      # distinct declared populations across the hierarchy
 54 |     n_runs: int
 55 |     seconds_per_run: float
 56 | 
 57 |     # plausibility of the simulated epidemic
 58 |     established_fraction: float
 59 |     median_attack_rate: float | None          # of ALL agents, established runs
 60 |     median_attack_rate_in_touched: float | None  # of the buildings that got any case
 61 |     median_buildings_touched: float | None     # buildings with >= 1 ever-infected
 62 |     peaks_and_declines_fraction: float | None  # established runs whose curve turns down
 63 | 
 64 |     # evidence gate vs its formula
 65 |     scope_evaluations_checked: int
 66 |     threshold_formula_violations: int          # engine threshold != max(5, ceil(sqrt n))
 67 |     pass_flag_violations: int                  # stat_pass != (qualifying >= threshold)
 68 |     selected_without_both_gates: int
 69 |     thresholds_by_level: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
 70 | 
 71 |     # privacy gate
 72 |     roster_refusals: int = 0                   # stat PASS but privacy FAIL (scope-days)
 73 |     roster_refusal_levels: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
 74 |     runs_with_a_roster_refusal: int = 0
 75 | 
 76 |     # timing (established runs that disclosed)
 77 |     n_established: int = 0
 78 |     n_disclosed: int = 0
 79 |     mean_fire_delay: float | None = None
 80 |     mean_disclosure_delay: float | None = None
 81 |     mean_gap: float | None = None
 82 |     infections_before_disclosure: float | None = None       # unbiased
 83 |     infections_before_disclosure_pct: float | None = None   # of all agents
 84 |     finest_level_histogram: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
 85 |     false_alarm_rate: float | None = None
 86 |     fizzle_rate: float = 0.0
 87 |     relative_fired_days: int = 0
 88 |     absolute_fired_days: int = 0
 89 |     errors: int = 0
 90 | 
 91 | 
 92 | def _ceil_sqrt(n: int) -> int:
 93 |     root = math.isqrt(n)
 94 |     return root if root * root == n else root + 1
 95 | 
 96 | 
 97 | def measure_size(
 98 |     n_agents: int, seeds: list[int], *, days: int = DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS
 99 | ) -> SizeReport:
100 |     spec = SCALE_SPECS[n_agents]
101 |     floor = DEFAULT_DISCLOSURE_CONFIG.statistical_gate_floor
102 |     outcomes = []
103 |     attack_rates: list[float] = []
104 |     attack_touched: list[float] = []
105 |     buildings_touched: list[int] = []
106 |     peaked = established = 0
107 |     checked = thr_bad = flag_bad = sel_bad = 0
108 |     thresholds: dict[str, int] = {}
109 |     roster = roster_runs = 0
110 |     roster_levels: dict[str, int] = {}
111 |     rel_days = abs_days = 0
112 |     errors = 0
113 |     n_suites = 0
114 |     populations_seen: set[int] = set()
115 |     t0 = time.perf_counter()
116 | 
117 |     for seed in seeds:
118 |         try:
119 |             run = run_with_detection(seed=seed, days=days, spec=spec)
120 |         except Exception:  # a component failing outright is a RESULT, not a crash
121 |             errors += 1
122 |             continue
123 |         sim, records = run.simulation, run.daily_records
124 |         n_suites = sum(1 for s in sim.registry.scopes.values() if s.level is ScopeLevel.SUITE)
125 |         populations_seen = {s.population for s in sim.registry.scopes.values()}
126 |         outcome = reduce_run(seed, sim, records)
127 |         outcomes.append(outcome)
128 | 
129 |         if outcome.established:
130 |             established += 1
131 |             attack_rates.append(outcome.total_infections / len(sim.agents))
132 |             final = sim.history[-1]
133 |             touched = {
134 |                 a.suite_id.rsplit("-F", 1)[0]
135 |                 for a in final.agents
136 |                 if a.state is not InfectionState.SUSCEPTIBLE
137 |             }
138 |             buildings_touched.append(len(touched))
139 |             bpop = sim.registry.get(sorted(touched)[0]).population
140 |             attack_touched.append(outcome.total_infections / (len(touched) * bpop))
141 |             active = [
142 |                 s.count(InfectionState.INCUBATING) + s.count(InfectionState.SYMPTOMATIC)
143 |                 for s in sim.history
144 |             ]
145 |             if max(active) > 0 and active.index(max(active)) < len(active) - 1 and active[-1] < max(active):
146 |                 peaked += 1
147 | 
148 |         run_refused = False
149 |         for rec in records:
150 |             rel_days += rec.relative_fired
151 |             abs_days += rec.absolute_fired
152 |             for ev in rec.disclosure_evaluations:
153 |                 checked += 1
154 |                 expected = max(floor, _ceil_sqrt(ev.population))
155 |                 thresholds[ev.scope_level.name] = expected
156 |                 if ev.statistical_threshold != expected:
157 |                     thr_bad += 1
158 |                 if ev.statistical_pass != (ev.qualifying_reports >= ev.statistical_threshold):
159 |                     flag_bad += 1
160 |                 if ev.selected and not (ev.statistical_pass and ev.privacy_pass):
161 |                     sel_bad += 1
162 |                 if ev.statistical_pass and not ev.privacy_pass:
163 |                     roster += 1
164 |                     run_refused = True
165 |                     roster_levels[ev.scope_level.name] = roster_levels.get(ev.scope_level.name, 0) + 1
166 |         roster_runs += run_refused
167 | 
168 |     elapsed = time.perf_counter() - t0
169 |     summary = summarize_setting(outcomes, param_name="size", value=n_agents)
170 |     finest: dict[str, int] = {}
171 |     for o in outcomes:
172 |         if o.established and o.finest_disclosed_level is not None:
173 |             name = o.finest_disclosed_level.name
174 |             finest[name] = finest.get(name, 0) + 1
175 | 
176 |     def _pct(x: float | None) -> float | None:
177 |         return None if x is None else x / n_agents * 100
178 | 
179 |     return SizeReport(
180 |         n_agents=n_agents,
181 |         n_suites=n_suites,
182 |         n_distinct_populations=len(populations_seen),
183 |         n_runs=len(outcomes),
184 |         seconds_per_run=elapsed / max(1, len(seeds)),
185 |         established_fraction=(established / len(outcomes)) if outcomes else 0.0,
186 |         median_attack_rate=median(attack_rates) if attack_rates else None,
187 |         median_attack_rate_in_touched=median(attack_touched) if attack_touched else None,
188 |         median_buildings_touched=median(buildings_touched) if buildings_touched else None,
189 |         peaks_and_declines_fraction=(peaked / established) if established else None,
190 |         scope_evaluations_checked=checked,
191 |         threshold_formula_violations=thr_bad,
192 |         pass_flag_violations=flag_bad,
193 |         selected_without_both_gates=sel_bad,
194 |         thresholds_by_level=thresholds,
195 |         roster_refusals=roster,
196 |         roster_refusal_levels=roster_levels,
197 |         runs_with_a_roster_refusal=roster_runs,
198 |         n_established=summary.n_established,
199 |         n_disclosed=summary.n_disclosed,
200 |         mean_fire_delay=summary.mean_fire_delay,
201 |         mean_disclosure_delay=summary.mean_disclosure_delay,
202 |         mean_gap=summary.mean_gap,
203 |         infections_before_disclosure=summary.mean_infections_before_disclosure_unbiased,
204 |         infections_before_disclosure_pct=_pct(summary.mean_infections_before_disclosure_unbiased),
205 |         finest_level_histogram=finest,
206 |         false_alarm_rate=summary.false_alarm_rate,
207 |         fizzle_rate=1 - (established / len(outcomes)) if outcomes else 0.0,
208 |         relative_fired_days=rel_days,
209 |         absolute_fired_days=abs_days,
210 |         errors=errors,
211 |     )
212 | 
213 | 
214 | def run_scale_validation(
215 |     seeds: list[int] | None = None, sizes: tuple[int, ...] = (150, 500, 1000, 2000)
216 | ) -> list[SizeReport]:
217 |     seed_list = seeds if seeds is not None else list(range(1, 201))
218 |     return [measure_size(n, seed_list) for n in sizes]
219 | 
220 | 
221 | def _f(v: float | None, d: int = 1) -> str:
222 |     return "n/a" if v is None else f"{v:.{d}f}"
223 | 
224 | 
225 | def format_reports(reports: list[SizeReport]) -> str:
226 |     lines = ["SCALE VALIDATION (same pipeline, same gates, bigger structures)", ""]
227 |     for r in reports:
228 |         lines += [
229 |             f"--- {r.n_agents} agents, {r.n_suites} suites, {r.n_distinct_populations} distinct scope populations "
230 |             f"({r.n_runs} runs, {r.seconds_per_run:.2f}s/run, {r.errors} errors)",
231 |             f"  epidemic : established {r.established_fraction:.0%}, median attack rate "
232 |             f"{_f(None if r.median_attack_rate is None else r.median_attack_rate * 100)}% of all agents, "
233 |             f"{_f(None if r.median_attack_rate_in_touched is None else r.median_attack_rate_in_touched * 100)}% of the building(s) touched, "
234 |             f"median buildings touched {_f(r.median_buildings_touched)}, "
235 |             f"peak-then-decline {_f(None if r.peaks_and_declines_fraction is None else r.peaks_and_declines_fraction * 100, 0)}%",
236 |             f"  evidence : {r.scope_evaluations_checked} scope evaluations checked; formula violations "
237 |             f"{r.threshold_formula_violations}, pass-flag violations {r.pass_flag_violations}, "
238 |             f"selected-without-both-gates {r.selected_without_both_gates}; thresholds {r.thresholds_by_level}",
239 |             f"  privacy  : roster refusals {r.roster_refusals} in {r.runs_with_a_roster_refusal} runs, by level {r.roster_refusal_levels}",
240 |             f"  timing   : fire day {_f(r.mean_fire_delay)}, disclosure day {_f(r.mean_disclosure_delay)}, gap {_f(r.mean_gap)}, "
241 |             f"infected before disclosure {_f(r.infections_before_disclosure)} "
242 |             f"({_f(r.infections_before_disclosure_pct)}% of agents); disclosed {r.n_disclosed}/{r.n_established}; "
243 |             f"false-alarm rate {_f(None if r.false_alarm_rate is None else r.false_alarm_rate * 100, 0)}%",
244 |             f"  finest level named (established runs): {r.finest_level_histogram}",
245 |             "",
246 |         ]
247 |     return "\n".join(lines)
248 | 
249 | 
250 | def main() -> None:
251 |     print(format_reports(run_scale_validation()))
252 | 
253 | 
254 | if __name__ == "__main__":
255 |     main()

simulation/simulator.py

  1 | """The run loop.
  2 | 
  3 | Step one simulated day at a time. After every step the full world state
  4 | is captured as a :class:`DayState` (per-state counts plus an immutable
  5 | snapshot of every agent) and appended to ``history`` so a run can be
  6 | recorded, replayed, or rendered later.
  7 | 
  8 | Contact is tiered (suite / floor / building), not suite-only -- see
  9 | ``simulation.contact``. Each suite's floor and building ids are resolved
 10 | once, through the ``ScopeRegistry``, and reused every step.
 11 | """
 12 | 
 13 | from __future__ import annotations
 14 | 
 15 | import random
 16 | from dataclasses import dataclass
 17 | 
 18 | from microcluster.models import ScopeRegistry
 19 | 
 20 | from . import contact, infection
 21 | from .contact import suite_ancestor_ids
 22 | from .agent import Agent, AgentSnapshot
 23 | from .config import DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG, SimulationConfig
 24 | from .infection import STATE_ORDER, InfectionState
 25 | from .population import StructureSpec, build_population
 26 | 
 27 | 
 28 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
 29 | class DayState:
 30 |     """The whole simulated world at the end of one day."""
 31 | 
 32 |     day: int
 33 |     counts: dict[InfectionState, int]
 34 |     agents: tuple[AgentSnapshot, ...]
 35 | 
 36 |     @property
 37 |     def total(self) -> int:
 38 |         return sum(self.counts.values())
 39 | 
 40 |     def count(self, state: InfectionState) -> int:
 41 |         return self.counts[state]
 42 | 
 43 | 
 44 | class Simulation:
 45 |     """One outbreak run. Fully determined by ``seed`` (given the same
 46 |     ``spec`` and ``config``)."""
 47 | 
 48 |     def __init__(
 49 |         self,
 50 |         *,
 51 |         seed: int,
 52 |         spec: StructureSpec | None = None,
 53 |         config: SimulationConfig = DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG,
 54 |         seed_infections: int | None = None,
 55 |     ) -> None:
 56 |         self.config = config
 57 |         self.spec = spec or StructureSpec()
 58 |         self.rng = random.Random(seed)
 59 |         self.agents: list[Agent]
 60 |         self.registry: ScopeRegistry
 61 |         self.agents, self.registry = build_population(self.spec, self.rng, config)
 62 | 
 63 |         self._suites: dict[str, list[Agent]] = {}
 64 |         for agent in self.agents:
 65 |             self._suites.setdefault(agent.suite_id, []).append(agent)
 66 | 
 67 |         # Where each suite sits in the hierarchy, read once from the
 68 |         # registry (never hard-coded from id strings) and reused every step.
 69 |         self._floor_of_suite: dict[str, str] = {}
 70 |         self._building_of_suite: dict[str, str] = {}
 71 |         for suite_id in self._suites:
 72 |             floor_id, building_id = suite_ancestor_ids(self.registry, suite_id)
 73 |             self._floor_of_suite[suite_id] = floor_id
 74 |             self._building_of_suite[suite_id] = building_id
 75 | 
 76 |         self.day = 0
 77 |         requested = (
 78 |             config.seed_infections if seed_infections is None else seed_infections
 79 |         )
 80 |         self._seed_infections(requested)
 81 | 
 82 |         # Running totals, split by contact tier that caused the infection
 83 |         # (see contact.new_infections). Useful for measuring how much of an
 84 |         # outbreak stayed within its starting suite versus spread further.
 85 |         self.total_within_suite_infections = 0
 86 |         self.total_cross_suite_infections = 0
 87 | 
 88 |         self.history: list[DayState] = [self._snapshot()]
 89 | 
 90 |     # -- setup ---------------------------------------------------------
 91 | 
 92 |     def _seed_infections(self, n: int) -> None:
 93 |         if n < 0:
 94 |             raise ValueError("seed_infections must be >= 0")
 95 |         if n > len(self.agents):
 96 |             raise ValueError(
 97 |                 f"cannot seed {n} infections in a population of {len(self.agents)}"
 98 |             )
 99 |         for agent in self.rng.sample(self.agents, n):
100 |             infection.infect(agent, self.day, self.rng, self.config)
101 | 
102 |     # -- stepping ----------------------------------------------------
103 | 
104 |     def step(self) -> DayState:
105 |         """Advance one day.
106 | 
107 |         Order within the step:
108 | 
109 |         1. Decide new infections from the START-OF-DAY state, so an agent
110 |            infected today cannot also transmit or progress today.
111 |         2. Apply time-based progression (INCUBATING -> SYMPTOMATIC ->
112 |            RECOVERED) to everyone.
113 |         3. Apply the new infections (SUSCEPTIBLE -> INCUBATING).
114 | 
115 |         Returns the resulting :class:`DayState`, also appended to
116 |         ``history``.
117 |         """
118 |         self.day += 1
119 |         day = self.day
120 | 
121 |         within_suite, cross_suite = contact.new_infections(
122 |             self._suites, self._floor_of_suite, self._building_of_suite,
123 |             self.rng, self.config,
124 |         )
125 |         self.total_within_suite_infections += len(within_suite)
126 |         self.total_cross_suite_infections += len(cross_suite)
127 |         newly_infected = within_suite + cross_suite
128 | 
129 |         for agent in self.agents:
130 |             infection.advance_time_based(agent, day)
131 | 
132 |         for agent in newly_infected:
133 |             infection.infect(agent, day, self.rng, self.config)
134 | 
135 |         snapshot = self._snapshot()
136 |         self.history.append(snapshot)
137 |         return snapshot
138 | 
139 |     def run(self, days: int) -> list[DayState]:
140 |         """Step ``days`` times; return the full history (day 0 .. day
141 |         ``days``)."""
142 |         if days < 0:
143 |             raise ValueError("days must be >= 0")
144 |         for _ in range(days):
145 |             self.step()
146 |         return self.history
147 | 
148 |     # -- snapshot ----------------------------------------------------
149 | 
150 |     def _snapshot(self) -> DayState:
151 |         counts: dict[InfectionState, int] = {state: 0 for state in STATE_ORDER}
152 |         agent_snapshots: list[AgentSnapshot] = []
153 |         for agent in self.agents:
154 |             counts[agent.state] += 1
155 |             agent_snapshots.append(
156 |                 AgentSnapshot(
157 |                     id=agent.id,
158 |                     suite_id=agent.suite_id,
159 |                     state=agent.state,
160 |                     state_changed_day=agent.state_changed_day,
161 |                 )
162 |             )
163 |         return DayState(day=self.day, counts=counts, agents=tuple(agent_snapshots))

simulation/smoke.py

  1 | """Smoke test entry point.
  2 | 
  3 | Run the default 150-agent structure, seeded with one infection, for
  4 | DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS days, across DEFAULT_SMOKE_SEEDS random seeds. For each
  5 | seed, print the daily per-state counts, then a one-line summary: peak
  6 | infected and the day it occurred, the seeded suite's final attack rate,
  7 | and how many distinct suites and floors were ever touched.
  8 | 
  9 |     python -m simulation.smoke
 10 | """
 11 | 
 12 | from __future__ import annotations
 13 | 
 14 | import sys
 15 | from pathlib import Path
 16 | 
 17 | sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
 18 | 
 19 | from simulation.config import (  # noqa: E402
 20 |     DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS,
 21 |     DEFAULT_SEED_INFECTIONS,
 22 |     DEFAULT_SMOKE_SEEDS,
 23 | )
 24 | from simulation.contact import suite_ancestor_ids  # noqa: E402
 25 | from simulation.infection import STATE_ORDER, InfectionState  # noqa: E402
 26 | from simulation.simulator import DayState, Simulation  # noqa: E402
 27 | 
 28 | 
 29 | def _peak(history: list[DayState]) -> tuple[int, int]:
 30 |     """(peak simultaneously-infectious count, day it was first reached)."""
 31 |     peak_count = -1
 32 |     peak_day = 0
 33 |     for snapshot in history:
 34 |         infectious_now = snapshot.count(InfectionState.INCUBATING) + snapshot.count(
 35 |             InfectionState.SYMPTOMATIC
 36 |         )
 37 |         if infectious_now > peak_count:
 38 |             peak_count = infectious_now
 39 |             peak_day = snapshot.day
 40 |     return peak_count, peak_day
 41 | 
 42 | 
 43 | def _summary(sim: Simulation, history: list[DayState]) -> str:
 44 |     # Identify the seeded suite from day 0, BEFORE any spread -- by the
 45 |     # end of the run other suites may also have non-susceptible agents.
 46 |     seeded_suite = next(
 47 |         a.suite_id for a in history[0].agents if a.state is not InfectionState.SUSCEPTIBLE
 48 |     )
 49 |     peak_count, peak_day = _peak(history)
 50 | 
 51 |     final = history[-1]
 52 |     suite_population = sum(1 for s in final.agents if s.suite_id == seeded_suite)
 53 |     suite_ever_infected = sum(
 54 |         1
 55 |         for s in final.agents
 56 |         if s.suite_id == seeded_suite and s.state is not InfectionState.SUSCEPTIBLE
 57 |     )
 58 |     attack_rate = suite_ever_infected / suite_population * 100
 59 | 
 60 |     touched_suites: set[str] = set()
 61 |     touched_floors: set[str] = set()
 62 |     for snapshot in history:
 63 |         for agent_snapshot in snapshot.agents:
 64 |             if agent_snapshot.state is not InfectionState.SUSCEPTIBLE:
 65 |                 touched_suites.add(agent_snapshot.suite_id)
 66 |     for suite_id in touched_suites:
 67 |         floor_id, _building_id = suite_ancestor_ids(sim.registry, suite_id)
 68 |         touched_floors.add(floor_id)
 69 | 
 70 |     return (
 71 |         f"  peak infected      : {peak_count} on day {peak_day}\n"
 72 |         f"  attack rate (seeded suite, n={suite_population}): {attack_rate:.1f}%\n"
 73 |         f"  suites ever touched: {len(touched_suites)}\n"
 74 |         f"  floors ever touched: {len(touched_floors)}"
 75 |     )
 76 | 
 77 | 
 78 | def _print_run(seed: int, days: int) -> None:
 79 |     sim = Simulation(seed=seed, seed_infections=DEFAULT_SEED_INFECTIONS)
 80 |     history = sim.run(days)
 81 | 
 82 |     columns = [state.name for state in STATE_ORDER]
 83 |     header = "day  " + "  ".join(f"{name:>11}" for name in columns)
 84 |     print(
 85 |         f"SEED {seed}   {len(sim.agents)} agents, "
 86 |         f"{DEFAULT_SEED_INFECTIONS} seeded, {days} days"
 87 |     )
 88 |     print(header)
 89 |     print("-" * len(header))
 90 |     for snapshot in history:
 91 |         cells = "  ".join(f"{snapshot.count(state):>11}" for state in STATE_ORDER)
 92 |         print(f"{snapshot.day:>3}  {cells}")
 93 |     print()
 94 |     print(_summary(sim, history))
 95 |     print()
 96 | 
 97 | 
 98 | def main() -> None:
 99 |     for seed in DEFAULT_SMOKE_SEEDS:
100 |         _print_run(seed, DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS)
101 | 
102 | 
103 | if __name__ == "__main__":
104 |     main()

api/__init__.py

 1 | """FastAPI layer for the MicroCluster outbreak simulator.
 2 | 
 3 | Detection and disclosure run SERVER SIDE only (CLAUDE.md, SECURITY
 4 | POSTURE). The API returns exactly what the floor-plan animation renders --
 5 | per-agent suite, position and infection state, plus that day's
 6 | detection/disclosure verdict -- and never a raw ``Report`` or any ground
 7 | truth beyond what is drawn on screen.
 8 | 
 9 | The ASGI app is ``api.app:app``; import it from there. ``api.bands`` is
10 | kept dependency-light (no FastAPI import) so other packages can reuse the
11 | count-banding rules.
12 | """

api/app.py

  1 | """HTTP API: start a run, then read one day at a time.
  2 | 
  3 | Endpoints
  4 | ---------
  5 | POST /api/run
  6 |     Body (all fields optional except nothing -- every field has a default):
  7 |         seed, days, seed_infections,
  8 |         suite_transmission_probability, floor_transmission_probability,
  9 |         building_transmission_probability,
 10 |         reporting_probability_min, reporting_probability_max,
 11 |         background_noise_daily_rate
 12 |     Returns: { run_id, seed, days, disclaimer, params, layout }
 13 | 
 14 | GET /api/run/{run_id}/day/{n}
 15 |     Returns the world at day n: every agent's suite, index within that
 16 |     suite, and infection state; the day's counts; and the
 17 |     detection/disclosure verdict. Nothing else.
 18 | 
 19 | The simulation's ground-truth firewall still holds: detection and
 20 | disclosure see only anonymous Reports and the ScopeRegistry (built inside
 21 | ``simulation.pipeline``), never agent state. This module then exposes the
 22 | agent states for *rendering* -- the same states the animation paints --
 23 | and the engine verdicts, and stops there.
 24 | """
 25 | 
 26 | from __future__ import annotations
 27 | 
 28 | import base64
 29 | import json
 30 | from dataclasses import dataclass
 31 | from pathlib import Path
 32 | 
 33 | from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
 34 | from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
 35 | from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
 36 | from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
 37 | 
 38 | from simulation.config import (
 39 |     DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS,
 40 |     DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG,
 41 |     SimulationConfig,
 42 | )
 43 | 
 44 | from microcluster.config import DEFAULT_DETECTION_CONFIG, DetectionConfig
 45 | 
 46 | from .bands import band, sanitize_reason
 47 | from simulation.contact import suite_ancestor_ids
 48 | from simulation.pipeline import (
 49 |     DailyRecord,
 50 |     SimulatedReports,
 51 |     analyze_report_stream,
 52 |     simulate_and_report,
 53 | )
 54 | from microcluster.config import DEFAULT_DISCLOSURE_CONFIG
 55 | from microcluster.disclosure import statistical_threshold
 56 | from simulation.population import StructureSpec
 57 | 
 58 | WEB_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "web"
 59 | RESULTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "results"
 60 | RESULT_FILES = ("disclosure_sweep", "benchmark", "adversarial")
 61 | 
 62 | MAX_DAYS = 120
 63 | MAX_RUNS_KEPT = 64  # in-memory only; oldest evicted past this
 64 | 
 65 | MIN_SUITE_SIZE = 21   # StructureSpec rejects agents_per_suite <= MIN_SCOPE_POPULATION (20)
 66 | MAX_SUITE_SIZE = 40
 67 | MIN_WINDOW_HOURS = 24
 68 | MAX_WINDOW_HOURS = 168
 69 | 
 70 | DISCLAIMER = (
 71 |     "Illustrative simulation. Transmission, reporting, contact and incubation values "
 72 |     "are chosen for demonstration, not measured from any real population. Tested at 150 to "
 73 |     "2,000 simulated people and against a harsher simulated reporting model, never against "
 74 |     "real reports. This predicts nothing about any real building, and no one is diagnosed "
 75 |     "or treated by this tool."
 76 | )
 77 | 
 78 | 
 79 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 80 | # In-memory run store
 81 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 82 | 
 83 | 
 84 | @dataclass
 85 | class StoredRun:
 86 |     seed: int
 87 |     days: int
 88 |     params: dict
 89 |     simulated: SimulatedReports
 90 |     daily_records: list[DailyRecord]
 91 |     # agent id -> (suite_id, floor_id, index within suite), computed once.
 92 |     agent_slot: dict[str, tuple[str, str, int]]
 93 |     layout: dict
 94 | 
 95 | 
 96 | RUNS: "dict[str, StoredRun]" = {}
 97 | _RUN_ORDER: list[str] = []
 98 | 
 99 | 
100 | def _remember(run_id: str, run: StoredRun) -> None:
101 |     if run_id in RUNS:
102 |         RUNS[run_id] = run
103 |         return
104 |     RUNS[run_id] = run
105 |     _RUN_ORDER.append(run_id)
106 |     while len(_RUN_ORDER) > MAX_RUNS_KEPT:
107 |         RUNS.pop(_RUN_ORDER.pop(0), None)
108 | 
109 | 
110 | # run id carries its own params so any process can rebuild the run
