111 | # deterministically after a restart or free-tier spin-down. RUNS stays a
112 | # pure cache
113 | def _encode_run_id(payload: dict) -> str:
114 |     raw = json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True).encode()
115 |     return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(raw).decode().rstrip("=")
116 | 
117 | 
118 | def _decode_run_id(run_id: str) -> "dict | None":
119 |     try:
120 |         pad = "=" * (-len(run_id) % 4)
121 |         data = json.loads(base64.urlsafe_b64decode(run_id + pad))
122 |     except Exception:
123 |         return None
124 |     return data if isinstance(data, dict) else None
125 | 
126 | 
127 | # results json read once at import, not per request
128 | _RESULTS_CACHE: "dict[str, dict]" = {}
129 | for _name in RESULT_FILES:
130 |     _p = RESULTS_DIR / f"{_name}.json"
131 |     if _p.is_file():
132 |         try:
133 |             _RESULTS_CACHE[_name] = json.loads(_p.read_text())
134 |         except Exception:
135 |             pass
136 | 
137 | 
138 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
139 | # Request / response models
140 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
141 | 
142 | 
143 | _OVERRIDABLE = (
144 |     "suite_transmission_probability",
145 |     "floor_transmission_probability",
146 |     "building_transmission_probability",
147 |     "reporting_probability_min",
148 |     "reporting_probability_max",
149 |     "background_noise_daily_rate",
150 | )
151 | 
152 | 
153 | class RunRequest(BaseModel):
154 |     seed: int = 1
155 |     days: int = Field(default=DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS, ge=1, le=MAX_DAYS)
156 |     seed_infections: int = Field(default=1, ge=0)
157 | 
158 |     population: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=MIN_SUITE_SIZE, le=MAX_SUITE_SIZE)
159 |     detection_window_hours: int | None = Field(
160 |         default=None, ge=MIN_WINDOW_HOURS, le=MAX_WINDOW_HOURS
161 |     )
162 | 
163 |     suite_transmission_probability: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
164 |     floor_transmission_probability: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
165 |     building_transmission_probability: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
166 |     reporting_probability_min: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
167 |     reporting_probability_max: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
168 |     background_noise_daily_rate: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
169 | 
170 |     def to_config(self) -> SimulationConfig:
171 |         base = DEFAULT_SIMULATION_CONFIG
172 |         values = {name: getattr(base, name) for name in _OVERRIDABLE}
173 |         for name in _OVERRIDABLE:
174 |             supplied = getattr(self, name)
175 |             if supplied is not None:
176 |                 values[name] = supplied
177 |         values["seed_infections"] = self.seed_infections
178 |         try:
179 |             return SimulationConfig(**values)
180 |         except ValueError as exc:
181 |             raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=str(exc)) from exc
182 | 
183 |     def to_spec(self) -> StructureSpec | None:
184 |         if self.population is None:
185 |             return None
186 |         return StructureSpec(agents_per_suite=self.population)
187 | 
188 |     def to_detection_config(self) -> DetectionConfig:
189 |         if self.detection_window_hours is None:
190 |             return DEFAULT_DETECTION_CONFIG
191 |         return DetectionConfig(detection_window_hours=self.detection_window_hours)
192 | 
193 | 
194 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
195 | # App
196 | # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
197 | 
198 | app = FastAPI(title="MicroCluster simulator", version="0.1")
199 | app.add_middleware(
200 |     CORSMiddleware,
201 |     allow_origins=["*"],
202 |     allow_methods=["*"],
203 |     allow_headers=["*"],
204 | )
205 | 
206 | 
207 | @app.middleware("http")
208 | async def _no_stale_assets(request, call_next):
209 |     # single-process demo, no CDN: make the browser revalidate every asset
210 |     # so an edited web/ file is never served from a stale cache
211 |     response = await call_next(request)
212 |     if not request.url.path.startswith("/api/"):
213 |         response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-cache, must-revalidate"
214 |     return response
215 | 
216 | 
217 | def _build_layout_and_slots(
218 |     simulated: SimulatedReports,
219 | ) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, tuple[str, str, int]]]:
220 |     registry = simulated.simulation.registry
221 | 
222 |     # Group agents by suite in their stable creation order.
223 |     suite_members: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
224 |     for agent in simulated.simulation.agents:
225 |         suite_members.setdefault(agent.suite_id, []).append(agent.id)
226 | 
227 |     agent_slot: dict[str, tuple[str, str, int]] = {}
228 |     suite_floor: dict[str, str] = {}
229 |     for suite_id, members in suite_members.items():
230 |         floor_id, _building_id = suite_ancestor_ids(registry, suite_id)
231 |         suite_floor[suite_id] = floor_id
232 |         for index, agent_id in enumerate(members):
233 |             agent_slot[agent_id] = (suite_id, floor_id, index)
234 | 
235 |     floors: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
236 |     for suite_id, floor_id in suite_floor.items():
237 |         floors.setdefault(floor_id, []).append(suite_id)
238 | 
239 |     layout = {
240 |         "floors": [
241 |             {
242 |                 "id": floor_id,
243 |                 "label": registry.get(floor_id).label if floor_id in registry.scopes else floor_id,
244 |                 "suites": [
245 |                     {
246 |                         "id": suite_id,
247 |                         "label": registry.get(suite_id).label,
248 |                         "size": len(suite_members[suite_id]),
249 |                     }
250 |                     for suite_id in sorted(suite_ids)
251 |                 ],
252 |             }
253 |             for floor_id, suite_ids in sorted(floors.items())
254 |         ]
255 |     }
256 |     return layout, agent_slot
257 | 
258 | 
259 | def _materialize(req: RunRequest) -> StoredRun:
260 |     config = req.to_config()
261 |     detection_config = req.to_detection_config()
262 |     try:
263 |         simulated = simulate_and_report(
264 |             seed=req.seed, days=req.days, spec=req.to_spec(), config=config
265 |         )
266 |     except ValueError as exc:
267 |         raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=str(exc)) from exc
268 |     daily_records = analyze_report_stream(simulated, detection_config=detection_config)
269 | 
270 |     layout, agent_slot = _build_layout_and_slots(simulated)
271 |     params = {
272 |         "population_per_suite": simulated.simulation.spec.agents_per_suite,
273 |         "total_population": len(simulated.simulation.agents),
274 |         "detection_window_hours": detection_config.detection_window_hours,
275 |         "suite_transmission_probability": config.suite_transmission_probability,
276 |         "floor_transmission_probability": config.floor_transmission_probability,
277 |         "building_transmission_probability": config.building_transmission_probability,
278 |         "reporting_probability_min": config.reporting_probability_min,
279 |         "reporting_probability_max": config.reporting_probability_max,
280 |         "background_noise_daily_rate": config.background_noise_daily_rate,
281 |         "seed_infections": config.seed_infections,
282 |     }
283 |     return StoredRun(
284 |         seed=req.seed,
285 |         days=req.days,
286 |         params=params,
287 |         simulated=simulated,
288 |         daily_records=daily_records,
289 |         agent_slot=agent_slot,
290 |         layout=layout,
291 |     )
292 | 
293 | 
294 | @app.post("/api/run")
295 | def start_run(req: RunRequest) -> dict:
296 |     run_id = _encode_run_id(req.model_dump(exclude_none=True))
297 |     run = RUNS.get(run_id) or _materialize(req)
298 |     _remember(run_id, run)
299 |     return {
300 |         "run_id": run_id,
301 |         "seed": req.seed,
302 |         "days": req.days,
303 |         "disclaimer": DISCLAIMER,
304 |         "params": run.params,
305 |         "layout": run.layout,
306 |     }
307 | 
308 | 
309 | @app.get("/api/layout")
310 | def get_layout(population: int | None = None) -> dict:
311 |     # pre-run empty state: the building structure before anything happens.
312 |     # 1-day sim just to build the population n registry, no run stored
313 |     fields: dict = {"days": 1}
314 |     if population is not None:
315 |         fields["population"] = population
316 |     try:
317 |         req = RunRequest(**fields)
318 |     except Exception as exc:
319 |         raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=str(exc)) from exc
320 |     run = _materialize(req)
321 |     return {"layout": run.layout, "params": run.params, "disclaimer": DISCLAIMER}
322 | 
323 | 
324 | @app.get("/api/run/{run_id}/day/{n}")
325 | def get_day(run_id: str, n: int) -> dict:
326 |     run = RUNS.get(run_id)
327 |     if run is None:
328 |         # cache miss: rebuild from the params baked into the id
329 |         payload = _decode_run_id(run_id)
330 |         if payload is None:
331 |             raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="unknown run id (start a new run)")
332 |         try:
333 |             run = _materialize(RunRequest(**payload))
334 |         except HTTPException:
335 |             raise
336 |         except Exception:
337 |             raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="unknown run id (start a new run)")
338 |         _remember(run_id, run)
339 |     if n < 0 or n > run.days:
340 |         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"day {n} out of range 0..{run.days}")
341 | 
342 |     snapshot = run.simulated.simulation.history[n]
343 |     record = run.daily_records[n]
344 |     registry = run.simulated.simulation.registry
345 | 
346 |     agents = []
347 |     for agent_snapshot in snapshot.agents:
348 |         suite_id, floor_id, index = run.agent_slot[agent_snapshot.id]
349 |         agents.append(
350 |             {
351 |                 "suite": suite_id,
352 |                 "floor": floor_id,
353 |                 "i": index,
354 |                 "state": agent_snapshot.state.value,
355 |             }
356 |         )
357 | 
358 |     disclosure = None
359 |     if record.disclosed_scope_id is not None:
360 |         scope = registry.get(record.disclosed_scope_id)
361 |         disclosure = {
362 |             "scope_id": scope.id,
363 |             "label": scope.label,
364 |             "level": scope.level.name,
365 |         }
366 | 
367 |     # The gate table (rule 12), ordered coarsest -> finest so the
368 |     # resolution slider can walk it directly. Exact qualifying counts and
369 |     # the exact report fraction are dropped here and never sent; only the
370 |     # band leaves the server (see api/bands.py).
371 |     evaluations = [
372 |         {
373 |             "scope_id": ev.scope_id,
374 |             "label": ev.scope_label,
375 |             "level": ev.scope_level.name,
376 |             "population": ev.population,
377 |             "qualifying_band": band(ev.qualifying_reports),
378 |             "statistical_threshold": ev.statistical_threshold,
379 |             "statistical_pass": ev.statistical_pass,
380 |             "min_population_pass": ev.min_population_pass,
381 |             "report_fraction_pass": ev.report_fraction_pass,
382 |             "privacy_pass": ev.privacy_pass,
383 |             "eligible": ev.disclosure_eligible,
384 |             "selected": ev.selected,
385 |             "reason": sanitize_reason(ev.disclosure_reason),
386 |         }
387 |         for ev in sorted(
388 |             record.disclosure_evaluations,
389 |             key=lambda e: (int(e.scope_level), -e.population, e.scope_id),
390 |         )
391 |     ]
392 | 
393 |     return {
394 |         "day": n,
395 |         "counts": {state.value: snapshot.counts[state] for state in snapshot.counts},
396 |         "agents": agents,
397 |         "detection": {
398 |             "fired": record.detection_fired,
399 |             "relative": record.relative_fired,
400 |             "absolute": record.absolute_fired,
401 |         },
402 |         "disclosure": disclosure,
403 |         "evaluations": evaluations,
404 |         "first_fired_day": record.first_fired_day,
405 |         "first_disclosed_day": record.first_disclosed_day,
406 |         "disclaimer": DISCLAIMER,
407 |     }
408 | 
409 | 
410 | @app.get("/api/results/{name}")
411 | def get_results(name: str) -> dict:
412 |     if name not in RESULT_FILES:
413 |         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"unknown results file: {name!r}")
414 |     if name in _RESULTS_CACHE:
415 |         return _RESULTS_CACHE[name]
416 |     path = RESULTS_DIR / f"{name}.json"
417 |     if not path.is_file():
418 |         raise HTTPException(
419 |             status_code=503,
420 |             detail="results not precomputed; run python -m scripts.precompute_results",
421 |         )
422 |     _RESULTS_CACHE[name] = json.loads(path.read_text())
423 |     return _RESULTS_CACHE[name]
424 | 
425 | 
426 | @app.get("/api/gates")
427 | def get_gates() -> dict:
428 |     """Worked examples for the gate explanations, computed by the engine's
429 |     own threshold function and the configured privacy constants -- never
430 |     typed into the page. Populations are the default structure's scope
431 |     sizes (suite / floor / building)."""
432 |     spec = StructureSpec()
433 |     suite = spec.agents_per_suite
434 |     floor = suite * spec.suites_per_floor
435 |     building = floor * spec.floors_per_building
436 |     cfg = DEFAULT_DISCLOSURE_CONFIG
437 |     scopes = (("Suite", suite), ("Floor", floor), ("Building", building))
438 | 
439 |     def first_refused(n: int) -> int:
440 |         # smallest report count the privacy gate refuses: the same
441 |         # comparison disclosure.py makes, qualifying / n <= max fraction
442 |         q = 0
443 |         while q / n <= cfg.max_report_fraction:
444 |             q += 1
445 |         return q
446 | 
447 |     return {
448 |         "statistical": {
449 |             "floor": cfg.statistical_gate_floor,
450 |             "rows": [
451 |                 {"scope": name, "population": n, "required": statistical_threshold(n, cfg)}
452 |                 for name, n in scopes
453 |             ],
454 |         },
455 |         "privacy": {
456 |             "min_population": cfg.min_scope_population,
457 |             "max_fraction": cfg.max_report_fraction,
458 |             "rows": [
459 |                 {
460 |                     "scope": name,
461 |                     "population": n,
462 |                     "meets_minimum": n >= cfg.min_scope_population,
463 |                     "refused_from": first_refused(n),
464 |                 }
465 |                 for name, n in scopes
466 |             ],
467 |         },
468 |     }

api/bands.py

 1 | """Report-count banding for the API's presentation boundary.
 2 | 
 3 | PRIVACY REQUIREMENT, not a styling choice. A ``ScopeEvaluation`` carries
 4 | the exact qualifying-report count for a scope. If that exact integer is
 5 | visible in the UI on two consecutive days that both name the same scope,
 6 | an observer subtracts the two and learns that exactly one identifiable
 7 | person became ill (or recovered) overnight -- a differencing attack that
 8 | would be live in the shipped product.
 9 | 
10 | So the exact count never leaves the server. Every count the API emits is
11 | first mapped to a BAND. The band edges below are a privacy policy, named
12 | here the same way every threshold in ``microcluster/config.py`` is:
13 | wider bands are safer and coarser; these are a chosen starting point, not
14 | a proven-safe value.
15 | 
16 | The same rule reaches the human-readable ``disclosure_reason`` strings,
17 | which sometimes embed a raw count ("7 qualifying reports < required 45"):
18 | :func:`sanitize_reason` rewrites those to the band before the string is
19 | sent.
20 | """
21 | 
22 | from __future__ import annotations
23 | 
24 | import re
25 | 
26 | # Lower bound of each band. A count of N lands in the band [lo, next_lo - 1]
27 | # for the largest lo <= N; at or above the last edge it is "<edge>+".
28 | # Chosen so the smallest bands ("1-4", "5-9", "10-19") are narrow enough
29 | # to stay informative and wide enough that a one-person night-to-night
30 | # change cannot be read off two consecutive disclosures.
31 | BAND_LOWER_BOUNDS: tuple[int, ...] = (1, 5, 10, 20, 50, 100)
32 | 
33 | 
34 | def band(count: int) -> str:
35 |     """Map an exact count to its band label ("0", "1-4", ..., "100+")."""
36 |     if count <= 0:
37 |         return "0"
38 |     edges = BAND_LOWER_BOUNDS
39 |     if count >= edges[-1]:
40 |         return f"{edges[-1]}+"
41 |     for lo, hi in zip(edges, edges[1:]):
42 |         if lo <= count < hi:
43 |             return f"{lo}-{hi - 1}" if hi - 1 > lo else f"{lo}"
44 |     # count in [1, edges[0]) is impossible while edges[0] == 1, but stay safe.
45 |     return f"1-{edges[0] - 1}"
46 | 
47 | 
48 | def band_interval(label: str) -> tuple[int, int | None]:
49 |     """Inverse of :func:`band`: the inclusive ``[lo, hi]`` a label stands
50 |     for. ``hi`` is ``None`` for the open-topped ``"<n>+"`` band."""
51 |     if label == "0":
52 |         return (0, 0)
53 |     if label.endswith("+"):
54 |         return (int(label[:-1]), None)
55 |     lo, hi = label.split("-")
56 |     return (int(lo), int(hi))
57 | 
58 | 
59 | _QUALIFYING_PAREN = re.compile(r"\((\d+) qualifying reports")
60 | _QUALIFYING_COUNT = re.compile(r"qualifying count \((\d+)\)")
61 | _SCOPES_COUNT = re.compile(r"scope's \((\d+)\)")
62 | _FRACTION = re.compile(r"report fraction \d+(?:\.\d+)? exceeds maximum (\d+(?:\.\d+)?)")
63 | 
64 | 
65 | def sanitize_reason(reason: str) -> str:
66 |     """Rewrite every raw report count inside a ``disclosure_reason`` to its
67 |     band, and blur the exact report fraction. Population, statistical
68 |     thresholds and the switch margin are public policy numbers and are
69 |     left untouched.
70 | 
71 |     Targets the exact phrasings ``microcluster.disclosure`` produces; a
72 |     reason with no embedded count passes through unchanged.
73 |     """
74 |     out = _QUALIFYING_PAREN.sub(
75 |         lambda m: f"({band(int(m.group(1)))} qualifying reports", reason
76 |     )
77 |     out = _QUALIFYING_COUNT.sub(
78 |         lambda m: f"qualifying count ({band(int(m.group(1)))})", out
79 |     )
80 |     out = _SCOPES_COUNT.sub(lambda m: f"scope's ({band(int(m.group(1)))})", out)
81 |     out = _FRACTION.sub(
82 |         r"report fraction over the limit of \1", out
83 |     )
84 |     return out

adversarial/__init__.py

 1 | """Adversarial self-test for the privacy claim.
 2 | 
 3 | Models an observer who sees only what the shipped UI shows -- day by day,
 4 | the disclosed scope and its reported report-count band -- and runs two
 5 | attacks against that stream:
 6 | 
 7 |   * the DIFFERENCING attack (adversarial.differencing): infer the
 8 |     overnight change in a named group from consecutive disclosures. Run
 9 |     against exact counts (the pre-band behaviour) and against bands; the
10 |     contrast is the result.
11 |   * SCOPE WANDERING (adversarial.wandering): how many distinct groups get
12 |     named across a run, scope stability on versus off.
13 |   * REPORT INJECTION (adversarial.injection): how many fabricated reports,
14 |     and how many sessions, it takes to manufacture a false disclosure --
15 |     with and without the intake submission cap.
16 | 
17 | The point is to make the privacy claim demonstrable. Where a defence only
18 | reduces leakage rather than removing it, that is reported as such.
19 | """
20 | 
21 | from .differencing import DifferencingOutcome, attack
22 | from .injection import InjectionResult, measure_injection
23 | from .observer import DayView, observe
24 | from .wandering import WanderingOutcome, measure_wandering
25 | 
26 | __all__ = [
27 |     "DayView",
28 |     "observe",
29 |     "DifferencingOutcome",
30 |     "attack",
31 |     "WanderingOutcome",
32 |     "measure_wandering",
33 |     "InjectionResult",
34 |     "measure_injection",
35 | ]

adversarial/differencing.py

  1 | """The differencing attack.
  2 | 
  3 | The observer takes two consecutive days that disclose the SAME scope and
  4 | asks how many individuals' reports changed overnight. The disclosed
  5 | per-scope qualifying count is a running total over the 72h detection
  6 | window, so the overnight change is::
  7 | 
  8 |     delta = count(day d+1) - count(day d)
  9 | 
 10 | With EXACT counts the observer learns ``delta`` as a single integer; when
 11 | ``abs(delta) == 1`` that is one identifiable person's report appearing or
 12 | ageing out of the window overnight -- a pinned one-person change.
 13 | 
 14 | With BANDS the observer knows only ``[lo, hi]`` for each day, so ``delta``
 15 | is an interval ``[lo(d+1) - hi(d), hi(d+1) - lo(d)]`` -- normally many
 16 | integers wide, and never a pinned single person.
 17 | """
 18 | 
 19 | from __future__ import annotations
 20 | 
 21 | from dataclasses import dataclass
 22 | from statistics import median
 23 | 
 24 | from api.bands import band_interval
 25 | 
 26 | from .observer import DayView
 27 | 
 28 | _NEG_INF = float("-inf")
 29 | _POS_INF = float("inf")
 30 | 
 31 | 
 32 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
 33 | class _Pair:
 34 |     scope_label: str
 35 |     day_from: int
 36 |     delta_low: float
 37 |     delta_high: float
 38 | 
 39 |     @property
 40 |     def known_exactly(self) -> bool:
 41 |         return self.delta_low == self.delta_high
 42 | 
 43 |     @property
 44 |     def unbounded(self) -> bool:
 45 |         return self.delta_low == _NEG_INF or self.delta_high == _POS_INF
 46 | 
 47 |     @property
 48 |     def possible_values(self) -> float:
 49 |         return _POS_INF if self.unbounded else self.delta_high - self.delta_low + 1
 50 | 
 51 |     @property
 52 |     def one_person_pinned(self) -> bool:
 53 |         return self.known_exactly and abs(self.delta_low) == 1
 54 | 
 55 |     @property
 56 |     def direction_known(self) -> bool:
 57 |         return not self.unbounded and not (self.delta_low <= 0 <= self.delta_high)
 58 | 
 59 | 
 60 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
 61 | class DifferencingOutcome:
 62 |     mode: str
 63 |     same_scope_pairs: int
 64 |     pinned_exactly: int
 65 |     one_person_pinned: int
 66 |     direction_known: int
 67 |     unbounded_pairs: int
 68 |     median_possible_values: float
 69 |     residual: str
 70 | 
 71 | 
 72 | def _delta_bounds(a: DayView, b: DayView) -> tuple[float, float]:
 73 |     if a.exact_count is not None and b.exact_count is not None:
 74 |         d = float(b.exact_count - a.exact_count)
 75 |         return d, d
 76 |     lo_a, hi_a = band_interval(a.band)
 77 |     lo_b, hi_b = band_interval(b.band)
 78 |     low = _NEG_INF if hi_a is None else float(lo_b - hi_a)
 79 |     high = _POS_INF if hi_b is None else float(hi_b - lo_a)
 80 |     return low, high
 81 | 
 82 | 
 83 | def _pairs(views: list[DayView]) -> list[_Pair]:
 84 |     out: list[_Pair] = []
 85 |     for a, b in zip(views, views[1:]):
 86 |         if a.scope_id is None or a.scope_id != b.scope_id:
 87 |             continue
 88 |         low, high = _delta_bounds(a, b)
 89 |         out.append(_Pair(a.scope_label, a.day, low, high))
 90 |     return out
 91 | 
 92 | 
 93 | def _residual(mode: str, pairs: list[_Pair]) -> str:
 94 |     if not pairs:
 95 |         return "no scope was disclosed on two consecutive days"
 96 |     if mode == "exact":
 97 |         return "overnight change in the named group is known to the exact person"
 98 |     return (
 99 |         "the named group is still named; its count stays bracketed to a band; "
100 |         "the overnight change stays a wide interval"
101 |     )
102 | 
103 | 
104 | def attack(views: list[DayView], *, mode: str) -> DifferencingOutcome:
105 |     """Run the differencing attack over one observed run.
106 | 
107 |     ``mode`` is ``"exact"`` or ``"band"`` and only labels the result --
108 |     which one actually applies is decided by whichever field
109 |     :func:`adversarial.observer.observe` populated.
110 |     """
111 |     pairs = _pairs(views)
112 |     finite = [p.possible_values for p in pairs if not p.unbounded]
113 |     return DifferencingOutcome(
114 |         mode=mode,
115 |         same_scope_pairs=len(pairs),
116 |         pinned_exactly=sum(1 for p in pairs if p.known_exactly),
117 |         one_person_pinned=sum(1 for p in pairs if p.one_person_pinned),
118 |         direction_known=sum(1 for p in pairs if p.direction_known),
119 |         unbounded_pairs=sum(1 for p in pairs if p.unbounded),
120 |         median_possible_values=median(finite) if finite else _POS_INF,
121 |         residual=_residual(mode, pairs),
122 |     )

adversarial/injection.py

  1 | """What the intake submission cap actually buys against report injection.
  2 | 
  3 | An attacker wants the system to disclose a cluster that is not real, at a
  4 | scope of their choosing. They fabricate reports: a fixed category, tagged
  5 | to suites under the target scope, all inside one detection window, each
  6 | carrying a ``session_id`` the attacker picks freely.
  7 | 
  8 | This module measures the cost of that attack with and without
  9 | ``microcluster.intake``'s per-session cap in force. The result is
 10 | deliberately unflattering: the cap does not reduce the number of
 11 | fabricated reports required, it only forces the attacker to spread them
 12 | across ``ceil(reports / session_cap)`` sessions instead of one. A
 13 | ``session_id`` is an unauthenticated, client-chosen string, so that is a
 14 | near-zero cost for a determined attacker. The cap stops a naive
 15 | single-session flood and nothing more.
 16 | """
 17 | 
 18 | from __future__ import annotations
 19 | 
 20 | import math
 21 | import random
 22 | from dataclasses import dataclass
 23 | from datetime import datetime, timezone
 24 | 
 25 | from microcluster.config import DEFAULT_INTAKE_CONFIG, IntakeConfig
 26 | from microcluster.engine import analyze
 27 | from microcluster.models import CHECKLIST, Category, Onset, Report, ScopeLevel, ScopeRegistry
 28 | from simulation.population import StructureSpec, build_population
 29 | 
 30 | _WINDOW_INSTANT = datetime(2026, 1, 10, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
 31 | _FAKE_CATEGORY = Category.GASTROINTESTINAL
 32 | _FAKE_SYMPTOM = CHECKLIST[_FAKE_CATEGORY][0]
 33 | 
 34 | # Cap per suite when the target is a FLOOR, so no single suite clears its
 35 | # own (finer) statistical gate and the disclosure that appears is the
 36 | # floor -- not a suite the attacker did not aim at. ceil(sqrt(25)) = 5, so
 37 | # 4 per suite is the most that keeps every suite gate failing.
 38 | _PER_SUITE_MAX_FOR_FLOOR = 4
 39 | 
 40 | 
 41 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
 42 | class InjectionResult:
 43 |     target_scope_id: str
 44 |     target_level: str
 45 |     target_population: int
 46 | 
 47 |     reports_to_disclose: int          # fabricated reports needed, cap or no cap
 48 |     sessions_without_cap: int         # = 1
 49 |     sessions_with_cap: int            # = ceil(reports / session_submission_cap)
 50 |     session_cap: int
 51 | 
 52 |     single_session_attack_succeeds: bool   # attacker uses ONE session, cap on
 53 |     multi_session_attack_succeeds: bool     # attacker uses ceil(k/cap) sessions, cap on
 54 | 
 55 | 
 56 | def _suites_under(registry: ScopeRegistry, scope_id: str) -> list[str]:
 57 |     target = registry.get(scope_id)
 58 |     if target.level is ScopeLevel.SUITE:
 59 |         return [scope_id]
 60 |     return sorted(
 61 |         s.id for s in registry.scopes.values()
 62 |         if s.level is ScopeLevel.SUITE and registry.contains(scope_id, s.id)
 63 |     )
 64 | 
 65 | 
 66 | def _fabricate(
 67 |     suites: list[str], n_reports: int, per_suite_max: int, session_ids: list[str | None]
 68 | ) -> list[Report]:
 69 |     reports: list[Report] = []
 70 |     per_suite = {s: 0 for s in suites}
 71 |     si = 0
 72 |     while len(reports) < n_reports:
 73 |         suite = suites[si % len(suites)]
 74 |         si += 1
 75 |         if per_suite[suite] >= per_suite_max:
 76 |             if all(per_suite[s] >= per_suite_max for s in suites):
 77 |                 break
 78 |             continue
 79 |         per_suite[suite] += 1
 80 |         reports.append(
 81 |             Report(
 82 |                 category=_FAKE_CATEGORY,
 83 |                 symptom=_FAKE_SYMPTOM,
 84 |                 onset=Onset.TODAY,
 85 |                 location_id=suite,
 86 |                 submitted_at=_WINDOW_INSTANT,
 87 |                 session_id=session_ids[len(reports) % len(session_ids)],
 88 |             )
 89 |         )
 90 |     return reports
 91 | 
 92 | 
 93 | def _discloses_target(
 94 |     reports: list[Report], registry: ScopeRegistry, target_scope_id: str,
 95 |     intake_config: IntakeConfig | None,
 96 | ) -> bool:
 97 |     result = analyze(reports, registry, now=_WINDOW_INSTANT, intake_config=intake_config)
 98 |     return result.disclosure.disclosed_scope_id == target_scope_id
 99 | 
100 | 
101 | def measure_injection(
102 |     target_scope_id: str = "C-B1-F2",
103 |     *,
104 |     intake_config: IntakeConfig = DEFAULT_INTAKE_CONFIG,
105 |     max_reports: int = 60,
106 | ) -> InjectionResult:
107 |     """Measure the injection attack against ``target_scope_id`` (a FLOOR by
108 |     default -- the representative mid scope, declared population 75)."""
109 |     _, registry = build_population(spec=StructureSpec(), rng=random.Random(0))
110 |     target = registry.get(target_scope_id)
111 |     suites = _suites_under(registry, target_scope_id)
112 |     per_suite_max = (
113 |         max_reports if target.level is ScopeLevel.SUITE else _PER_SUITE_MAX_FOR_FLOOR
114 |     )
115 |     cap = intake_config.session_submission_cap
116 | 
117 |     # 1. reports needed when the attacker is NOT rate-limited (one session,
118 |     #    or none): smallest k whose fabricated reports name the target.
119 |     reports_needed = 0
120 |     for k in range(1, max_reports + 1):
121 |         fab = _fabricate(suites, k, per_suite_max, session_ids=[None])
122 |         if len(fab) < k:
123 |             break  # ran out of room under per_suite_max
124 |         if _discloses_target(fab, registry, target_scope_id, intake_config=None):
125 |             reports_needed = k
126 |             break
127 |     if reports_needed == 0:
128 |         raise RuntimeError(f"could not manufacture a disclosure at {target_scope_id}")
129 | 
130 |     sessions_with_cap = math.ceil(reports_needed / cap)
131 | 
132 |     # 2. cap ON, attacker uses ONE session -> only `cap` reports survive.
133 |     one_session = _fabricate(
134 |         suites, reports_needed, per_suite_max, session_ids=["attacker-1"]
135 |     )
136 |     single_ok = _discloses_target(
137 |         one_session, registry, target_scope_id, intake_config=intake_config
138 |     )
139 | 
140 |     # 3. cap ON, attacker uses ceil(k/cap) sessions -> all reports survive.
141 |     sids: list[str | None] = [
142 |         f"attacker-{i // cap}" for i in range(reports_needed)
143 |     ]
144 |     many_sessions = _fabricate(suites, reports_needed, per_suite_max, session_ids=sids)
145 |     multi_ok = _discloses_target(
146 |         many_sessions, registry, target_scope_id, intake_config=intake_config
147 |     )
148 | 
149 |     return InjectionResult(
150 |         target_scope_id=target_scope_id,
151 |         target_level=target.level.name,
152 |         target_population=target.population,
153 |         reports_to_disclose=reports_needed,
154 |         sessions_without_cap=1,
155 |         sessions_with_cap=sessions_with_cap,
156 |         session_cap=cap,
157 |         single_session_attack_succeeds=single_ok,
158 |         multi_session_attack_succeeds=multi_ok,
159 |     )
160 | 
161 | 
162 | def format_result(r: InjectionResult) -> str:
163 |     return "\n".join([
164 |         "=" * 78,
165 |         f"REPORT-INJECTION ATTACK   target {r.target_scope_id} "
166 |         f"({r.target_level}, declared population {r.target_population})",
167 |         "=" * 78,
168 |         "",
169 |         f"  fabricated reports to manufacture the disclosure : {r.reports_to_disclose}",
170 |         f"    (the statistical gate for this scope; the cap does not change it)",
171 |         "",
172 |         f"  sources needed, NO per-session cap               : "
173 |         f"{r.sessions_without_cap}",
174 |         f"  sources needed, per-session cap = {r.session_cap}              : "
175 |         f"{r.sessions_with_cap}   (= ceil({r.reports_to_disclose} / {r.session_cap}))",
176 |         "",
177 |         f"  cap on, attacker uses 1 session  -> disclosure?  : "
178 |         f"{'YES' if r.single_session_attack_succeeds else 'NO (attack blocked)'}",
179 |         f"  cap on, attacker uses {r.sessions_with_cap} sessions -> disclosure?  : "
180 |         f"{'YES (attack succeeds)' if r.multi_session_attack_succeeds else 'NO'}",
181 |         "",
182 |         "HONEST READ",
183 |         "  The cap stops a single-session flood. It does not stop the attack: a",
184 |         f"  session_id is a free, unauthenticated string, and ceil(k / {r.session_cap}) of them",
185 |         "  is a near-zero cost. Injection is possible for any attacker willing to",
186 |         "  rotate sessions. Closing this needs identity or session binding, which the",
187 |         "  system deliberately does not have.",
188 |     ])
189 | 
190 | 
191 | def main() -> None:
192 |     print(format_result(measure_injection()))
193 | 
194 | 
195 | if __name__ == "__main__":
196 |     main()

adversarial/observer.py

 1 | """What a day-by-day observer of the shipped UI can see, and nothing else."""
 2 | 
 3 | from __future__ import annotations
 4 | 
 5 | from dataclasses import dataclass
 6 | 
 7 | from api.bands import band
 8 | from simulation.pipeline import DailyRecord
 9 | 
10 | 
11 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
12 | class DayView:
13 |     """One day as the observer sees it.
14 | 
15 |     Exactly one of ``exact_count`` / ``band`` is populated: ``exact_count``
16 |     for the pre-band scenario, ``band`` for the shipped one. A day with
17 |     nothing disclosed leaves every field but ``day`` as ``None``.
18 |     """
19 | 
20 |     day: int
21 |     scope_id: str | None
22 |     scope_label: str | None
23 |     scope_level: str | None
24 |     exact_count: int | None
25 |     band: str | None
26 | 
27 | 
28 | def _selected_evaluation(record: DailyRecord):
29 |     for ev in record.disclosure_evaluations:
30 |         if ev.scope_id == record.disclosed_scope_id:
31 |             return ev
32 |     return None
33 | 
34 | 
35 | def observe(records: list[DailyRecord], *, leak_exact_counts: bool) -> list[DayView]:
36 |     """Reduce a run's daily records to the observer's view.
37 | 
38 |     With ``leak_exact_counts=True`` the observer is handed the exact
39 |     qualifying-report count of the disclosed scope (what the UI showed
40 |     before banding). With ``False`` they get only the band.
41 |     """
42 |     views: list[DayView] = []
43 |     for record in records:
44 |         ev = _selected_evaluation(record)
45 |         if ev is None:
46 |             views.append(DayView(record.day, None, None, None, None, None))
47 |             continue
48 |         views.append(
49 |             DayView(
50 |                 day=record.day,
51 |                 scope_id=ev.scope_id,
52 |                 scope_label=ev.scope_label,
53 |                 scope_level=ev.scope_level.name,
54 |                 exact_count=ev.qualifying_reports if leak_exact_counts else None,
55 |                 band=None if leak_exact_counts else band(ev.qualifying_reports),
56 |             )
57 |         )
58 |     return views

adversarial/selftest.py

  1 | """Run the adversarial self-test across many seeds and summarise."""
  2 | 
  3 | from __future__ import annotations
  4 | 
  5 | from dataclasses import dataclass
  6 | from statistics import mean, median
  7 | from typing import Sequence
  8 | 
  9 | from simulation.config import DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS
 10 | from simulation.pipeline import analyze_report_stream, simulate_and_report
 11 | 
 12 | from .differencing import attack
 13 | from .injection import format_result, measure_injection
 14 | from .observer import observe
 15 | from .wandering import measure_wandering
 16 | 
 17 | DEFAULT_SEEDS = 200
 18 | 
 19 | 
 20 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
 21 | class SelfTestSummary:
 22 |     n_runs: int
 23 |     days: int
 24 | 
 25 |     same_scope_pairs: int
 26 |     exact_one_person_pinned: int
 27 |     band_one_person_pinned: int
 28 |     exact_direction_known: int
 29 |     band_direction_known: int
 30 |     band_median_possible_values: float
 31 |     band_residual: str
 32 | 
 33 |     wandering_on_mean: float
 34 |     wandering_off_mean: float
 35 |     wandering_on_max: int
 36 |     wandering_off_max: int
 37 | 
 38 | 
 39 | def run_self_test(
 40 |     seeds: Sequence[int] | None = None,
 41 |     *,
 42 |     n_seeds: int = DEFAULT_SEEDS,
 43 |     days: int = DEFAULT_RUN_DAYS,
 44 | ) -> SelfTestSummary:
 45 |     seed_list = list(seeds) if seeds is not None else list(range(1, n_seeds + 1))
 46 | 
 47 |     pairs = 0
 48 |     exact_pins = band_pins = 0
 49 |     exact_dir = band_dir = 0
 50 |     band_spans: list[float] = []
 51 |     band_residual = "no consecutive same-scope disclosures in any run"
 52 |     wander_on: list[int] = []
 53 |     wander_off: list[int] = []
 54 | 
 55 |     for seed in seed_list:
 56 |         simulated = simulate_and_report(seed=seed, days=days)
 57 |         records = analyze_report_stream(simulated)
 58 | 
 59 |         exact = attack(observe(records, leak_exact_counts=True), mode="exact")
 60 |         band = attack(observe(records, leak_exact_counts=False), mode="band")
 61 | 
 62 |         pairs += exact.same_scope_pairs
 63 |         exact_pins += exact.one_person_pinned
 64 |         band_pins += band.one_person_pinned
 65 |         exact_dir += exact.direction_known
 66 |         band_dir += band.direction_known
 67 |         if band.same_scope_pairs and band.median_possible_values != float("inf"):
 68 |             band_spans.append(band.median_possible_values)
 69 |             band_residual = band.residual
 70 | 
 71 |         w = measure_wandering(simulated)
 72 |         wander_on.append(w.stability_on)
 73 |         wander_off.append(w.stability_off)
 74 | 
 75 |     return SelfTestSummary(
 76 |         n_runs=len(seed_list),
 77 |         days=days,
 78 |         same_scope_pairs=pairs,
 79 |         exact_one_person_pinned=exact_pins,
 80 |         band_one_person_pinned=band_pins,
 81 |         exact_direction_known=exact_dir,
 82 |         band_direction_known=band_dir,
 83 |         band_median_possible_values=median(band_spans) if band_spans else float("inf"),
 84 |         band_residual=band_residual,
 85 |         wandering_on_mean=mean(wander_on) if wander_on else 0.0,
 86 |         wandering_off_mean=mean(wander_off) if wander_off else 0.0,
 87 |         wandering_on_max=max(wander_on) if wander_on else 0,
 88 |         wandering_off_max=max(wander_off) if wander_off else 0,
 89 |     )
 90 | 
 91 | 
 92 | def format_summary(s: SelfTestSummary) -> str:
 93 |     lines = [
 94 |         "=" * 78,
 95 |         f"ADVERSARIAL SELF-TEST   ({s.n_runs} runs, {s.days}-day)",
 96 |         "observer sees only: the disclosed scope, and its report count as the UI shows it",
 97 |         "=" * 78,
 98 |         "",
 99 |         "DIFFERENCING ATTACK",
100 |         f"  consecutive same-scope disclosures            : {s.same_scope_pairs} day-pairs",
101 |         "",
102 |         f"  >>> pre-band (EXACT counts) one-person pins   : {s.exact_one_person_pinned}",
103 |         f"  >>> shipped  (BANDS)        one-person pins   : {s.band_one_person_pinned}",
104 |         "",
105 |         f"  exact  : overnight change forced to a known sign on {s.exact_direction_known}/{s.same_scope_pairs} pairs",
106 |         f"  bands  : overnight change forced to a known sign on {s.band_direction_known}/{s.same_scope_pairs} pairs",
107 |         f"  bands  : residual per pair -- {s.band_residual}",
108 |         f"           median count of still-possible overnight deltas : "
109 |         f"{_fmt(s.band_median_possible_values)}",
110 |         "",
111 |         "SCOPE WANDERING   (distinct groups named across a run)",
112 |         f"  scope stability ON   : mean {s.wandering_on_mean:.2f}   max {s.wandering_on_max}",
113 |         f"  scope stability OFF  : mean {s.wandering_off_mean:.2f}   max {s.wandering_off_max}",
114 |         "",
115 |         "HONEST READ",
116 |         "  Bands remove the one-person differencing pin entirely. They do NOT make the",
117 |         "  observer learn nothing: the named group is still named, its activity is still",
118 |         "  bracketed to a band, and on many pairs the direction of change is still forced.",
119 |         "  Scope stability shrinks the set of groups ever named but does not hide the",
120 |         "  lineage the disclosure walks. This is leakage reduced, not leakage removed.",
121 |     ]
122 |     return "\n".join(lines)
123 | 
124 | 
125 | def _fmt(value: float) -> str:
126 |     return "unbounded" if value == float("inf") else f"{value:.0f}"
127 | 
128 | 
129 | def main() -> None:
130 |     print(format_summary(run_self_test()))
131 |     print()
132 |     print(format_result(measure_injection()))
133 | 
134 | 
135 | if __name__ == "__main__":
136 |     main()

adversarial/wandering.py

 1 | """What scope wandering leaks: distinct groups named across a run.
 2 | 
 3 | Every distinct scope the system ever discloses is a group the observer
 4 | learns about. Scope stability (config.SIBLING_SWITCH_MARGIN) exists to
 5 | keep that set small. This measures it against the same run analysed with
 6 | stability threading turned off.
 7 | """
 8 | 
 9 | from __future__ import annotations
10 | 
11 | from dataclasses import dataclass
12 | 
13 | from simulation.pipeline import DailyRecord, SimulatedReports, analyze_report_stream
14 | 
15 | 
16 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
17 | class WanderingOutcome:
18 |     stability_on: int
19 |     stability_off: int
20 |     names_on: tuple[str, ...]
21 |     names_off: tuple[str, ...]
22 | 
23 | 
24 | def _distinct_named(records: list[DailyRecord]) -> list[str]:
25 |     seen: list[str] = []
26 |     for record in records:
27 |         sid = record.disclosed_scope_id
28 |         if sid is not None and sid not in seen:
29 |             seen.append(sid)
30 |     return seen
31 | 
32 | 
33 | def measure_wandering(simulated: SimulatedReports) -> WanderingOutcome:
34 |     on = _distinct_named(analyze_report_stream(simulated))
35 |     off = _distinct_named(
36 |         analyze_report_stream(simulated, thread_scope_stability=False)
37 |     )
38 |     return WanderingOutcome(len(on), len(off), tuple(on), tuple(off))

web/app.js

   1 | "use strict";
   2 | 
   3 | // MicroCluster outbreak animation. Plain DOM, no framework, no build step.
   4 | // Detection and disclosure happen server-side; this file only draws what
   5 | // /api/run and /api/run/{id}/day/{n} return.
   6 | 
   7 | // render free tier edge returns intermittent 404/502 (x-render-routing:
   8 | // no-server) before the request reaches the app. retry those a few times
   9 | async function apiFetch(url, opts, tries = 4) {
  10 |   let lastErr;
  11 |   for (let attempt = 0; attempt < tries; attempt++) {
  12 |     if (attempt > 0) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300 * 2 ** (attempt - 1)));
  13 |     try {
  14 |       // a stalled connection should surface as a plain "timed out" error,
  15 |       // not hang the UI (and the "Running..." label) forever
  16 |       const res = await fetch(url, { ...opts, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(20000) });
  17 |       if (res.ok) return res;
  18 |       if (![404, 502, 503, 504].includes(res.status) || attempt === tries - 1) return res;
  19 |       lastErr = new Error(res.status + " " + (await res.clone().text()).slice(0, 120));
  20 |     } catch (err) {
  21 |       lastErr = err;
  22 |     }
  23 |   }
  24 |   throw lastErr;
  25 | }
  26 | 
  27 | const els = {
  28 |   seed: document.getElementById("seed"),
  29 |   days: document.getElementById("days"),
  30 |   run: document.getElementById("run"),
  31 |   runGuided: document.getElementById("run-guided"),

  34 |   summaryList: document.getElementById("summary-list"),
  35 |   play: document.getElementById("play"),
  36 |   pause: document.getElementById("pause"),
  37 |   step: document.getElementById("step"),

  70 | // captured once at load, before either button's label can be swapped to
  71 | // "Running..." while a fetch is in flight

  73 | const GUIDED_LABEL = els.runGuided.textContent;

  76 |   runId: null,

  80 |   timer: null,
  81 |   agentCells: new Map(), // key `${suite}#${i}` -> <div class="agent">
  82 |   suiteEls: new Map(),   // suite id -> <div class="suite">
  83 |   evals: [],           // current day's candidate scopes, coarsest -> finest
  84 |   wallIdx: -1,         // first index that FAILED the privacy gate, or -1
  85 |   maxResIdx: 0,        // furthest the slider may travel (the wall, inclusive)
  86 |   resIdx: 0,           // slider position

  90 | 
  91 | // SUITE is the finest scope level; used to compare disclosures across a run
  92 | // to find the most specific one ever made (never hardcoded against a tier list)
  93 | const LEVEL_RANK = { CAMPUS: 1, BUILDING: 2, FLOOR: 3, SUITE: 4 };
  94 | 
  95 | // scope ids nest by "-" prefix: "C-B1-F2" covers "C-B1-F2-S1".
  96 | function scopeCoversSuite(scopeId, suiteId) {
  97 |   return scopeId === suiteId || String(suiteId).startsWith(scopeId + "-");
  98 | }
  99 | 
 100 | function setPlaybackEnabled(on) {
 101 |   for (const b of [els.play, els.pause, els.step, els.reset]) b.disabled = !on;
 102 |   els.scrub.disabled = !on;
 103 | }

 105 | function stopTimer() {
 106 |   if (state.timer !== null) {
 107 |     clearInterval(state.timer);
 108 |     state.timer = null;
 109 |   }
