 114 | function renderSkeleton() {
 115 |   els.plan.classList.add("loading");
 116 |   els.plan.innerHTML = "";
 117 |   els.plan.setAttribute("aria-label", "Loading the floor plan.");
 118 |   for (let f = 0; f < 2; f++) {
 119 |     const floor = document.createElement("div");
 120 |     floor.className = "floor";
 121 |     const label = document.createElement("div");
 122 |     label.className = "floor-label";
 123 |     label.textContent = "loading";
 124 |     const suites = document.createElement("div");
 125 |     suites.className = "suites";
 126 |     for (let s = 0; s < 3; s++) {
 127 |       const sk = document.createElement("div");
 128 |       sk.className = "skeleton-suite";
 129 |       suites.appendChild(sk);
 130 |     }
 131 |     floor.append(label, suites);
 132 |     els.plan.appendChild(floor);
 133 |   }
 134 | }
 135 | 
 136 | function buildPlan(layout) {
 137 |   els.plan.classList.remove("loading");
 138 |   els.plan.innerHTML = "";
 139 |   els.plan.setAttribute("aria-label", "Floor plan. Everyone susceptible; the simulation has not run yet.");
 140 |   state.agentCells.clear();
 141 |   state.suiteEls.clear();
 142 | 
 143 |   for (const floor of layout.floors) {
 144 |     const floorEl = document.createElement("div");
 145 |     floorEl.className = "floor";
 146 | 
 147 |     const label = document.createElement("div");
 148 |     label.className = "floor-label";
 149 |     label.textContent = "Floor " + floor.label;
 150 | 
 151 |     const suitesEl = document.createElement("div");
 152 |     suitesEl.className = "suites";
 153 | 
 154 |     for (const suite of floor.suites) {
 155 |       const suiteEl = document.createElement("div");
 156 |       suiteEl.className = "suite";
 157 |       suiteEl.dataset.suite = suite.id;
 158 | 
 159 |       const sLabel = document.createElement("div");
 160 |       sLabel.className = "suite-label";
 161 |       sLabel.textContent = suite.label + "  n=" + suite.size;
 162 | 
 163 |       const agentsEl = document.createElement("div");
 164 |       agentsEl.className = "agents";
 165 |       for (let i = 0; i < suite.size; i++) {
 166 |         const cell = document.createElement("div");
 167 |         cell.className = "agent s-susceptible";
 168 |         agentsEl.appendChild(cell);
 169 |         state.agentCells.set(suite.id + "#" + i, cell);
 170 |       }
 171 | 
 172 |       suiteEl.append(sLabel, agentsEl);
 173 |       suitesEl.appendChild(suiteEl);
 174 |       state.suiteEls.set(suite.id, suiteEl);
 175 |     }
 176 | 
 177 |     floorEl.append(label, suitesEl);
 178 |     els.plan.appendChild(floorEl);
 179 |   }
 180 | }
 181 | 
 182 | // a one-shot ring, nothing else, on the instant a circle turns symptomatic.
 183 | // restart-safe (remove, force reflow, re-add) so a rapid re-trigger still plays.
 184 | function triggerPulse(cell) {
 185 |   cell.classList.remove("pulse");
 186 |   void cell.offsetWidth;
 187 |   cell.classList.add("pulse");
 188 |   setTimeout(() => cell.classList.remove("pulse"), 400);
 189 | }
 190 | 
 191 | function triggerStatusChange() {
 192 |   els.status.classList.remove("change-in");
 193 |   void els.status.offsetWidth;
 194 |   els.status.classList.add("change-in");
 195 |   setTimeout(() => els.status.classList.remove("change-in"), 250);
 196 | }
 197 | 
 198 | // `stepped` is true only for a genuine single forward step during real
 199 | // playback (advance(), driven by Step or the timer) -- never on initial
 200 | // load, a scrub jump, or ?auto=dayN. That is what keeps every screenshot
 201 | // (which always jumps straight to a day) free of the pulse / row-flash
 202 | // motion below: there is no previous rendered day to diff against.
 203 | function paintDay(n, opts = {}) {
 204 |   const frame = state.frames[n];
 205 |   if (!frame) return;
 206 |   const stepped = Boolean(opts.stepped) && n === state.currentDay + 1 && Boolean(state.frames[n - 1]);
 207 |   const prevFrame = stepped ? state.frames[n - 1] : null;
 208 |   state.currentDay = n;
 209 | 
 210 |   const prevAgentState = new Map();
 211 |   if (prevFrame) {
 212 |     for (const a of prevFrame.agents) prevAgentState.set(a.suite + "#" + a.i, a.state);
 213 |   }
 214 |   // diffed, not reset-then-set: every agent is present in every frame, so
 215 |   // only cells whose state actually changed get a new class -- a colour
 216 |   // transition on every cell every day would read as the grid flashing
 217 |   for (const a of frame.agents) {
 218 |     const key = a.suite + "#" + a.i;
 219 |     const cell = state.agentCells.get(key);
 220 |     if (!cell) continue;
 221 |     const cls = "agent s-" + a.state;
 222 |     if (cell.className !== cls) cell.className = cls;
 223 |     if (prevFrame && a.state === "symptomatic" && prevAgentState.get(key) !== "symptomatic") {
 224 |       triggerPulse(cell);
 225 |     }
 226 |   }
 227 | 
 228 |   renderResolution(frame);
 229 |   renderGateTable(frame, prevFrame);
 230 | 
 231 |   const d = frame.disclosure;
 232 |   const resScope = state.evals[state.resIdx];
 233 |   for (const [suiteId, suiteEl] of state.suiteEls) {
 234 |     suiteEl.classList.toggle(
 235 |       "disclosed", Boolean(d && scopeCoversSuite(d.scope_id, suiteId))
 236 |     );
 237 |     suiteEl.classList.toggle(
 238 |       "resolution", Boolean(resScope && scopeCoversSuite(resScope.scope_id, suiteId))
 239 |     );
 240 |   }
 241 | 
 242 |   const c = frame.counts;
 243 |   els.counts.textContent =
 244 |     `susceptible ${c.susceptible} · incubating ${c.incubating} · ` +
 245 |     `symptomatic ${c.symptomatic} · recovered ${c.recovered}`;
 246 |   // text alternative for the role=img plan -- the numbers, not the pixels
 247 |   els.plan.setAttribute(
 248 |     "aria-label",
 249 |     `Floor plan, day ${n}. ${c.susceptible} susceptible, ${c.incubating} incubating, ` +
 250 |     `${c.symptomatic} symptomatic, ${c.recovered} recovered` +
 251 |     (d ? `. Disclosed scope: ${d.level} ${d.label}.` : ".")
 252 |   );
 253 | 
 254 |   els.dayLabel.textContent = String(n);
 255 |   els.scrub.value = String(n);
 256 | 
 257 |   // undefined only before the very first paint -- a fresh load (including
 258 |   // every ?auto=dayN screenshot) never sees this as a "change"
 259 |   const newScopeId = d ? d.scope_id : null;
 260 |   const scopeChanged =
 261 |     state.lastDisclosedScopeId !== undefined && state.lastDisclosedScopeId !== newScopeId;
 262 |   state.lastDisclosedScopeId = newScopeId;
 263 | 
 264 |   if (d) {
 265 |     els.status.textContent =
 266 |       `System says: ${d.level} ${d.label} disclosed` +
 267 |       (frame.first_disclosed_day !== null ? ` (first on day ${frame.first_disclosed_day})` : "");
 268 |     els.status.classList.add("flagged");
 269 |   } else if (frame.detection.fired) {
 270 |     els.status.textContent =
 271 |       "System says: activity detected, nothing disclosed (no scope passes both gates)";
 272 |     els.status.classList.remove("flagged");
 273 |   } else {
 274 |     els.status.textContent = "System says: nothing";
 275 |     els.status.classList.remove("flagged");
 276 |   }
 277 |   if (scopeChanged) triggerStatusChange();
 278 | 
 279 |   renderRefusal(frame);
 280 | }
 281 | 
 282 | // the money moment: a scope with enough evidence that the privacy gate
 283 | // refuses. wording comes from the engine's own reason text, not invented here
 284 | function renderRefusal(frame) {
 285 |   const evals = frame.evaluations || [];
 286 |   const refused = evals.find((e) => e.statistical_pass && !e.privacy_pass);
 287 |   const d = frame.disclosure;
 288 | 
 289 |   // :empty hides the box; writing into it is the change role=status announces
 290 |   function show(title, text) {
 291 |     const head = document.createElement("strong");
 292 |     head.textContent = title;
 293 |     const body = document.createElement("span");
 294 |     body.textContent = text;
 295 |     els.refusal.replaceChildren(head, body);
 296 |   }
 297 | 
 298 |   if (refused) {
 299 |     const named = d ? `${d.level} ${d.label}` : "no scope at all";
 300 |     // strip the engine's "Rejected: " prefix n trailing punctuation, frame it here
 301 |     const reason = refused.reason.replace(/^Rejected:\s*/, "").replace(/[.\s]+$/, "");
 302 |     show(
 303 |       "Privacy refusal",
 304 |       `The system has enough evidence to name ${refused.level} ${refused.label}, ` +
 305 |       `but will not: ${reason}. It disclosed ${named} instead.`
 306 |     );
 307 |     return;
 308 |   }
 309 | 
 310 |   if (frame.detection.fired && !d) {
 311 |     show(
 312 |       "Nothing disclosed",
 313 |       "Activity was detected, but no scope has both enough evidence and a safe " +
 314 |       "population to name, so the system says nothing."
 315 |     );
 316 |     return;
 317 |   }
 318 | 
 319 |   els.refusal.replaceChildren();
 320 | }
 321 | 
 322 | // ---- resolution slider -------------------------------------------------
 323 | 
 324 | // The slider walks the candidate scopes from coarsest to finest. It may
 325 | // travel up to and including the first scope that FAILED the privacy gate
 326 | // (state.wallIdx); dragging further snaps back to it. The engine already
 327 | // decided pass/fail -- this only reads frame.evaluations.
 328 | function renderResolution(frame) {
 329 |   const evals = frame.evaluations || [];
 330 |   state.evals = evals;
 331 | 
 332 |   if (evals.length === 0) {
 333 |     state.wallIdx = -1;
 334 |     state.maxResIdx = 0;
 335 |     state.resIdx = 0;
 336 |     els.resSlider.disabled = true;
 337 |     els.resSlider.min = "0";
 338 |     els.resSlider.max = "0";
 339 |     els.resSlider.value = "0";
 340 |     els.resReadout.className = "res-readout";
 341 |     els.resReadout.textContent = frame.detection.fired
 342 |       ? "Detection fired but no scope had a qualifying report this day."
 343 |       : "No candidate scopes. Detection has not fired on this day.";
 344 |     return;
 345 |   }
 346 | 
 347 |   // label the slider ends with the actual scope levels this day offers,
 348 |   // coarsest -> finest (the server already orders evaluations that way),
 349 |   // instead of the generic "coarsest"/"finest" placeholder shown pre-run
 350 |   els.resEndCoarse.textContent = evals[0].level.toLowerCase();
 351 |   els.resEndFine.textContent = evals[evals.length - 1].level.toLowerCase();
 352 | 
 353 |   state.wallIdx = evals.findIndex((e) => !e.privacy_pass);
 354 |   state.maxResIdx = state.wallIdx === -1 ? evals.length - 1 : state.wallIdx;
 355 |   const selIdx = evals.findIndex((e) => e.selected);
 356 |   state.resIdx = selIdx !== -1 ? selIdx : state.maxResIdx;
 357 | 
 358 |   els.resSlider.disabled = false;
 359 |   els.resSlider.min = "0";
 360 |   els.resSlider.max = String(evals.length - 1);
 361 |   els.resSlider.value = String(state.resIdx);
 362 |   renderResReadout();
 363 | }
 364 | 
 365 | function renderResReadout() {
 366 |   const e = state.evals[state.resIdx];
 367 |   if (!e) return;
 368 |   const atWall = state.wallIdx !== -1 && state.resIdx === state.wallIdx;
 369 |   els.resReadout.classList.toggle("wall", atWall);
 370 | 
 371 |   if (atWall) {
 372 |     els.resReadout.textContent =
 373 |       `Resolution stops at ${e.level} ${e.label}. ${e.reason}`;
 374 |   } else if (e.selected) {
 375 |     els.resReadout.textContent =
 376 |       `Resolution: ${e.level} ${e.label}. The scope the system disclosed.`;
 377 |   } else if (e.eligible) {
 378 |     els.resReadout.textContent =
 379 |       `Resolution: ${e.level} ${e.label}. Passes both gates; a finer scope was disclosed.`;
 380 |   } else {
 381 |     els.resReadout.textContent =
 382 |       `Resolution: ${e.level} ${e.label}. Passes the privacy gate. ${e.reason}`;
 383 |   }
 384 | }
 385 | 
 386 | function applyResolutionHighlight() {
 387 |   const resScope = state.evals[state.resIdx];
 388 |   for (const [suiteId, suiteEl] of state.suiteEls) {
 389 |     suiteEl.classList.toggle(
 390 |       "resolution", Boolean(resScope && scopeCoversSuite(resScope.scope_id, suiteId))
 391 |     );
 392 |   }
 393 | }
 394 | 
 395 | els.resSlider.addEventListener("input", () => {
 396 |   let v = Number(els.resSlider.value);
 397 |   if (v > state.maxResIdx) {
 398 |     v = state.maxResIdx;                 // hard stop at the privacy wall
 399 |     els.resSlider.value = String(v);
 400 |   }
 401 |   state.resIdx = v;
 402 |   renderResReadout();
 403 |   applyResolutionHighlight();
 404 | });
 405 | 
 406 | // ---- gate table ------------------------------------------------------
 407 | 
 408 | function td(text) {
 409 |   const el = document.createElement("td");
 410 |   el.textContent = text;
 411 |   return el;
 412 | }
 413 | 
 414 | function gateCell(pass, sub) {
 415 |   const el = document.createElement("td");
 416 |   el.className = "gate-cell";
 417 |   const verdict = document.createElement("span");
 418 |   verdict.className = pass ? "pass" : "fail";
 419 |   verdict.textContent = pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL";
 420 |   el.appendChild(verdict);
 421 |   if (sub) {
 422 |     const note = document.createElement("small");
 423 |     note.textContent = sub;
 424 |     el.appendChild(note);
 425 |   }
 426 |   return el;
 427 | }
 428 | 
 429 | function _verdictSignature(e) {
 430 |   return `${e.statistical_pass}|${e.privacy_pass}|${e.selected}`;
 431 | }
 432 | 
 433 | // prevFrame is only passed for a genuine single forward step (see paintDay);
 434 | // rows are always rebuilt fresh either way, so a jump/scrub/screenshot load
 435 | // never sees the flash (there is nothing to diff against, prevFrame is null)
 436 | function renderGateTable(frame, prevFrame) {
 437 |   els.gateDay.textContent = String(frame.day);
 438 |   const body = els.gateBody;
 439 |   body.innerHTML = "";
 440 |   const evals = frame.evaluations || [];
 441 | 
 442 |   if (evals.length === 0) {
 443 |     const tr = document.createElement("tr");
 444 |     const cell = document.createElement("td");
 445 |     cell.colSpan = 6;
 446 |     cell.className = "muted";
 447 |     cell.textContent =
 448 |       "No candidate scopes evaluated" +
 449 |       (frame.detection.fired ? "." : " (detection did not fire).");
 450 |     tr.appendChild(cell);
 451 |     body.appendChild(tr);
 452 |     return;
 453 |   }
 454 | 
 455 |   const prevVerdict = new Map();
 456 |   if (prevFrame) {
 457 |     for (const e of prevFrame.evaluations || []) prevVerdict.set(e.scope_id, _verdictSignature(e));
 458 |   }
 459 | 
 460 |   const flashRows = [];
 461 |   for (const e of evals) {
 462 |     const tr = document.createElement("tr");
 463 |     if (e.selected) tr.className = "selected";
 464 |     tr.appendChild(td(`${e.level} ${e.label}`));
 465 |     tr.appendChild(td(String(e.population)));
 466 |     tr.appendChild(td(e.qualifying_band));
 467 |     tr.appendChild(gateCell(e.statistical_pass, `needs at least ${e.statistical_threshold}`));
 468 |     tr.appendChild(gateCell(e.privacy_pass, ""));
 469 |     tr.appendChild(td(e.reason));
 470 |     body.appendChild(tr);
 471 | 
 472 |     if (prevFrame) {
 473 |       const before = prevVerdict.get(e.scope_id);
 474 |       if (before !== undefined && before !== _verdictSignature(e)) {
 475 |         tr.classList.add("verdict-changed");
 476 |         flashRows.push(tr);
 477 |       }
 478 |     }
 479 |   }
 480 |   // double rAF: let the browser paint the flash once, THEN remove the class,
 481 |   // so the removal (not the arrival) is what transitions -- a fade out, not a snap
 482 |   if (flashRows.length) {
 483 |     requestAnimationFrame(() => {
 484 |       requestAnimationFrame(() => {
 485 |         for (const tr of flashRows) tr.classList.remove("verdict-changed");
 486 |       });
 487 |     });
 488 |   }
 489 | }
 490 | 
 491 | // ---- end-of-run summary -------------------------------------------------
 492 | 
 493 | // Built while every day's frame is fetched in startRun's prefetch loop, not
 494 | // recomputed from a single day's frame -- "most specific disclosure" and
 495 | // "was a finer scope ever refused" are properties of the whole run.
 496 | function noteSummaryFrame(summary, n, frame) {
 497 |   if (frame.detection.fired && summary.firedDay === null) {
 498 |     summary.fired = true;
 499 |     summary.firedDay = n;
 500 |   }
 501 |   const d = frame.disclosure;
 502 |   if (d) {
 503 |     const rank = LEVEL_RANK[d.level] || 0;
 504 |     if (!summary.finest || rank > LEVEL_RANK[summary.finest.level]) {
 505 |       summary.finest = d;
 506 |       summary.finestDay = n;
 507 |     }
 508 |   }
 509 |   if (!summary.refused) {
 510 |     const r = (frame.evaluations || []).find((e) => e.statistical_pass && !e.privacy_pass);
 511 |     if (r) {
 512 |       summary.refused = true;
 513 |       summary.refusedDay = n;
 514 |     }
 515 |   }
 516 | }
 517 | 
 518 | function renderRunSummary() {
 519 |   const s = state.summary;
 520 |   if (!s) return;
 521 |   els.sumDetected.textContent = s.fired ? "Yes" : "No";
 522 |   els.sumDetectedDay.textContent = s.fired ? String(s.firedDay) : "n/a";
 523 |   els.sumDisclosure.textContent = s.finest
 524 |     ? `${s.finest.level} ${s.finest.label} (day ${s.finestDay})`
 525 |     : "Nothing disclosed";
 526 |   els.sumRefused.textContent = s.refused ? "Yes" : "No";
 527 |   els.sumRefusedDay.textContent = s.refused ? String(s.refusedDay) : "n/a";
 528 |   els.runSummary.hidden = false;
 529 | }
 530 | 
 531 | // ---- run + playback -----------------------------------------------------
 532 | 
 533 | // Both run buttons are disabled and the clicked one relabelled for the
 534 | // couple of seconds the fetch takes, so the page never just sits there
 535 | // looking inert. Always restored together, win or lose.
 536 | function setRunningFeedback(triggerButton) {
 537 |   els.run.disabled = true;
 538 |   els.runGuided.disabled = true;
 539 |   if (triggerButton) triggerButton.textContent = "Running…";
 540 | }
 541 | function clearRunningFeedback() {
 542 |   els.run.disabled = false;
 543 |   els.runGuided.disabled = false;
 544 |   els.run.textContent = RUN_LABEL;
 545 |   els.runGuided.textContent = GUIDED_LABEL;
 546 | }
 547 | 
 548 | // opts.triggerButton: the button clicked, so its label can say "Running...".
 549 | // opts.autoplay (default true): start playback once frames land, same as
 550 | // the page-load autoplay -- the ?auto= smoke-test path below passes false
 551 | // because it drives its own jump-to-day / play().
 552 | async function startRun(opts = {}) {
 553 |   const autoplay = opts.autoplay !== false;
 554 |   stopTimer();
 555 |   setPlaybackEnabled(false);
 556 |   setRunningFeedback(opts.triggerButton);
 557 |   els.emptyNote && (els.emptyNote.style.display = "none");
 558 |   renderSkeleton();
 559 |   els.status.textContent = "System says: starting the simulation…";
 560 |   els.status.classList.remove("flagged");
 561 |   els.refusal.replaceChildren();
 562 |   els.resSlider.disabled = true;
 563 |   els.resReadout.className = "res-readout";
 564 |   els.resReadout.textContent = "Loading";
 565 |   els.gateBody.innerHTML = "";
 566 |   els.gateDay.textContent = "0";
 567 |   els.runSummary.hidden = true;
 568 |   state.summary = { fired: false, firedDay: null, finest: null, finestDay: null, refused: false, refusedDay: null };
 569 |   // reset the scrubber immediately, not just once frames arrive -- otherwise
 570 |   // a disabled slider sits at the previous run's end position while this
 571 |   // fetch is still in flight
 572 |   els.scrub.value = "0";
 573 |   els.dayLabel.textContent = "0";
 574 |   els.dayMax.textContent = "0";
 575 | 
 576 |   const reporting = Number(els.pReporting.value);
 577 |   const body = {
 578 |     seed: Number(els.seed.value) || 0,
 579 |     days: Math.max(1, Math.min(120, Number(els.days.value) || 30)),
 580 |     population: Number(els.pPopulation.value) || 25,
 581 |     suite_transmission_probability: Number(els.pTransmission.value),
 582 |     reporting_probability_min: reporting,
 583 |     reporting_probability_max: reporting,
 584 |     detection_window_hours: Number(els.pWindow.value) || 72,
 585 |   };
 586 |   const qs = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
 587 |   for (const key of [
 588 |     "suite_transmission_probability", "floor_transmission_probability",
 589 |     "building_transmission_probability", "reporting_probability_min",
 590 |     "reporting_probability_max", "background_noise_daily_rate", "seed_infections",
 591 |     "population", "detection_window_hours",
 592 |   ]) {
 593 |     if (qs.has(key)) body[key] = Number(qs.get(key));
 594 |   }
 595 |   if (qs.has("seed")) { body.seed = Number(qs.get("seed")); els.seed.value = body.seed; }
 596 |   if (qs.has("days")) { body.days = Number(qs.get("days")); els.days.value = body.days; }
 597 |   if (qs.has("population")) els.pPopulation.value = body.population;
 598 |   if (qs.has("suite_transmission_probability")) els.pTransmission.value = body.suite_transmission_probability;
 599 |   if (qs.has("reporting_probability_min")) els.pReporting.value = body.reporting_probability_min;
 600 |   if (qs.has("detection_window_hours")) els.pWindow.value = body.detection_window_hours;
 601 |   refreshParamHints();
 602 |   els.paramsDirty.hidden = true;
 603 |   els.status.textContent = `System says: simulating ${body.days} days…`;
 604 | 
 605 |   let meta;
 606 |   try {
 607 |     const res = await apiFetch("/api/run", {
 608 |       method: "POST",
 609 |       headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
 610 |       body: JSON.stringify(body),
 611 |     });
 612 |     if (!res.ok) throw new Error("run failed: " + res.status + " " + (await res.text()));
 613 |     meta = await res.json();
 614 |   } catch (err) {
 615 |     els.plan.classList.remove("loading");
 616 |     els.plan.innerHTML = "";
 617 |     els.status.textContent = "System says: the simulation could not be started (" + err.message + "). Try again.";
 618 |     clearRunningFeedback();
 619 |     return;
 620 |   }
 621 | 
 622 |   state.runId = meta.run_id;
 623 |   state.totalDays = meta.days;
 624 |   state.frames = new Array(meta.days + 1).fill(null);
 625 |   if (meta.disclaimer) els.disclaimer.textContent = meta.disclaimer;
 626 |   els.dayMax.textContent = String(meta.days);
 627 |   els.scrub.max = String(meta.days);
 628 | 
 629 |   buildPlan(meta.layout);
 630 | 
 631 |   // Prefetch every day up front so playback is smooth.
 632 |   for (let n = 0; n <= meta.days; n++) {
 633 |     let res;
 634 |     try {
 635 |       res = await apiFetch(`/api/run/${state.runId}/day/${n}`);
 636 |     } catch (err) {
 637 |       els.status.textContent = "System says: the simulation stopped partway through (day " + n + ": " + err.message + "). Try again.";
 638 |       clearRunningFeedback();
 639 |       return;
 640 |     }
 641 |     if (!res.ok) {
 642 |       els.status.textContent = "System says: the simulation stopped partway through (day " + n + ": HTTP " + res.status + "). Try again.";
 643 |       clearRunningFeedback();
 644 |       return;
 645 |     }
 646 |     state.frames[n] = await res.json();
 647 |     noteSummaryFrame(state.summary, n, state.frames[n]);
 648 |     if (n === 0) paintDay(0);
 649 |   }
 650 | 
 651 |   setPlaybackEnabled(true);
 652 |   clearRunningFeedback();
 653 |   paintDay(0);
 654 |   renderRunSummary();
 655 |   if (autoplay) play();
 656 | }
 657 | 
 658 | function advance() {
 659 |   if (state.currentDay >= state.totalDays) {
 660 |     stopTimer();
 661 |     return;
 662 |   }
 663 |   paintDay(state.currentDay + 1, { stepped: true });
 664 | }
 665 | 
 666 | function play() {
 667 |   if (state.timer !== null) return;
 668 |   if (state.currentDay >= state.totalDays) paintDay(0);
 669 |   const interval = Number(els.speed.value) || 500;
 670 |   state.timer = setInterval(advance, interval);
 671 | }
 672 | 
 673 | // guided example: the exact scenario from docs/demo_video_script.md.
 674 | // Shared by the button and the autoplay-on-load below.
 675 | function runGuidedScenario() {
 676 |   els.seed.value = "4";
 677 |   els.days.value = "30";
 678 |   els.pPopulation.value = "25";
 679 |   els.pTransmission.value = "0.025";
 680 |   els.pReporting.value = "0.8";
 681 |   els.pWindow.value = "72";
 682 |   refreshParamHints();
 683 |   return startRun({ triggerButton: els.runGuided });
 684 | }
 685 | 
 686 | // once the user has touched Run, the guided button, or any Playback
 687 | // control, the scheduled autoplay (below) must not start a run on top of
 688 | // whatever they are doing
 689 | let userActed = false;
 690 | function noteUserAction() { userActed = true; }
 691 | for (const el of [els.run, els.runGuided, els.play, els.pause, els.step, els.reset]) {
 692 |   el.addEventListener("click", noteUserAction);
 693 | }
 694 | els.scrub.addEventListener("input", noteUserAction);
 695 | 
 696 | els.run.addEventListener("click", () => startRun({ triggerButton: els.run }));
 697 | els.runGuided.addEventListener("click", () => runGuidedScenario());
 698 | els.play.addEventListener("click", play);
 699 | els.pause.addEventListener("click", stopTimer);
 700 | els.step.addEventListener("click", () => { stopTimer(); advance(); });
 701 | els.reset.addEventListener("click", () => { stopTimer(); paintDay(0); });
 702 | els.scrub.addEventListener("input", () => { stopTimer(); paintDay(Number(els.scrub.value)); });
 703 | els.speed.addEventListener("change", () => {
 704 |   if (state.timer !== null) { stopTimer(); play(); }
 705 | });
 706 | 
 707 | // ---- model parameter controls ---------------------------------------
 708 | 
 709 | function refreshParamHints() {
 710 |   const perSuite = Number(els.pPopulation.value) || 25;
 711 |   els.pPopulationHint.textContent = `6 suites, ${perSuite * 6} people`;
 712 |   els.pTransmissionHint.textContent = Number(els.pTransmission.value).toFixed(3);
 713 |   els.pReportingHint.textContent = Number(els.pReporting.value).toFixed(2);
 714 | }
 715 | 
 716 | for (const el of [
 717 |   els.pPopulation, els.pTransmission, els.pReporting, els.pWindow, els.seed, els.days,
 718 | ]) {
 719 |   el.addEventListener("input", () => {
 720 |     refreshParamHints();
 721 |     if (state.runId) els.paramsDirty.hidden = false;
 722 |   });
 723 | }
 724 | refreshParamHints();
 725 | 
 726 | // ts keeps the pre-run building drawing in sync w suite size
 727 | let preloadTimer = null;
 728 | els.pPopulation.addEventListener("input", () => {
 729 |   if (state.runId) return;
 730 |   clearTimeout(preloadTimer);
 731 |   preloadTimer = setTimeout(preloadLayout, 250);
 732 | });
 733 | 
 734 | // ---- pre-run empty state: draw the building before anything happens ----
 735 | 
 736 | function layoutQuery() {
 737 |   const qs = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
 738 |   const pop = qs.get("population") || els.pPopulation.value;
 739 |   return pop ? `?population=${encodeURIComponent(pop)}` : "";
 740 | }
 741 | 
 742 | async function preloadLayout() {
 743 |   if (state.runId) return false;
 744 |   try {
 745 |     const res = await apiFetch("/api/layout" + layoutQuery());
 746 |     if (!res.ok) return false;
 747 |     const data = await res.json();
 748 |     if (state.runId) return false;            // a real run started meanwhile
 749 |     buildPlan(data.layout);
 750 |     if (data.params && data.params.total_population) {
 751 |       els.planPop.textContent = String(data.params.total_population);
 752 |     }
 753 |     if (data.disclaimer) els.disclaimer.textContent = data.disclaimer;
 754 |     return true;
 755 |   } catch (_) {
 756 |     const note = document.getElementById("empty-note");
 757 |     if (note) note.textContent = "Press Run to draw the building and start an outbreak.";
 758 |     return false;
 759 |   }
 760 | }
 761 | 
 762 | // ---- precomputed results panel ---------------------------------------
 763 | 
 764 | const SVG_NS = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
 765 | 
 766 | function svg(name, attrs, text) {
 767 |   const el = document.createElementNS(SVG_NS, name);
 768 |   for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(attrs || {})) el.setAttribute(k, String(v));
 769 |   if (text != null) el.textContent = text;
 770 |   return el;
 771 | }
 772 | 
 773 | function renderCostChart(data) {
 774 |   const host = document.getElementById("cost-chart");
 775 |   host.innerHTML = "";
 776 |   const rows = (data.sweeps[data.primary_sweep].rows || []).filter(
 777 |     (r) => !r.degenerate && r.disc_delay != null && r.inf_before_disclosure_unbiased != null
 778 |   );
 779 |   if (rows.length < 2) {
 780 |     host.textContent = "not enough sweep points to plot.";
 781 |     return;
 782 |   }
 783 |   rows.sort((a, b) => a.disc_delay - b.disc_delay);
 784 | 
 785 |   const W = 760, H = 430, L = 66, Rm = 46, T = 22, Bm = 52;
 786 |   const xs = rows.map((r) => r.disc_delay);
 787 |   const ys = rows.map((r) => r.inf_before_disclosure_unbiased);
 788 |   const xMin = Math.floor(Math.min(...xs)) - 0.5;
 789 |   const xMax = Math.ceil(Math.max(...xs)) + 0.5;
 790 |   const yMax = Math.max(15, Math.ceil(Math.max(...ys) / 15) * 15);
 791 |   const px = (x) => L + ((x - xMin) / (xMax - xMin)) * (W - L - Rm);
 792 |   const py = (y) => H - Bm - (y / yMax) * (H - T - Bm);
 793 | 
 794 |   const root = svg("svg", { viewBox: `0 0 ${W} ${H}`, preserveAspectRatio: "xMidYMid meet",
 795 |     role: "img", "aria-label": "cost of the privacy policy" });
 796 | 
 797 |   root.appendChild(svg("line", { class: "axis", x1: L, y1: H - Bm, x2: W - Rm, y2: H - Bm }));
 798 |   root.appendChild(svg("line", { class: "axis", x1: L, y1: T, x2: L, y2: H - Bm }));
 799 | 
 800 |   for (let x = Math.ceil(xMin); x <= Math.floor(xMax); x += 2) {
 801 |     root.appendChild(svg("line", { class: "axis", x1: px(x), y1: H - Bm, x2: px(x), y2: H - Bm + 5 }));
 802 |     root.appendChild(svg("text", { class: "tick", x: px(x), y: H - Bm + 18, "text-anchor": "middle" }, String(x)));
 803 |   }
 804 |   for (let i = 0; i <= 3; i++) {
 805 |     const y = (yMax / 3) * i;
 806 |     root.appendChild(svg("line", { class: "axis", x1: L - 5, y1: py(y), x2: L, y2: py(y) }));
 807 |     root.appendChild(svg("text", { class: "tick", x: L - 9, y: py(y) + 4, "text-anchor": "end" }, String(Math.round(y))));
 808 |   }
 809 | 
 810 |   const d = rows.map((r, i) => `${i ? "L" : "M"}${px(r.disc_delay).toFixed(1)},${py(r.inf_before_disclosure_unbiased).toFixed(1)}`).join(" ");
 811 |   root.appendChild(svg("path", { class: "series", d }));
 812 | 
 813 |   rows.forEach((r, i) => {
 814 |     const cx = px(r.disc_delay), cy = py(r.inf_before_disclosure_unbiased);
 815 |     root.appendChild(svg("circle", { class: "point", cx, cy, r: 4 }));
 816 |     const first = i === 0;
 817 |     root.appendChild(svg("text", {
 818 |       class: "point-label",
 819 |       x: cx + (first ? 8 : -8),
 820 |       y: cy - 8,
 821 |       "text-anchor": first ? "start" : "end",
 822 |     }, String(r.value)));
 823 |   });
 824 | 
 825 |   root.appendChild(svg("text", { x: (L + W - Rm) / 2, y: H - 10, "text-anchor": "middle" },
 826 |     "mean days to first disclosure"));
 827 |   root.appendChild(svg("text", {
 828 |     x: 16, y: (T + H - Bm) / 2, "text-anchor": "middle",
 829 |     transform: `rotate(-90 16 ${(T + H - Bm) / 2})`,
 830 |   }, "mean infections before disclosure"));
 831 | 
 832 |   host.appendChild(root);
 833 |   const caption = document.createElement("p");
 834 |   caption.className = "results-note";
 835 |   caption.textContent =
 836 |     `point labels are the ${data.primary_sweep} value; the shipped default is ${rows[0].value}. Independent reporting.`;
 837 |   host.appendChild(caption);
 838 | }
 839 | 
 840 | function _cell(text, cls) {
 841 |   const td = document.createElement("td");
 842 |   td.textContent = text;
 843 |   if (cls) td.className = cls;
 844 |   return td;
 845 | }
 846 | 
 847 | function _fmtScore(v) {
 848 |   return v == null ? "n/a" : v.toFixed(2);
 849 | }
 850 | 
 851 | // precision/recall are fractions of 1 -- also show the percentage so the
 852 | // number reads without translating it in your head
 853 | function _fmtFraction(v) {
 854 |   return v == null ? "n/a" : `${v.toFixed(2)} (${Math.round(v * 100)}%)`;
 855 | }
 856 | 
 857 | function _detectorRows(scores, cols) {
 858 |   // scores: {rules, model}; cols: extra leading cell text per row or null
 859 |   const rules = document.createElement("tr");
 860 |   const model = document.createElement("tr");
 861 |   model.className = "model";
 862 |   if (cols) {
 863 |     const lead = _cell(cols);
 864 |     lead.rowSpan = 2;
 865 |     rules.appendChild(lead);
 866 |   }
 867 |   rules.append(
 868 |     _cell("authored rules"),
 869 |     _cell(_fmtFraction(scores.rules.precision)),
 870 |     _cell(_fmtFraction(scores.rules.recall)),
 871 |     _cell(_fmtScore(scores.rules.delay))
 872 |   );
 873 |   model.append(
 874 |     _cell("learned model"),
 875 |     _cell(_fmtFraction(scores.model.precision)),
 876 |     _cell(_fmtFraction(scores.model.recall)),
 877 |     _cell(_fmtScore(scores.model.delay))
 878 |   );
 879 |   return [rules, model];
 880 | }
 881 | 
 882 | function renderBenchmark(data) {
 883 |   const body = document.getElementById("benchmark-body");
 884 |   body.innerHTML = "";
 885 |   body.append(..._detectorRows(data.in_distribution, null));
 886 |   document.getElementById("benchmark-conclusion").textContent = data.conclusion;
 887 | }
 888 | 
 889 | function renderRobustness(data) {
 890 |   const body = document.getElementById("robustness-body");
 891 |   body.innerHTML = "";
 892 |   for (const regime of data.cross_regime) {
 893 |     body.append(..._detectorRows(regime, regime.label));
 894 |   }
 895 |   document.getElementById("robustness-conclusion").textContent = data.cross_regime_conclusion;
 896 | }
 897 | 
 898 | function renderAdversarial(data) {
 899 |   document.getElementById("adv-exact").textContent = String(data.exact_one_person_pins);
 900 |   document.getElementById("adv-band").textContent = String(data.band_one_person_pins);
 901 |   document.getElementById("adv-residual").textContent =
 902 |     `Bands remove the one-person pin, not every inference. What the observer still gets: ` +
 903 |     `${data.residual}. The direction of the overnight change is still forced on ` +
 904 |     `${data.band_direction_known} of ${data.same_scope_pairs.toLocaleString()} day-pairs, ` +
 905 |     `and with scope stability off a run names about ${data.wandering_off_mean} distinct ` +
 906 |     `groups instead of ${data.wandering_on_mean}.`;
 907 | }
 908 | 
 909 | // headline numbers, straight from results/*.json, nothing hardcoded
 910 | function renderSummary(sweep, bench, adv) {
 911 |   const rows = (sweep.sweeps[sweep.primary_sweep].rows || []).filter(
 912 |     (r) => !r.degenerate && r.disc_delay != null && r.fire_delay != null
 913 |   );
 914 |   const shipped = rows[0];
 915 |   const items = [];
 916 | 
 917 |   if (shipped) {
 918 |     const gap = shipped.disc_delay - shipped.fire_delay;
 919 |     items.push(
 920 |       `Averaged over ${sweep.seeds.toLocaleString()} simulated outbreaks at the default ` +
 921 |       `transmission rate, with independent reporting, the detector fires around day ` +
 922 |       `${shipped.fire_delay.toFixed(0)} ` +
 923 |       `but the privacy rules hold disclosure until about day ${shipped.disc_delay.toFixed(0)}. ` +
 924 |       `That gap of roughly ${gap.toFixed(0)} days, during which about ` +
 925 |       `${Math.round(shipped.inf_before_disclosure_unbiased)} people are infected, ` +
 926 |       `is the measurable cost of the policy.`
 927 |     );
 928 |   }
 929 | 
 930 |   const r = bench.in_distribution.rules, m = bench.in_distribution.model;
 931 |   items.push(
 932 |     `On held-out runs in the training regime, with independent reporting, the authored ` +
 933 |     `rules score precision ` +
 934 |     `${r.precision.toFixed(2)}, recall ${r.recall.toFixed(2)}. A learned model is about ` +
 935 |     `${(r.delay - m.delay).toFixed(1)} day faster but less precise (${m.precision.toFixed(2)}), ` +
 936 |     `so the rules ship and the model stays a benchmark.`
 937 |   );
 938 | 
 939 |   items.push(
 940 |     `In simulated runs with independent reporting, banding the report counts stops an ` +
 941 |     `observer pinning an exact one-person overnight ` +
 942 |     `change: ${adv.exact_one_person_pins} days against exact counts, ` +
 943 |     `${adv.band_one_person_pins} against the bands. On about ` +
 944 |     `${adv.band_direction_known} of ${adv.same_scope_pairs.toLocaleString()} same-scope ` +
 945 |     `day-pairs the bands still reveal which way the count moved, though not by how much.`
 946 |   );
 947 | 
 948 |   els.summaryList.innerHTML = "";
 949 |   for (const text of items) {
 950 |     const li = document.createElement("li");
 951 |     li.textContent = text;
 952 |     els.summaryList.appendChild(li);
 953 |   }
 954 | }
 955 | 
 956 | async function loadResults() {
 957 |   try {
 958 |     const [sweep, bench, adv] = await Promise.all([
 959 |       apiFetch("/api/results/disclosure_sweep").then((r) => r.json()),
 960 |       apiFetch("/api/results/benchmark").then((r) => r.json()),
 961 |       apiFetch("/api/results/adversarial").then((r) => r.json()),
 962 |     ]);
 963 |     renderSummary(sweep, bench, adv);
 964 |     renderCostChart(sweep);
 965 |     renderBenchmark(bench);
 966 |     renderRobustness(bench);
 967 |     renderAdversarial(adv);
 968 |     return true;
 969 |   } catch (err) {
 970 |     document.getElementById("cost-chart").textContent =
 971 |       "precomputed results unavailable: run python -m scripts.precompute_results";
 972 |     els.summaryList.innerHTML = "<li class=\"muted\">Findings unavailable.</li>";
 973 |     return false;
 974 |   }
 975 | }
 976 | 
 977 | // Autoplay the guided example once the layout and the precomputed results
 978 | // have both loaded, so a visitor sees motion with no click. Does nothing if:
 979 | //   - ?auto=... is present (that query param already drives its own
 980 | //     playback below -- used for every gallery/verification screenshot),
 981 | //   - ?autoplay=0 is present (escape hatch to capture the plain pre-run
 982 | //     state deliberately),
 983 | //   - loading is slow (over AUTOPLAY_TIMEOUT_MS) or either fetch failed --
 984 | //     falls back to the static pre-run state rather than a broken run,
 985 | //   - the visitor has already clicked Run, the guided button, or anything
 986 | //     in the Playback panel first.
 987 | const AUTOPLAY_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000;
 988 | async function autoplayOnLoad(layoutReady, resultsReady) {
 989 |   const qs = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
 990 |   if (qs.get("auto") !== null || qs.get("autoplay") === "0") return;
 991 |   let ok = false;
 992 |   try {
 993 |     ok = await Promise.race([
 994 |       Promise.all([layoutReady, resultsReady]).then((results) => results.every(Boolean)),
 995 |       new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(false), AUTOPLAY_TIMEOUT_MS)),
 996 |     ]);
 997 |   } catch (_) {
 998 |     ok = false;
 999 |   }
1000 |   if (!ok || userActed || state.runId) return;
1001 |   runGuidedScenario();
1002 | }
1003 | 
1004 | const layoutReady = preloadLayout();
1005 | const resultsReady = loadResults();
1006 | autoplayOnLoad(layoutReady, resultsReady);
1007 | 
1008 | // Optional: ?auto runs a seed on load (used for smoke screenshots).
1009 | // ?auto=day30 also jumps to the final day. Harmless otherwise.
1010 | const auto = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("auto");
1011 | if (auto !== null) {
1012 |   startRun({ autoplay: false }).then(() => {
1013 |     const m = /^day(\d+)$/.exec(auto);
1014 |     if (auto === "end") paintDay(state.totalDays);
1015 |     else if (m) paintDay(Math.min(state.totalDays, Number(m[1])));
1016 |     else play();
1017 |     // ?res=wall drives the slider to its hard stop (for screenshots).
1018 |     if (new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("res") === "wall") {
1019 |       els.resSlider.value = String(state.maxResIdx);
1020 |       els.resSlider.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
1021 |     }
1022 |   });
1023 | }

web/gates.js

 1 | "use strict";
 2 | 
 3 | // Fills the worked-example tables under the gate explanations. The numbers
 4 | // come from GET /api/gates, which calls the engine's own threshold function,
 5 | // so nothing here (or in the HTML) is a typed-in copy of a threshold.
 6 | (function () {
 7 |   const hosts = document.querySelectorAll("[data-gate-examples]");
 8 |   if (!hosts.length) return;
 9 | 
10 |   function cell(tag, text) {
11 |     const el = document.createElement(tag);
12 |     el.textContent = text;
13 |     return el;
14 |   }
15 | 
16 |   function table(caption, head, rows) {
17 |     const t = document.createElement("table");
18 |     t.className = "gate-example";
19 |     const cap = document.createElement("caption");
20 |     cap.textContent = caption;
21 |     t.appendChild(cap);
22 |     const thead = document.createElement("thead");
23 |     const hr = document.createElement("tr");
24 |     for (const h of head) hr.appendChild(cell("th", h));
25 |     thead.appendChild(hr);
26 |     t.appendChild(thead);
27 |     const tbody = document.createElement("tbody");
28 |     for (const r of rows) {
29 |       const tr = document.createElement("tr");
30 |       for (const c of r) tr.appendChild(cell("td", String(c)));
31 |       tbody.appendChild(tr);
32 |     }
33 |     t.appendChild(tbody);
34 |     return t;
35 |   }
36 | 
37 |   // constants typed in the notation come from the same response, so the
38 |   // page cannot drift from the configured thresholds
39 |   function fillConstants(data) {
40 |     const p = data.privacy;
41 |     const values = {
42 |       "statistical.floor": data.statistical.floor,
43 |       "privacy.min_population": p.min_population,
44 |       "privacy.max_fraction": p.max_fraction,
45 |       "privacy.max_fraction_words":
46 |         p.max_fraction === 0.5 ? "half" : Math.round(p.max_fraction * 100) + "%",
47 |     };
48 |     for (const el of document.querySelectorAll("[data-gate-const]")) {
49 |       const v = values[el.dataset.gateConst];
50 |       if (v !== undefined) el.textContent = String(v);
51 |     }
52 |   }
53 | 
54 |   function render(data) {
55 |     fillConstants(data);
56 |     for (const host of hosts) {
57 |       host.textContent = "";
58 |       const scroll = document.createElement("div");
59 |       scroll.className = "table-scroll";
60 |       if (host.dataset.gateExamples === "statistical") {
61 |         scroll.appendChild(table(
62 |           "Reports needed before a group can be named",
63 |           ["Group", "People", "Reports needed"],
64 |           data.statistical.rows.map((r) => [r.scope, r.population, r.required])
65 |         ));
66 |       } else {
67 |         const p = data.privacy;
68 |         scroll.appendChild(table(
69 |           `Minimum ${p.min_population} people; refused once more than ` +
70 |           `${Math.round(p.max_fraction * 100)}% of the group has reported`,
71 |           ["Group", "People", "Big enough to name?", "Refused once reports reach"],
72 |           p.rows.map((r) => [
73 |             r.scope, r.population, r.meets_minimum ? "yes" : "no", r.refused_from,
74 |           ])
75 |         ));
76 |       }
77 |       host.appendChild(scroll);
78 |     }
79 |   }
80 | 
81 |   async function load(tries) {
82 |     for (let i = 0; i < tries; i++) {
83 |       try {
84 |         const res = await fetch("/api/gates");
85 |         if (res.ok) return render(await res.json());
86 |       } catch (_) { /* retry */ }
87 |       await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400 * (i + 1)));
88 |     }
89 |     for (const host of hosts) {
90 |       host.textContent = "Worked numbers load from the running server and are unavailable right now.";
91 |     }
92 |   }
93 | 
94 |   load(4);
95 | })();

web/index.html

  1 | <!doctype html>
  2 | <html lang="en">
  3 | <head>
  4 | <meta charset="utf-8">
  5 | <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  6 | <title>MicroCluster simulator</title>
  7 | <link rel="stylesheet" href="style.css?v=16">
  8 | </head>
  9 | <body>
 10 | <a class="skip-link" href="#main">Skip to main content</a>
 11 | <p id="disclaimer" class="limitations" role="note">
 12 |   Illustrative simulation. Transmission, reporting, contact and incubation values are
 13 |   chosen for demonstration, not measured from any real population. Tested at 150 to 2,000
 14 |   simulated people and against a harsher simulated reporting model, never against real
 15 |   reports. This predicts nothing about any real building, and no one is diagnosed or
 16 |   treated by this tool.
 17 | </p>
 18 | 
 19 | <header class="intro">
 20 |   <h1>MicroCluster</h1>
 21 |   <p class="tagline">It detects the signal without identifying the reporter, and won't name a group too small or too covered to name safely.</p>
 22 |   <p class="explainer">
 23 |     An illness spreads through a simulated dorm floor. A detector that sees only
 24 |     anonymous symptom reports, never a name or a room, tries to find the outbreak it
 25 |     was never told about. When it finds one, it names the finest scope that is still
 26 |     safe to name, never one small enough to identify a reporter. Everything on this
 27 |     page is simulated.
 28 |   </p>
 29 |   <details class="how">
 30 |     <summary>How it works</summary>
 31 |     <ol>
 32 |       <li>Anonymous reports flow in: one symptom category from a fixed checklist, a
 33 |         rough onset, and which group the reporter is in. No name, no free text, no
 34 |         room number.</li>
 35 |       <li>The detector flags a statistical excess of reports inside a rolling time
 36 |         window, either against a location's neighbours or against a background rate.</li>
 37 |       <li>Two independent gates then decide whether anything may be said. Both must pass.
 38 |         <div class="gate-rule">
 39 |           <p><strong>Evidence gate.</strong> The bigger the group you want to name, the more
 40 |           reports you need before the system will name it, and never fewer than
 41 |           <span data-gate-const="statistical.floor">5</span>.</p>
 42 |           <p class="gate-formula">In symbols:
 43 |           <math aria-label="at least five, or the square root of the population rounded up, whichever is larger"><mrow><mi>needed</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>max</mi><mo>(</mo><mn data-gate-const="statistical.floor">5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&lceil;</mo><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt><mo>&rceil;</mo><mo>)</mo></mrow></math>,
 44 |           where <math aria-label="n"><mi>n</mi></math> is how many people are in the group.</p>
 45 |           <div class="gate-examples" data-gate-examples="statistical">Worked numbers load from the running server.</div>
 46 |         </div>
 47 |         <div class="gate-rule">
 48 |           <p><strong>Privacy gate.</strong> The system never names a group with fewer than
 49 |           <span data-gate-const="privacy.min_population">20</span> people, and never names a group where
 50 |           more than <span data-gate-const="privacy.max_fraction_words">half</span> of the members have
 51 |           reported, because that announcement would be close to a list of who is ill.</p>
 52 |           <p class="gate-formula">In symbols, with <math aria-label="q"><mi>q</mi></math> reports from a group of
 53 |           <math aria-label="n"><mi>n</mi></math> people:
 54 |           <math aria-label="the group has at least twenty people"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&ge;</mo><mn data-gate-const="privacy.min_population">20</mn></mrow></math>
 55 |           and
 56 |           <math aria-label="the reports divided by the group size is at most one half"><mrow><mfrac><mi>q</mi><mi>n</mi></mfrac><mo>&le;</mo><mn data-gate-const="privacy.max_fraction">0.5</mn></mrow></math>.</p>
 57 |           <div class="gate-examples" data-gate-examples="privacy">Worked numbers load from the running server.</div>
 58 |         </div>
 59 |       </li>
 60 |       <li>The system names the finest scope that clears both gates. If no scope
 61 |         clears them, it discloses nothing.</li>
 62 |     </ol>
 63 |   </details>
 64 | </header>
 65 |

 78 |         settings (seed 4, transmission 0.025, reporting 0.80), the detector fires on
 79 |         day 3, one suite is named on day 4, then by day 8 the privacy gate forces the

127 |         <span class="hint" id="p-population-help">people sharing one suite &ndash; also the detector's smallest group</span>
128 |       </label>
129 |       <label class="param">Transmission (per contact / day)
130 |         <input type="range" id="p-transmission" min="0.001" max="0.04" step="0.001" value="0.008"
131 |           aria-describedby="p-transmission-hint p-transmission-help">
132 |         <span class="hint" id="p-transmission-hint">0.008</span>
133 |         <span class="hint" id="p-transmission-help">chance an infected person passes it to each person they're near, per day</span>
134 |       </label>
135 |       <label class="param">Reporting rate (while symptomatic)
136 |         <input type="range" id="p-reporting" min="0.1" max="1" step="0.05" value="0.5"
137 |           aria-describedby="p-reporting-hint p-reporting-help">
138 |         <span class="hint" id="p-reporting-hint">0.50</span>
139 |         <span class="hint" id="p-reporting-help">chance a symptomatic person files a report at all</span>
140 |       </label>
141 |       <label class="param">Detection window (hours)
142 |         <input type="number" id="p-window" value="72" min="24" max="168" step="12"
143 |           aria-describedby="p-window-hint p-window-help">
144 |         <span class="hint" id="p-window-hint">rolling window for &ldquo;current&rdquo; reports</span>
145 |         <span class="hint" id="p-window-help">hours of recent reports the detector treats as happening right now</span>
146 |       </label>
147 |     </fieldset>

236 | <section class="results-summary" aria-label="headline findings">
237 |   <h2>What the experiments found</h2>
238 |   <p class="panel-caption">Three findings from the precomputed runs, all with reports generated independently of one another.</p>
239 |   <ul id="summary-list">
240 |     <li class="muted">Loading findings.</li>
241 |   </ul>
242 |   <p class="results-conclusion" id="reporting-scope">These figures assume independent, memoryless reporting. Under correlated reporting the measured effect is a large rise in false alarms, from 60% to 78 to 100% of outbreaks that never take off, with about 3% of those then naming a group falsely.</p>
243 | </section>
244 | 
245 | <section class="results" id="result-cost" aria-label="the cost of the privacy policy">
246 |   <h2>The cost of the privacy policy</h2>
247 |   <p class="panel-caption">
248 |     How many more people are infected for each extra day the privacy rules delay
249 |     disclosure. A flatter line means restraint is cheap; a steep one means it is
250 |     expensive.
251 |   </p>
252 |   <p class="results-note">
253 |     Each point is one setting of the statistical gate, over 500 simulated outbreaks
254 |     with detection held fixed and independent reporting. Non-disclosing outbreaks count their full day-30 total.
255 |   </p>
256 |   <div id="cost-chart" class="chart"></div>
257 | </section>
258 | 
259 | <section class="results" id="result-benchmark" aria-label="rules versus learned model">
260 |   <h2>Rules vs learned model</h2>
261 |   <p class="panel-caption">
262 |     Whether a model fitted to the data beats the hand-written rules. A good result for
263 |     the rules is comparable precision and recall without the model's extra false
264 |     alarms.
265 |   </p>
266 |   <p class="results-note">
267 |     A logistic-regression detector trained on the three detection findings, scored
268 |     against the authored threshold rules on held-out simulation runs with independent
269 |     reporting. Both stay in the product; the model never replaces the rules.
270 |   </p>
271 |   <p class="results-note">
272 |     Precision is how often an alert is correct. Recall is how many of the real outbreaks
273 |     get caught. Mean detection delay is measured between the outbreak actually taking
274 |     hold in the simulation and the detector firing.
275 |   </p>

291 | <section class="results" id="result-robustness" aria-label="cross-parameter robustness">
292 |   <h2>Does the result hold up on data it wasn't trained on?<span class="h2-sub">Cross-parameter robustness check</span></h2>
293 |   <p class="panel-caption">
294 |     Whether the model's small edge is real or just memorised: it is trained at one
295 |     transmission probability and tested at another. If the edge disappears here, it was
296 |     overfitting.
297 |   </p>
298 |   <p class="results-note">
299 |     The circularity risk stated plainly: a classifier trained on this simulator learns
300 |     this simulator. This panel reports what happens when the transmission regime shifts;
301 |     reporting stays independent in every regime.
302 |   </p>
303 |   <p class="results-note">
304 |     Precision is how often an alert is correct. Recall is how many of the real outbreaks
305 |     get caught. Mean delay is measured between the outbreak actually taking hold and the
306 |     detector firing.

327 |     result is that the banded output leaves them almost nothing.

web/privacy.html

  1 | <!doctype html>
  2 | <html lang="en">
  3 | <head>
  4 | <meta charset="utf-8">
  5 | <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  6 | <title>MicroCluster Privacy policy</title>
  7 | <link rel="stylesheet" href="style.css?v=16">
  8 | </head>
  9 | <body>
 10 | <p class="limitations" role="note">
 11 |   Illustrative simulation. Transmission, reporting, contact and incubation values are
 12 |   chosen for demonstration, not measured from any real population. Tested at 150 to 2,000
 13 |   simulated people and against a harsher simulated reporting model, never against real
 14 |   reports. This predicts nothing about any real building, and no one is diagnosed or
 15 |   treated by this tool.
 16 | </p>
 17 | <article class="static-page">
 18 |   <p class="back"><a href="index.html">Back to the simulator</a></p>
 19 |   <h1>Privacy policy</h1>
 20 |   <p class="updated">This describes the mechanism as built. It is not legal boilerplate.</p>
 21 | 
 22 |   <p>
 23 |     MicroCluster is a privacy-preserving health-signal system for small shared
 24 |     communities. It looks for statistically unusual symptom activity and discloses
 25 |     it only at a scope that is both statistically supported and privacy-safe. The
 26 |     running page on this site is an illustrative simulation of that system: the
 27 |     people, the outbreak, and every parameter are synthetic.
 28 |   </p>
 29 | 
 30 |   <h2>What a real deployment collects</h2>
 31 |   <p>Each report is a single submission from a fixed checklist:</p>
 32 |   <ul>
 33 |     <li>one of four <strong>symptom categories</strong> (respiratory, gastrointestinal, general, other);</li>
 34 |     <li>a specific symptom from that category, recorded for the reporter's own clarity and <strong>never used for matching</strong>;</li>
 35 |     <li>a <strong>coarse onset bucket</strong>: today, 1&ndash;2 days, 3&ndash;7 days, 1+ week;</li>
 36 |     <li>one <strong>location group</strong> from the community's scope hierarchy, at the finest level the reporter belongs to.</li>
 37 |   </ul>
 38 | 
 39 |   <h2>What it never collects</h2>
 40 |   <ul>
 41 |     <li>No name, account, login, device identifier, or contact information.</li>
 42 |     <li>No free text. Every field is a choice from a closed vocabulary, so there is nothing to interpret and nothing to leak in prose.</li>
 43 |     <li>No precise timestamp. Onset is a bucket; submission time is only used to decide whether a report falls inside the current detection window.</li>
 44 |     <li>No precise location. The finest location is a declared group, not a coordinate or a room.</li>
 45 |     <li>No medical concept finer than a category. The system has no code path that reasons about a specific diagnosis.</li>
 46 |   </ul>
 47 |   <p>
 48 |     The privacy strategy is <strong>data minimisation, not encryption</strong>. The system
 49 |     never holds identity, free text, precise time, or precise location, so there is very
 50 |     little to encrypt. You cannot leak what you did not collect.
 51 |   </p>
 52 | 
 53 |   <h2>How the gates work</h2>
 54 |   <p>
 55 |     Detection and disclosure are separate. Detection decides whether something unusual is
 56 |     happening. Disclosure decides what, if anything, may be said, and at what scope. A
 57 |     scope is disclosed only if it passes <strong>both</strong> of two gates, which are
 58 |     evaluated independently and never combined into one number:
 59 |   </p>
 60 |   <div class="gate-rule">
 61 |     <p><strong>Evidence gate.</strong> The bigger the group you want to name, the more
 62 |     reports you need before the system will name it, and never fewer than
 63 |     <span data-gate-const="statistical.floor">5</span>.</p>
 64 |     <p class="gate-formula">In symbols:
 65 |     <math aria-label="at least five, or the square root of the population rounded up, whichever is larger"><mrow><mi>needed</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>max</mi><mo>(</mo><mn data-gate-const="statistical.floor">5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&lceil;</mo><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt><mo>&rceil;</mo><mo>)</mo></mrow></math>,
 66 |     where <math aria-label="n"><mi>n</mi></math> is how many people are in the group.</p>
 67 |     <div class="gate-examples" data-gate-examples="statistical">Worked numbers load from the running server.</div>
 68 |   </div>
 69 |   <div class="gate-rule">
 70 |     <p><strong>Privacy gate.</strong> The system never names a group with fewer than
 71 |     <span data-gate-const="privacy.min_population">20</span> people, and never names a group where
 72 |     more than <span data-gate-const="privacy.max_fraction_words">half</span> of the members have
 73 |     reported, because that announcement would be close to a list of who is ill.</p>
 74 |     <p class="gate-formula">In symbols, with <math aria-label="q"><mi>q</mi></math> reports from a group of
 75 |     <math aria-label="n"><mi>n</mi></math> people:
 76 |     <math aria-label="the group has at least twenty people"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&ge;</mo><mn data-gate-const="privacy.min_population">20</mn></mrow></math>
 77 |     and
 78 |     <math aria-label="the reports divided by the group size is at most one half"><mrow><mfrac><mi>q</mi><mi>n</mi></mfrac><mo>&le;</mo><mn data-gate-const="privacy.max_fraction">0.5</mn></mrow></math>.</p>
 79 |     <div class="gate-examples" data-gate-examples="privacy">Worked numbers load from the running server.</div>
 80 |   </div>
 81 |   <p>
 82 |     The two gates pull in opposite directions by design. The system discloses the finest
 83 |     scope that clears both; if none does, it discloses nothing. Every candidate scope,
 84 |     including the rejected ones, gets a full evaluation record with the reason for its
 85 |     outcome. The disclosure engine is only ever handed aggregate per-scope counts and
 86 |     declared populations, never individual reports.
 87 |   </p>
 88 | 
 89 |   <h2>What the gates guarantee, and what they do not</h2>
 90 |   <p>
 91 |     In established terms, the two gates together enforce three things about every group the
 92 |     system names, every time, by construction:
 93 |   </p>
 94 |   <ul>
 95 |     <li><strong>A group-size floor.</strong> The named group has at least 20 people. This is a
 96 |       k-anonymity-style property (k of at least 20): the statement is about a group, and
 97 |       any member is indistinguishable from the others in it, because reports carry nothing
 98 |       finer than a group.</li>
 99 |     <li><strong>Minimum support.</strong> The statement rests on at least 5 reports, more for
100 |       bigger groups. This is a standard small-count threshold rule.</li>
101 |     <li><strong>A prevalence cap.</strong> At most half of the group can be among the reporters,
102 |       so knowing someone is in the named group never makes them more than 50% likely to be a
103 |       reporter. This limits the homogeneity weakness of k-anonymity. It is a simple cap, not
104 |       a full l-diversity or t-closeness guarantee.</li>
105 |   </ul>
106 |   <p>
107 |     That is all the gates promise. They do <strong>not</strong> provide differential privacy. Differential
108 |     privacy adds calibrated random noise so that any one person's data changes what is
109 |     released by at most a bounded amount. These gates add no noise and use hard thresholds on
110 |     exact counts: one extra report can be the difference between naming a group and saying
111 |     nothing, so the change in output from one person is not bounded. They also keep no
112 |     privacy budget, so many daily releases over overlapping data are not accounted for, and
113 |     they give no protection against someone who already knows almost all of the reports.
114 |   </p>
115 |   <p>
116 |     They also do not guarantee protection from the operator who holds the reports, do not
117 |     stop an attacker who injects reports, do not verify that a group's declared size is
118 |     true, and do not make being named as a group with unusual illness harmless to its
119 |     members. The full write-up is in docs/privacy_guarantee.md in the repository.
120 |   </p>
121 | 
122 |   <h2>What the bands are for</h2>
123 |   <p>
124 |     The interface shows qualifying report counts as ranges (&ldquo;5&ndash;9&rdquo;,
125 |     &ldquo;10&ndash;19&rdquo;), never exact integers. This is a defence against a
126 |     differencing attack: if the exact count for a named group were visible on two
127 |     consecutive days, an observer could subtract them and learn that exactly one
128 |     identifiable person changed state overnight. The exact count stays on the server;
129 |     only the band is sent to the page. This reduces that leak; it does not eliminate
130 |     every inference. A named group with sustained activity in a known band still tells an
131 |     observer something, and the system's own adversarial self-test reports exactly what.
132 |   </p>
133 | 
134 |   <h2>Abuse</h2>
135 |   <p>
136 |     Because reports are anonymous, someone can submit reports to manufacture a signal. The
137 |     system accepts this as the cost of anonymity. It rate-limits submissions per session
138 |     (a per-session and a per-session-per-day cap, enforced server side), but it does not
139 |     authenticate reporters, so a determined attacker using multiple sessions can still
140 |     inject reports. No identity-based control is used, because identity is the thing the
141 |     system exists to protect.
142 |   </p>
143 | 
144 |   <p class="back"><a href="index.html">Back to the simulator</a></p>
145 | </article>
146 | <script src="gates.js?v=16"></script>
147 | </body>
148 | </html>

  4 |    interaction feedback, capped at 400ms, never looping -- see the MOTION

662 | 
663 | /* ---- motion: state-change and interaction feedback only ---------------
664 |    CLAUDE.md UI CONSTRAINTS, "Motion" -- added because the page was fully
665 |    static and nothing moved until a judge clicked a button they might not
666 |    click. Every rule below either (a) carries a state change already
667 |    happening in the simulation, or (b) is ordinary interaction feedback.
668 |    Nothing loops, nothing exceeds 400ms, nothing here is purely decorative
669 |    except the single symptomatic-onset ring. Every rule is neutralised
670 |    under prefers-reduced-motion: reduce, at the bottom of this section.
671 |    This section is purely additive -- it sets no property any other rule
672 |    in this file also sets, so no existing rest-state appearance changes. */
673 | 
674 | .agent { transition: background-color 180ms ease-out, border-color 180ms ease-out; }
675 | 
676 | /* one-shot ring, monochrome, no repeat -- app.js adds .pulse for exactly
677 |    one frame on the instant a circle turns symptomatic during a real
678 |    forward playback step, and only then (never on load, scrub, or
679 |    ?auto=dayN), so no screenshot ever shows it mid-flight */
680 | .agent.pulse::before {
681 |   content: "";
682 |   position: absolute;
683 |   top: 50%; left: 50%;
684 |   width: 16px; height: 16px;
685 |   margin: -8px 0 0 -8px;
686 |   border: 1px solid var(--ink);
687 |   border-radius: 50%;
688 |   opacity: 0.5;
689 |   pointer-events: none;
690 |   animation: agent-ring 350ms ease-out;
691 | }
692 | @keyframes agent-ring {
693 |   from { transform: scale(0.9); opacity: 0.5; }
694 |   to   { transform: scale(1.9); opacity: 0; }
695 | }
696 | 
697 | @keyframes fade-rise-in {
698 |   from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(4px); }
699 |   to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
700 | }
701 | /* :not(:empty) starting to match IS the refusal box appearing; animation-name
702 |    goes none -> fade-rise-in right then, so this needs no JS trigger */
703 | .refusal:not(:empty) { animation: fade-rise-in 200ms ease-out; }
704 | /* app.js toggles .change-in for one tick when the disclosed scope changes */
705 | .status.change-in { animation: fade-rise-in 200ms ease-out; }
706 | 
707 | /* app.js toggles .verdict-changed on a row for one tick, only on a real
708 |    forward step, only when that scope's PASS/FAIL actually changed; the
709 |    removal (not the arrival) is what transitions, so it reads as a fade,
710 |    and it always settles back to no background -- nothing persists */
711 | #gate-table tr { transition: background-color 150ms ease-out; }
712 | #gate-table tr.verdict-changed { background-color: var(--panel-head); }
713 | 
714 | button, input[type="number"], input[type="range"], select {
715 |   transition: background-color 120ms ease-out, border-color 120ms ease-out;
716 | }
717 | 
718 | @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
719 |   .agent, #gate-table tr, button, input[type="number"], input[type="range"], select {
720 |     transition: none !important;
721 |   }
722 |   .agent.pulse::before { content: none; }
723 |   #gate-table tr.verdict-changed { background-color: transparent; }
724 |   .refusal:not(:empty), .status.change-in { animation: none !important; }
725 | }

web/terms.html

 1 | <!doctype html>
 2 | <html lang="en">
 3 | <head>
 4 | <meta charset="utf-8">
 5 | <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
 6 | <title>MicroCluster Terms of Service</title>
 7 | <link rel="stylesheet" href="style.css?v=16">
 8 | </head>
 9 | <body>
10 | <p class="limitations" role="note">
11 |   Illustrative simulation. Transmission, reporting, contact and incubation values are
12 |   chosen for demonstration, not measured from any real population. Tested at 150 to 2,000
13 |   simulated people and against a harsher simulated reporting model, never against real
14 |   reports. This predicts nothing about any real building, and no one is diagnosed or
15 |   treated by this tool.
16 | </p>
17 | <article class="static-page">
18 |   <p class="back"><a href="index.html">Back to the simulator</a></p>
19 |   <h1>Terms of Service</h1>
20 |   <p class="updated">Plain terms for the demonstration site.</p>
21 | 
22 |   <h2>What this is</h2>
23 |   <p>
24 |     This site runs an illustrative simulation of the MicroCluster health-signal system.
25 |     The population, the outbreak, the reports, and every parameter are synthetic and are
26 |     chosen to produce a legible demonstration. The simulation does not model, predict, or
27 |     describe real disease transmission in any real community.
28 |   </p>
29 | 
30 |   <h2>Not medical advice</h2>
31 |   <p>
32 |     Nothing on this site is medical, epidemiological, or public-health advice. The system
33 |     does not diagnose anyone and is not designed to. Do not use any output here to make a
34 |     decision about your health or anyone else's.
35 |   </p>
36 | 
37 |   <h2>Acceptable use</h2>
38 |   <p>
39 |     You may run simulations, read the precomputed results, and inspect the code. Do not
40 |     attempt to overload the service, and do not present its output as describing a real
41 |     outbreak or a real group of people.
42 |   </p>
43 | 
44 |   <h2>Data you submit</h2>
45 |   <p>
46 |     Starting a simulation sends a seed and a set of numeric parameters to the server. No
47 |     personal information is requested or required. What the system does and does not
48 |     collect in a real deployment is described in the
49 |     <a href="privacy.html">privacy policy</a>.
50 |   </p>
51 | 
52 |   <h2>Availability and changes</h2>
53 |   <p>
54 |     The service is provided as is, with no warranty and no guarantee of availability.
55 |     Runs are held in memory and are discarded over time. These terms and the underlying
56 |     system may change without notice.
57 |   </p>

59 |   <p class="back"><a href="index.html">Back to the simulator</a></p>
