tests/fixtures.py

 1 | """Shared test fixtures: a reference scope registry and report builders."""
 2 | 
 3 | from __future__ import annotations
 4 | 
 5 | from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
 6 | 
 7 | from microcluster.models import (
 8 |     CHECKLIST,
 9 |     Category,
10 |     Onset,
11 |     Report,
12 |     Scope,
13 |     ScopeLevel,
14 |     ScopeRegistry,
15 | )
16 | 
17 | # A fixed evaluation instant so every test is deterministic.
18 | NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 1, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
19 | 
20 | 
21 | def standard_registry() -> ScopeRegistry:
22 |     """Campus n=2000: five buildings of 400; building B1 has three floors
23 |     (~130 each); floor F1 has five suites (~26 each)."""
24 |     scopes: dict[str, Scope] = {}
25 | 
26 |     def add(scope: Scope) -> None:
27 |         scopes[scope.id] = scope
28 | 
29 |     add(Scope("C", "Campus", ScopeLevel.CAMPUS, population=2000))
30 |     for b in range(1, 6):
31 |         add(Scope(f"B{b}", f"Building {b}", ScopeLevel.BUILDING, 400, parent_id="C"))
32 | 
33 |     add(Scope("F1", "Bldg 1 / Floor 1", ScopeLevel.FLOOR, 130, parent_id="B1"))
34 |     add(Scope("F2", "Bldg 1 / Floor 2", ScopeLevel.FLOOR, 130, parent_id="B1"))
35 |     add(Scope("F3", "Bldg 1 / Floor 3", ScopeLevel.FLOOR, 140, parent_id="B1"))
36 | 
37 |     for s in range(1, 6):
38 |         add(Scope(f"S{s}", f"Bldg 1 / Fl 1 / Suite {s}", ScopeLevel.SUITE, 26, parent_id="F1"))
39 | 
40 |     return ScopeRegistry(scopes)
41 | 
42 | 
43 | def small_registry_floor18() -> ScopeRegistry:
44 |     """A campus with a floor of declared population 18 whose parent
45 |     building is declared 25. Used for the privacy-refusal scenario."""
46 |     return ScopeRegistry(
47 |         {
48 |             "C": Scope("C", "Campus", ScopeLevel.CAMPUS, population=300),
49 |             "B": Scope("B", "Annex building", ScopeLevel.BUILDING, 25, parent_id="C"),
50 |             "F": Scope("F", "Annex / top floor", ScopeLevel.FLOOR, 18, parent_id="B"),
51 |         }
52 |     )
53 | 
54 | 
55 | def roster_registry() -> ScopeRegistry:
56 |     """A large campus (n=2000) with one small declared group (n=25).
57 |     Used for the "disclosure would be a roster" scenario: enough reports
58 |     to clear the statistical gate and the population minimum, but so many
59 |     relative to n=25 that naming the group names its members."""
60 |     return ScopeRegistry(
61 |         {
62 |             "C": Scope("C", "Campus", ScopeLevel.CAMPUS, population=2000),
63 |             "G": Scope("G", "Studio group", ScopeLevel.SUITE, 25, parent_id="C"),
64 |         }
65 |     )
66 | 
67 | 
68 | def single_scope_registry(n: int) -> ScopeRegistry:
69 |     """A campus-only registry of declared population ``n``. Used for the
70 |     across-populations gate table."""
71 |     return ScopeRegistry({"C": Scope("C", f"Campus n={n}", ScopeLevel.CAMPUS, population=n)})
72 | 
73 | 
74 | def report(
75 |     location_id: str,
76 |     *,
77 |     category: Category = Category.RESPIRATORY,
78 |     hours_ago: float = 1.0,
79 |     onset: Onset = Onset.TODAY,
80 |     now: datetime = NOW,
81 | ) -> Report:
82 |     """One report at ``location_id``, submitted ``hours_ago`` before
83 |     ``now``. Symptom is the first in the category's checklist -- it is
84 |     never used for matching, so its value does not matter to the engines."""
85 |     return Report(
86 |         category=category,
87 |         symptom=CHECKLIST[category][0],
88 |         onset=onset,
89 |         location_id=location_id,
90 |         submitted_at=now - timedelta(hours=hours_ago),
91 |     )
92 | 
93 | 
94 | def reports(location_id: str, count: int, **kwargs) -> list[Report]:
95 |     return [report(location_id, **kwargs) for _ in range(count)]

tests/test_adversarial.py

  1 | """Adversarial self-test tests.
  2 | 
  3 | The important ones are on the ATTACK: the differencing attack must
  4 | provably pin a one-person overnight change when it is handed exact counts,
  5 | otherwise the banded-vs-exact contrast demonstrates nothing.
  6 | """
  7 | 
  8 | from __future__ import annotations
  9 | 
 10 | import unittest
 11 | 
 12 | from api.bands import band
 13 | from adversarial.differencing import attack
 14 | from adversarial.observer import DayView, observe
 15 | from adversarial.selftest import run_self_test
 16 | from adversarial.wandering import measure_wandering
 17 | from simulation.pipeline import run_with_detection, simulate_and_report
 18 | 
 19 | 
 20 | def _views(scope, counts, *, exact):
 21 |     out = []
 22 |     for i, c in enumerate(counts):
 23 |         out.append(
 24 |             DayView(
 25 |                 day=i,
 26 |                 scope_id=scope,
 27 |                 scope_label=scope,
 28 |                 scope_level="SUITE",
 29 |                 exact_count=c if exact else None,
 30 |                 band=None if exact else band(c),
 31 |             )
 32 |         )
 33 |     return out
 34 | 
 35 | 
 36 | class DifferencingAgainstExactCountsTest(unittest.TestCase):
 37 |     def test_pins_every_one_person_overnight_change(self) -> None:
 38 |         out = attack(_views("S", [7, 8, 9, 8, 8], exact=True), mode="exact")
 39 |         self.assertEqual(out.same_scope_pairs, 4)
 40 |         self.assertEqual(out.pinned_exactly, 4)
 41 |         self.assertEqual(out.one_person_pinned, 3)
 42 |         self.assertEqual(out.direction_known, 3)
 43 |         self.assertEqual(out.unbounded_pairs, 0)
 44 | 
 45 |     def test_two_person_change_is_pinned_but_not_one_person(self) -> None:
 46 |         out = attack(_views("S", [10, 12], exact=True), mode="exact")
 47 |         self.assertEqual(out.pinned_exactly, 1)
 48 |         self.assertEqual(out.one_person_pinned, 0)
 49 | 
 50 |     def test_a_scope_change_between_days_yields_no_pair(self) -> None:
 51 |         views = [
 52 |             DayView(0, "A", "A", "SUITE", 7, None),
 53 |             DayView(1, "B", "B", "SUITE", 8, None),
 54 |         ]
 55 |         self.assertEqual(attack(views, mode="exact").same_scope_pairs, 0)
 56 | 
 57 | 
 58 | class DifferencingAgainstBandsTest(unittest.TestCase):
 59 |     def test_one_person_pin_is_impossible(self) -> None:
 60 |         out = attack(_views("S", [7, 8, 9, 8, 8], exact=False), mode="band")
 61 |         self.assertEqual(out.same_scope_pairs, 4)
 62 |         self.assertEqual(out.one_person_pinned, 0)
 63 |         self.assertEqual(out.pinned_exactly, 0)
 64 | 
 65 |     def test_direction_can_still_leak_across_a_band_edge(self) -> None:
 66 |         out = attack(_views("S", [9, 10], exact=False), mode="band")
 67 |         self.assertEqual(out.one_person_pinned, 0)
 68 |         self.assertEqual(out.direction_known, 1)
 69 | 
 70 |     def test_within_a_band_nothing_leaks(self) -> None:
 71 |         out = attack(_views("S", [5, 9], exact=False), mode="band")
 72 |         self.assertEqual(out.direction_known, 0)
 73 |         self.assertGreater(out.median_possible_values, 1)
 74 | 
 75 | 
 76 | class ObserverTest(unittest.TestCase):
 77 |     def test_exact_and_band_views_populate_exactly_one_count_field(self) -> None:
 78 |         run = run_with_detection(seed=4, days=30)
 79 |         exact = observe(run.daily_records, leak_exact_counts=True)
 80 |         banded = observe(run.daily_records, leak_exact_counts=False)
 81 |         self.assertEqual(len(exact), len(run.daily_records))
 82 |         for ev, bv, rec in zip(exact, banded, run.daily_records):
 83 |             if rec.disclosed_scope_id is None:
 84 |                 self.assertIsNone(ev.scope_id)
 85 |                 continue
 86 |             self.assertIsNotNone(ev.exact_count)
 87 |             self.assertIsNone(ev.band)
 88 |             self.assertIsNone(bv.exact_count)
 89 |             self.assertEqual(bv.band, band(ev.exact_count))
 90 | 
 91 |     def test_exact_count_matches_the_selected_scope_evaluation(self) -> None:
 92 |         run = run_with_detection(seed=4, days=30)
 93 |         for rec, view in zip(run.daily_records, observe(run.daily_records, leak_exact_counts=True)):
 94 |             if rec.disclosed_scope_id is None:
 95 |                 continue
 96 |             selected = next(
 97 |                 e for e in rec.disclosure_evaluations if e.scope_id == rec.disclosed_scope_id
 98 |             )
 99 |             self.assertEqual(view.exact_count, selected.qualifying_reports)
100 | 
101 | 
102 | class WanderingTest(unittest.TestCase):
103 |     def test_stability_off_names_more_groups_in_aggregate(self) -> None:
104 |         total_on = total_off = 0
105 |         for seed in range(1, 16):
106 |             w = measure_wandering(simulate_and_report(seed=seed, days=30))
107 |             total_on += w.stability_on
108 |             total_off += w.stability_off
109 |         self.assertLess(total_on, total_off)
110 | 
111 | 
112 | class SelfTestIntegrationTest(unittest.TestCase):
113 |     def test_attack_succeeds_on_exact_and_fails_on_bands(self) -> None:
114 |         s = run_self_test(seeds=list(range(1, 25)))
115 |         self.assertGreater(s.same_scope_pairs, 0)
116 |         self.assertGreater(
117 |             s.exact_one_person_pinned, 0, "differencing attack proved nothing against exact counts"
118 |         )
119 |         self.assertEqual(s.band_one_person_pinned, 0)
120 |         self.assertGreaterEqual(s.wandering_off_mean, s.wandering_on_mean)
121 | 
122 | 
123 | class InjectionAttackTest(unittest.TestCase):
124 |     """The intake cap must raise the attacker's session count without
125 |     claiming to close the hole -- exactly what the docs say it does."""
126 | 
127 |     def test_cap_blocks_one_session_but_not_a_session_rotating_attacker(self) -> None:
128 |         from adversarial.injection import measure_injection
129 | 
130 |         r = measure_injection()  # target FLOOR C-B1-F2, declared population 75
131 |         self.assertEqual(r.target_level, "FLOOR")
132 | 
133 |         # the report count is set by the statistical gate, not the cap
134 |         self.assertEqual(r.reports_to_disclose, 9)  # ceil(sqrt(75)) = 9
135 |         self.assertEqual(r.sessions_without_cap, 1)
136 |         self.assertEqual(
137 |             r.sessions_with_cap,
138 |             -(-r.reports_to_disclose // r.session_cap),  # ceil(9 / 3) = 3
139 |         )
140 | 
141 |         # the cap stops a single-session flood ...
142 |         self.assertFalse(r.single_session_attack_succeeds)
143 |         # ... and does nothing against an attacker who rotates sessions
144 |         self.assertTrue(r.multi_session_attack_succeeds)
145 | 
146 | 
147 | if __name__ == "__main__":
148 |     unittest.main()

tests/test_api.py

  1 | """API-layer tests.
  2 | 
  3 | The engines and the simulator have their own suites; these only check the
  4 | HTTP surface: shapes, the day range, the parameter passthrough, and -- the
  5 | security-relevant one -- that a day payload carries no raw reports or
  6 | ground truth beyond the agent states the animation renders.
  7 | """
  8 | 
  9 | from __future__ import annotations
 10 | 
 11 | import unittest
 12 | 
 13 | import re
 14 | 
 15 | from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
 16 | 
 17 | from api.app import DISCLAIMER, app
 18 | 
 19 | # High transmission + near-certain reporting: a suite reliably blows past
 20 | # the report-fraction cap and fails the privacy gate (the "roster" refusal).
 21 | _ROSTER_PARAMS = {
 22 |     "suite_transmission_probability": 0.03,
 23 |     "floor_transmission_probability": 0.002,
 24 |     "building_transmission_probability": 0.0005,
 25 |     "reporting_probability_min": 0.85,
 26 |     "reporting_probability_max": 1.0,
 27 |     "background_noise_daily_rate": 0.003,
 28 | }
 29 | _BAND_RE = re.compile(r"^(0|\d+-\d+|\d+\+)$")
 30 | 
 31 | 
 32 | class ApiTest(unittest.TestCase):
 33 |     def setUp(self) -> None:
 34 |         self.client = TestClient(app)
 35 | 
 36 |     def _run(self, **body) -> dict:
 37 |         body.setdefault("seed", 4)
 38 |         body.setdefault("days", 12)
 39 |         res = self.client.post("/api/run", json=body)
 40 |         self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200, res.text)
 41 |         return res.json()
 42 | 
 43 |     def test_run_returns_id_layout_and_always_visible_disclaimer(self) -> None:
 44 |         data = self._run()
 45 |         self.assertIn("run_id", data)
 46 |         self.assertEqual(data["disclaimer"], DISCLAIMER)
 47 |         self.assertIn("predicts nothing about any real building", data["disclaimer"])
 48 |         self.assertIn("no one is diagnosed or treated", data["disclaimer"])
 49 |         floors = data["layout"]["floors"]
 50 |         self.assertEqual(len(floors), 2)
 51 |         self.assertEqual(sum(len(f["suites"]) for f in floors), 6)
 52 |         self.assertTrue(all(s["size"] == 25 for f in floors for s in f["suites"]))
 53 | 
 54 |     def test_population_and_detection_window_controls_take_effect(self) -> None:
 55 |         data = self._run(population=30, detection_window_hours=48, days=10)
 56 |         self.assertEqual(data["params"]["population_per_suite"], 30)
 57 |         self.assertEqual(data["params"]["total_population"], 180)
 58 |         self.assertEqual(data["params"]["detection_window_hours"], 48)
 59 |         day = self.client.get(f"/api/run/{data['run_id']}/day/5").json()
 60 |         self.assertEqual(len(day["agents"]), 180)
 61 | 
 62 |     def test_out_of_range_controls_are_422_not_500(self) -> None:
 63 |         self.assertEqual(self.client.post("/api/run", json={"seed": 1, "population": 15}).status_code, 422)
 64 |         self.assertEqual(
 65 |             self.client.post("/api/run", json={"seed": 1, "detection_window_hours": 6}).status_code,
 66 |             422,
 67 |         )
 68 | 
 69 |     def test_assets_are_served_no_cache(self) -> None:
 70 |         res = self.client.get("/style.css")
 71 |         self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
 72 |         self.assertIn("no-cache", res.headers.get("cache-control", ""))
 73 |         api_res = self.client.get("/api/health")
 74 |         self.assertNotIn("no-cache", api_res.headers.get("cache-control", ""))
 75 | 
 76 |     def test_day_zero_and_last_day_are_in_range_and_beyond_is_404(self) -> None:
 77 |         data = self._run(days=10)
 78 |         rid = data["run_id"]
 79 |         self.assertEqual(self.client.get(f"/api/run/{rid}/day/0").status_code, 200)
 80 |         self.assertEqual(self.client.get(f"/api/run/{rid}/day/10").status_code, 200)
 81 |         self.assertEqual(self.client.get(f"/api/run/{rid}/day/11").status_code, 404)
 82 |         self.assertEqual(self.client.get(f"/api/run/{rid}/day/-1").status_code, 404)

 84 |     def test_unknown_run_is_404(self) -> None:
 85 |         self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/run/deadbeef/day/0").status_code, 404)

 87 |     def test_day_payload_shape(self) -> None:

 90 |         self.assertEqual(day["day"], 5)

139 |     def test_parameter_passthrough_and_validation(self) -> None:
140 |         echoed = self._run(background_noise_daily_rate=0.02)["params"]
141 |         self.assertAlmostEqual(echoed["background_noise_daily_rate"], 0.02)
142 |         # min > max is rejected by SimulationConfig -> 422, not a 500.
143 |         bad = self.client.post(
144 |             "/api/run",
145 |             json={"seed": 1, "reporting_probability_min": 0.9, "reporting_probability_max": 0.1},
146 |         )

149 |     def test_evaluations_shape_and_ordering(self) -> None:
150 |         data = self._run(seed=4, days=30)
151 |         rid = data["run_id"]
152 |         levels_rank = {"CAMPUS": 1, "BUILDING": 2, "FLOOR": 3, "SUITE": 4}
153 |         saw_a_firing_day = False
154 |         for n in range(31):
155 |             day = self.client.get(f"/api/run/{rid}/day/{n}").json()
156 |             evs = day["evaluations"]
157 |             if day["detection"]["fired"] and day["disclosure"] is not None:
158 |                 saw_a_firing_day = saw_a_firing_day or len(evs) > 0
159 |             order_key = [(levels_rank[e["level"]], -e["population"]) for e in evs]
160 |             self.assertEqual(
161 |                 order_key, sorted(order_key),
162 |                 "evaluations must be ordered coarsest -> finest",
163 |             )
164 |             for e in evs:
165 |                 self.assertEqual(
166 |                     set(e),
167 |                     {
168 |                         "scope_id", "label", "level", "population", "qualifying_band",
169 |                         "statistical_threshold", "statistical_pass", "min_population_pass",
170 |                         "report_fraction_pass", "privacy_pass", "eligible", "selected",
171 |                         "reason",
172 |                     },
173 |                 )
174 |                 self.assertNotIn("qualifying_reports", e)
175 |                 self.assertNotIn("report_fraction", e)
176 |                 self.assertRegex(e["qualifying_band"], _BAND_RE)
177 |         self.assertTrue(saw_a_firing_day)
178 | 
179 |     def test_exact_qualifying_count_never_appears_in_a_reason(self) -> None:
180 |         # Cross-check against the engine's own numbers, computed here.
181 |         from simulation.config import SimulationConfig
182 |         from simulation.pipeline import run_with_detection
183 | 
184 |         run = run_with_detection(seed=4, days=30, config=SimulationConfig())
185 |         data = self._run(seed=4, days=30)
186 |         rid = data["run_id"]
187 |         for record in run.daily_records:
188 |             if not record.disclosure_evaluations:
189 |                 continue
190 |             day = self.client.get(f"/api/run/{rid}/day/{record.day}").json()
191 |             api_reasons = " || ".join(e["reason"] for e in day["evaluations"])
192 |             for ev in record.disclosure_evaluations:
193 |                 exact = ev.qualifying_reports
194 |                 self.assertNotIn(f"({exact} qualifying reports", api_reasons)
195 |                 self.assertNotIn(f"qualifying count ({exact})", api_reasons)
196 | 
197 |     def test_slider_wall_data_exists_when_a_suite_fails_privacy(self) -> None:
198 |         data = self._run(seed=4, days=30, **_ROSTER_PARAMS)
199 |         rid = data["run_id"]
200 |         found_wall = False
201 |         for n in range(31):
202 |             evs = self.client.get(f"/api/run/{rid}/day/{n}").json()["evaluations"]
203 |             passed_then_failed = any(
204 |                 evs[i]["privacy_pass"] and not evs[j]["privacy_pass"]
205 |                 for i in range(len(evs))
206 |                 for j in range(i + 1, len(evs))
207 |             )
208 |             suite_roster_fail = [
209 |                 e for e in evs
210 |                 if e["level"] == "SUITE"
211 |                 and not e["privacy_pass"]
212 |                 and e["min_population_pass"]
213 |                 and not e["report_fraction_pass"]
214 |             ]
215 |             if passed_then_failed and suite_roster_fail:
216 |                 found_wall = True
217 |                 self.assertIn("over the limit", suite_roster_fail[0]["reason"])
218 |                 break
219 |         self.assertTrue(found_wall, "expected a day with a coarser pass then a suite privacy fail")
220 | 
221 |     def test_layout_endpoint_serves_prerun_structure(self) -> None:
222 |         res = self.client.get("/api/layout")
223 |         self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
224 |         data = res.json()
225 |         floors = data["layout"]["floors"]
226 |         self.assertEqual(len(floors), 2)
227 |         self.assertEqual(sum(len(f["suites"]) for f in floors), 6)
228 |         self.assertEqual(data["params"]["total_population"], 150)
229 |         self.assertIn("predicts nothing about any real building", data["disclaimer"])
230 | 
231 |     def test_layout_endpoint_honours_population_and_rejects_bad_values(self) -> None:
232 |         data = self.client.get("/api/layout?population=30").json()
233 |         self.assertEqual(data["params"]["total_population"], 180)
234 |         self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/api/layout?population=5").status_code, 422)
235 | 
236 |     def test_gates_endpoint_uses_the_real_threshold_function(self) -> None:
237 |         from microcluster.disclosure import statistical_threshold
238 | 
239 |         data = self.client.get("/api/gates").json()
240 |         rows = data["statistical"]["rows"]
241 |         self.assertEqual([r["population"] for r in rows], [25, 75, 150])
242 |         for r in rows:
243 |             self.assertEqual(r["required"], statistical_threshold(r["population"]))
244 |         self.assertEqual([r["required"] for r in rows], [5, 9, 13])
245 |         privacy = data["privacy"]
246 |         self.assertEqual(privacy["min_population"], 20)
247 |         self.assertEqual(privacy["max_fraction"], 0.5)
248 |         # 12 of 25 is exactly half (allowed); the 13th report is refused
249 |         self.assertEqual([r["refused_from"] for r in privacy["rows"]], [13, 38, 76])
250 |         self.assertTrue(all(r["meets_minimum"] for r in privacy["rows"]))
251 | 
252 |     def test_gate_constants_typed_in_the_html_match_the_config(self) -> None:
253 |         # the pages ship fallback text for the thresholds; gates.js overwrites
254 |         # it from /api/gates, but the fallback must not be able to drift
255 |         import re
256 |         from pathlib import Path
257 | 
258 |         from microcluster.config import DEFAULT_DISCLOSURE_CONFIG as cfg
259 | 
260 |         expected = {
261 |             "statistical.floor": str(cfg.statistical_gate_floor),
262 |             "privacy.min_population": str(cfg.min_scope_population),
263 |             "privacy.max_fraction": str(cfg.max_report_fraction),
264 |             "privacy.max_fraction_words": "half",
265 |         }
266 |         web = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "web"
267 |         for page in ("index.html", "privacy.html"):
268 |             html = (web / page).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
269 |             found = re.findall(r'data-gate-const="([^"]+)">([^<]*)<', html)
270 |             self.assertTrue(found, page)
271 |             for key, text in found:
272 |                 self.assertEqual(text, expected[key], f"{page}: {key}")
273 |

275 |         res = self.client.get("/")
276 |         self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
277 |         self.assertIn("outbreak", res.text.lower())

281 |             res = self.client.get(f"/{page}")
282 |             self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200, page)
283 |             self.assertIn(needle, res.text.lower())

293 |         self.assertEqual(set(bench["in_distribution"]), {"rules", "model"})

tests/test_bands.py

 1 | """api.bands: count banding and reason sanitisation.
 2 | 
 3 | The differencing-attack defence (CLAUDE.md rule 3 for the UI): an exact
 4 | qualifying-report count must never leave the API, in a field or embedded
 5 | in a reason string.
 6 | """
 7 | 
 8 | from __future__ import annotations
 9 | 
10 | import unittest
11 | 
12 | from api.bands import BAND_LOWER_BOUNDS, band, band_interval, sanitize_reason
13 | 
14 | 
15 | class BandTest(unittest.TestCase):
16 |     def test_zero_and_small_bands(self) -> None:
17 |         self.assertEqual(band(0), "0")
18 |         self.assertEqual(band(1), "1-4")
19 |         self.assertEqual(band(4), "1-4")
20 |         self.assertEqual(band(5), "5-9")
21 |         self.assertEqual(band(9), "5-9")
22 |         self.assertEqual(band(10), "10-19")
23 |         self.assertEqual(band(19), "10-19")
24 | 
25 |     def test_open_top_band(self) -> None:
26 |         top = BAND_LOWER_BOUNDS[-1]
27 |         self.assertEqual(band(top), f"{top}+")
28 |         self.assertEqual(band(top + 1000), f"{top}+")
29 | 
30 |     def test_every_count_maps_somewhere_and_hides_plus_or_minus_one(self) -> None:
31 |         # No two counts one apart are ever distinguishable unless they
32 |         # straddle a band edge -- and even then only "which side", never
33 |         # the value. Concretely: many consecutive counts share a label.
34 |         for lo in BAND_LOWER_BOUNDS[:-1]:
35 |             self.assertEqual(band(lo), band(lo + 1))
36 | 
37 |     def test_negative_is_zero(self) -> None:
38 |         self.assertEqual(band(-3), "0")
39 | 
40 |     def test_band_interval_inverts_band(self) -> None:
41 |         self.assertEqual(band_interval("0"), (0, 0))
42 |         self.assertEqual(band_interval("1-4"), (1, 4))
43 |         self.assertEqual(band_interval("10-19"), (10, 19))
44 |         top = BAND_LOWER_BOUNDS[-1]
45 |         self.assertEqual(band_interval(f"{top}+"), (top, None))
46 |         for c in (1, 4, 5, 12, 40, 99):
47 |             lo, hi = band_interval(band(c))
48 |             self.assertLessEqual(lo, c)
49 |             self.assertGreaterEqual(hi, c)
50 | 
51 | 
52 | class SanitizeReasonTest(unittest.TestCase):
53 |     def test_statistical_failure_count_becomes_a_band(self) -> None:
54 |         out = sanitize_reason(
55 |             "Rejected: statistical gate failed (7 qualifying reports < required 45 for n=2000)"
56 |         )
57 |         self.assertNotIn("(7 qualifying", out)
58 |         self.assertIn("(5-9 qualifying reports", out)
59 |         self.assertIn("required 45", out)  # threshold untouched
60 |         self.assertIn("n=2000", out)       # declared population untouched
61 | 
62 |     def test_report_fraction_is_blurred(self) -> None:
63 |         out = sanitize_reason(
64 |             "Rejected: report fraction 0.60 exceeds maximum 0.50 (roster)"
65 |         )
66 |         self.assertNotIn("0.60", out)          # the exact fraction is gone
67 |         self.assertIn("over the limit of 0.50", out)  # the policy max stays
68 |         self.assertNotIn("exceeds maximum", out)      # no redundant phrasing
69 | 
70 |     def test_stability_note_counts_become_bands(self) -> None:
71 |         out = sanitize_reason(
72 |             "Disclosed: qualifying count (14) exceeded the previously disclosed "
73 |             "scope's (8) by more than the switch margin (5); disclosure moved here."
74 |         )
75 |         self.assertIn("qualifying count (10-19)", out)
76 |         self.assertIn("scope's (5-9)", out)
77 |         self.assertIn("switch margin (5)", out)  # public policy number, kept
78 | 
79 |     def test_reason_without_a_count_is_unchanged(self) -> None:
80 |         original = "Rejected: population n=18 below minimum privacy scope (20)"
81 |         self.assertEqual(sanitize_reason(original), original)
82 | 
83 | 
84 | if __name__ == "__main__":
85 |     unittest.main()

tests/test_benchmark.py

  1 | """Benchmark tests.
  2 | 
  3 | The dataset builder and the scoring logic are exercised on small, short
  4 | simulated runs -- enough seeds for both label classes to appear, few
  5 | enough to stay fast. The cross-regime harness is run once at a tiny size
  6 | to prove it wires together.
  7 | """
  8 | 
  9 | from __future__ import annotations
 10 | 
 11 | import unittest
 12 | 
 13 | from simulation.benchmark import (
 14 |     FEATURE_NAMES,
 15 |     DayRow,
 16 |     benchmark_cross_regime,
 17 |     build_dataset,
 18 |     score_model,
 19 |     score_rows,
 20 |     score_rules,
 21 |     split_by_run,
 22 |     train_model,
 23 | )
 24 | 
 25 | 
 26 | class DatasetShapeTest(unittest.TestCase):
 27 |     def test_one_row_per_run_day_including_day_zero(self) -> None:
 28 |         rows = build_dataset([1, 2, 3], days=10)
 29 |         self.assertEqual(len(rows), 3 * 11)
 30 |         for seed in (1, 2, 3):
 31 |             days = sorted(r.day for r in rows if r.seed == seed)
 32 |             self.assertEqual(days, list(range(11)))
 33 | 
 34 |     def test_features_and_label_are_well_formed(self) -> None:
 35 |         for r in build_dataset([1, 2], days=10):
 36 |             self.assertIn(r.label, (0, 1))
 37 |             self.assertIsInstance(r.rule_fired, bool)
 38 |             self.assertGreaterEqual(r.relative_excess_ratio, 0.0)
 39 |             self.assertGreaterEqual(r.count_within_window, 0)
 40 |             self.assertGreaterEqual(r.count_within_window, r.count_sharing_category)
 41 | 
 42 |     def test_three_named_features(self) -> None:
 43 |         self.assertEqual(
 44 |             FEATURE_NAMES,
 45 |             ("relative_excess_ratio", "count_within_window", "count_sharing_category"),
 46 |         )
 47 | 
 48 |     def test_deterministic(self) -> None:
 49 |         self.assertEqual(build_dataset([1, 2], days=8), build_dataset([1, 2], days=8))
 50 | 
 51 |     def test_day_zero_is_never_an_established_outbreak(self) -> None:
 52 |         for r in build_dataset([1, 2, 3, 4, 5], days=6):
 53 |             if r.day == 0:
 54 |                 self.assertEqual(r.label, 0)
 55 | 
 56 | 
 57 | class SplitByRunTest(unittest.TestCase):
 58 |     def test_no_seed_in_both_halves(self) -> None:
 59 |         rows = build_dataset(list(range(1, 11)), days=6)
 60 |         train, test = split_by_run(rows)
 61 |         self.assertTrue(train and test)
 62 |         self.assertEqual(
 63 |             set(r.seed for r in train) & set(r.seed for r in test), set()
 64 |         )
 65 | 
 66 |     def test_stable_given_the_split_seed(self) -> None:
 67 |         rows = build_dataset(list(range(1, 11)), days=6)
 68 |         a = split_by_run(rows, split_seed=99)
 69 |         b = split_by_run(rows, split_seed=99)
 70 |         self.assertEqual([r.seed for r in a[1]], [r.seed for r in b[1]])
 71 | 
 72 | 
 73 | class TrainingTest(unittest.TestCase):
 74 |     def test_single_class_training_set_is_rejected(self) -> None:
 75 |         rows = [DayRow(1, d, 0.0, 0, 0, 0, False) for d in range(5)]
 76 |         with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
 77 |             train_model(rows, kind="logistic")
 78 | 
 79 |     def test_unknown_kind_is_rejected(self) -> None:
 80 |         rows = build_dataset([1, 2, 3, 4], days=12)
 81 |         with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
 82 |             train_model(rows, kind="forest")
 83 | 
 84 |     def test_both_model_kinds_fit_and_score(self) -> None:
 85 |         rows = build_dataset(list(range(1, 13)), days=15)
 86 |         train, test = split_by_run(rows)
 87 |         for kind in ("logistic", "tree"):
 88 |             model = train_model(train, kind=kind)
 89 |             score = score_model(model, test, label=kind)
 90 |             self.assertGreaterEqual(score.precision, 0.0)
 91 |             self.assertLessEqual(score.precision, 1.0)
 92 |             self.assertGreaterEqual(score.recall, 0.0)
 93 |             self.assertLessEqual(score.recall, 1.0)
 94 | 
 95 | 
 96 | class ScoreRowsTest(unittest.TestCase):
 97 |     def _rows(self) -> list[DayRow]:
 98 |         # Seed 1: outbreak days 2..4. Seed 2: no outbreak.
 99 |         return [
100 |             DayRow(1, 0, 0.0, 0, 0, 0, False),
101 |             DayRow(1, 1, 0.0, 0, 0, 0, False),
102 |             DayRow(1, 2, 0.0, 0, 0, 1, False),
103 |             DayRow(1, 3, 0.0, 0, 0, 1, False),
104 |             DayRow(1, 4, 0.0, 0, 0, 1, False),
105 |             DayRow(2, 0, 0.0, 0, 0, 0, False),
106 |             DayRow(2, 1, 0.0, 0, 0, 0, False),
107 |         ]
108 | 
109 |     def test_perfect_prediction(self) -> None:
110 |         rows = self._rows()
111 |         preds = [r.label for r in rows]
112 |         s = score_rows(rows, preds, label="perfect")
113 |         self.assertEqual((s.precision, s.recall), (1.0, 1.0))
114 |         self.assertEqual(s.mean_detection_delay, 0.0)
115 |         self.assertEqual((s.n_runs_with_outbreak, s.n_runs_detected), (1, 1))
116 | 
117 |     def test_late_and_missed_detection(self) -> None:
118 |         rows = self._rows()
119 |         # fire only on day 4 for seed 1 -> delay 2; never for seed 2 (fine).
120 |         preds = [1 if (r.seed == 1 and r.day == 4) else 0 for r in rows]
121 |         s = score_rows(rows, preds, label="late")
122 |         self.assertEqual(s.mean_detection_delay, 2.0)
123 |         self.assertEqual(s.never_detected_runs, 0)
124 | 
125 |     def test_never_detected_run_is_counted(self) -> None:
126 |         rows = self._rows()
127 |         s = score_rows(rows, [0] * len(rows), label="silent")
128 |         self.assertEqual(s.never_detected_runs, 1)
129 |         self.assertIsNone(s.mean_detection_delay)
130 |         self.assertEqual(s.recall, 0.0)
131 | 
132 |     def test_score_rules_uses_the_rule_fired_flag(self) -> None:
133 |         rows = [
134 |             DayRow(1, 0, 0.0, 0, 0, 0, False),
135 |             DayRow(1, 1, 0.0, 0, 0, 1, True),
136 |         ]
137 |         s = score_rules(rows)
138 |         self.assertEqual(s.n_predicted_positive, 1)
139 |         self.assertEqual(s.recall, 1.0)
140 | 
141 | 
142 | class CrossRegimeHarnessTest(unittest.TestCase):
143 |     def test_runs_end_to_end_at_a_tiny_size(self) -> None:
144 |         results = benchmark_cross_regime(
145 |             train_seeds=list(range(1, 11)),
146 |             test_seeds=list(range(11, 17)),
147 |             days=15,
148 |             test_suite_transmissions=(0.012,),
149 |         )
150 |         self.assertEqual(len(results), 1)
151 |         res = results[0]
152 |         self.assertEqual(len(res.scores), 3)
153 |         self.assertEqual(res.scores[0].label, "authored rules")
154 |         self.assertGreater(res.n_test_rows, 0)

tests/test_detection.py

  1 | """Detection engine tests -- independent of the disclosure engine."""
  2 | 
  3 | from __future__ import annotations
  4 | 
  5 | import math
  6 | import unittest
  7 | 
  8 | from microcluster import detection
  9 | from microcluster.config import DetectionConfig

 15 | 
 16 | class LocalizedClusterTest(unittest.TestCase):
 17 |     """A burst in one suite: the relative detector fires, the absolute
 18 |     detector does not."""
 19 | 
 20 |     def setUp(self) -> None:
 21 |         self.reg = standard_registry()
 22 |         # All reports at suite level (see the mixed-level assumption in
 23 |         # detection.evaluate): the cluster in S1, scattered peers in the
 24 |         # sibling suites.
 25 |         self.reports = (
 26 |             reports("S1", 9, category=Category.RESPIRATORY)
 27 |             + reports("S2", 2, category=Category.GENERAL)
 28 |             + reports("S3", 1, category=Category.GASTROINTESTINAL)
 29 |             + reports("S4", 1, category=Category.OTHER)
 30 |             + reports("S5", 1, category=Category.GENERAL)
 31 |             # Old reports in the same suite: outside the 72h window, ignored.
 32 |             + reports("S1", 6, category=Category.RESPIRATORY, hours_ago=500)
 33 |         )
 34 |         self.result = detection.evaluate(self.reports, self.reg, now=NOW)
 35 | 
 36 |     def test_relative_fires_absolute_does_not(self) -> None:
 37 |         self.assertTrue(self.result.relative_detector_fired)
 38 |         self.assertFalse(self.result.absolute_detector_fired)
 39 |         self.assertTrue(self.result.fired)
 40 | 
 41 |     def test_three_findings_reported_plainly(self) -> None:
 42 |         f = self.result.findings
 43 |         self.assertEqual(f.count_within_window, 14)
 44 |         self.assertEqual(f.count_sharing_category, 9)
 45 |         self.assertGreater(f.relative_excess_ratio, 2.0)
 46 | 
 47 |     def test_hotspot_and_dominant_category(self) -> None:
 48 |         self.assertEqual(self.result.hotspot_location_id, "S1")
 49 |         self.assertEqual(self.result.dominant_category, Category.RESPIRATORY)
 50 | 
 51 |     def test_absolute_expectation_is_from_background_rate(self) -> None:
 52 |         # 0.02 * 2000 declared campus population.
 53 |         self.assertAlmostEqual(self.result.absolute_expected, 40.0)
 54 | 
 55 |     def test_aggregate_for_disclosure_is_per_scope_counts_only(self) -> None:
 56 |         self.assertEqual(
 57 |             self.result.qualifying_scope_counts,
 58 |             {"S1": 9, "F1": 9, "B1": 9, "C": 9},
 59 |         )

 61 | 
 62 | class CampusWideRiseTest(unittest.TestCase):
 63 |     """An even rise across every building: the absolute detector fires,
 64 |     the relative detector does not (no location stands out)."""
 65 | 
 66 |     def setUp(self) -> None:
 67 |         self.reg = standard_registry()
 68 |         self.reports = []
 69 |         for b in ("B1", "B2", "B3", "B4", "B5"):
 70 |             self.reports += reports(b, 14, category=Category.RESPIRATORY)
 71 |         self.result = detection.evaluate(self.reports, self.reg, now=NOW)
 72 | 
 73 |     def test_absolute_fires_relative_does_not(self) -> None:
 74 |         self.assertTrue(self.result.absolute_detector_fired)
 75 |         self.assertFalse(self.result.relative_detector_fired)
 76 |         self.assertTrue(self.result.fired)
 77 | 
 78 |     def test_relative_excess_ratio_is_about_one(self) -> None:
 79 |         # Every building reports at the same per-capita rate, so no
 80 |         # location stands out: the smoothed ratio sits close to 1.
 81 |         ratio = self.result.findings.relative_excess_ratio
 82 |         self.assertGreater(ratio, 0.9)
 83 |         self.assertLess(ratio, 1.15)
 84 | 
 85 |     def test_counts(self) -> None:
 86 |         self.assertEqual(self.result.findings.count_within_window, 70)
 87 |         self.assertEqual(self.result.findings.count_sharing_category, 70)
 88 |         self.assertEqual(self.result.qualifying_scope_counts["C"], 70)
 89 |         for b in ("B1", "B2", "B3", "B4", "B5"):
 90 |             self.assertEqual(self.result.qualifying_scope_counts[b], 14)

 93 | class ScatteredNoiseTest(unittest.TestCase):
 94 |     """Low volume, spread across locations and categories: neither
 95 |     detector fires."""
 96 | 
 97 |     def setUp(self) -> None:
 98 |         self.reg = standard_registry()
 99 |         # Uniform building level; three reports per building, no category
100 |         # concentrated anywhere.

102 |             reports("B1", 3, category=Category.GENERAL)
103 |             + reports("B2", 3, category=Category.RESPIRATORY)
104 |             + reports("B3", 3, category=Category.GASTROINTESTINAL)
105 |             + reports("B4", 3, category=Category.OTHER)
106 |             + reports("B5", 3, category=Category.GENERAL)
107 |         )
108 |         self.result = detection.evaluate(self.reports, self.reg, now=NOW)
109 |

111 |         self.assertFalse(self.result.relative_detector_fired)

116 | class DetectorEdgeCaseTest(unittest.TestCase):
117 |     def test_zero_reports(self) -> None:
118 |         reg = standard_registry()
119 |         result = detection.evaluate([], reg, now=NOW)
120 |         self.assertFalse(result.fired)
121 |         self.assertFalse(result.relative_detector_fired)
122 |         self.assertFalse(result.absolute_detector_fired)
123 |         self.assertIsNone(result.dominant_category)
124 |         self.assertIsNone(result.hotspot_location_id)
125 |         self.assertEqual(result.findings.count_within_window, 0)
126 |         self.assertEqual(result.findings.count_sharing_category, 0)
127 |         self.assertEqual(result.findings.relative_excess_ratio, 0.0)
128 |         self.assertEqual(result.qualifying_scope_counts, {})
129 | 
130 |     def test_reports_outside_window_are_excluded(self) -> None:
131 |         reg = standard_registry()
132 |         old = reports("S1", 9, category=Category.RESPIRATORY, hours_ago=200)
133 |         result = detection.evaluate(old, reg, now=NOW)
134 |         self.assertEqual(result.findings.count_within_window, 0)
135 |         self.assertIsNone(result.dominant_category)
136 |         self.assertFalse(result.fired)
137 |         self.assertEqual(result.qualifying_scope_counts, {})
138 | 
139 |     def test_relative_detector_has_a_minimum_cluster_size(self) -> None:
140 |         # Two reports in a tiny suite produce a huge ratio but must not
141 |         # fire -- MIN_RELATIVE_CLUSTER_REPORTS is 3.
142 |         reg = standard_registry()
143 |         result = detection.evaluate(
144 |             reports("S1", 2, category=Category.RESPIRATORY), reg, now=NOW
145 |         )
146 |         self.assertGreater(result.findings.relative_excess_ratio, 2.0)
147 |         self.assertFalse(result.relative_detector_fired)

149 |     def test_location_that_is_the_whole_campus_cannot_fire_relative(self) -> None:
150 |         reg = standard_registry()

152 |             reports("C", 50, category=Category.RESPIRATORY), reg, now=NOW
153 |         )
154 |         # No peers to compare against -> relative ratio stays 0, not inf.
155 |         self.assertEqual(result.findings.relative_excess_ratio, 0.0)
156 |         self.assertFalse(result.relative_detector_fired)
157 |         # Absolute still works: 50 >= 1.5 * (0.02 * 2000) = 60 is False here.
158 |         self.assertFalse(result.absolute_detector_fired)
159 | 
160 |     def test_unknown_location_is_rejected(self) -> None:
161 |         reg = standard_registry()
162 |         bad = [report("does-not-exist")]
163 |         with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
164 |             detection.evaluate(bad, reg, now=NOW)
165 | 
166 |     def test_relative_ratio_stays_finite_when_peers_are_silent(self) -> None:
167 |         # Smoothing keeps the ratio finite even when the control rate
168 |         # would otherwise be exactly zero.
169 |         reg = standard_registry()
170 |         result = detection.evaluate(
171 |             reports("S1", 5, category=Category.RESPIRATORY), reg, now=NOW
172 |         )
173 |         ratio = result.findings.relative_excess_ratio
174 |         self.assertTrue(math.isfinite(ratio))
175 |         self.assertGreater(ratio, 2.0)
176 |         self.assertTrue(result.relative_detector_fired)
177 | 
178 |     def test_smoothing_constant_damps_the_ratio(self) -> None:
179 |         # Same data, larger smoothing constant -> ratio pulled toward 1.
180 |         reg = standard_registry()
181 |         data = reports("S1", 9, category=Category.RESPIRATORY)
182 |         light = detection.evaluate(
183 |             data, reg, now=NOW, config=DetectionConfig(relative_smoothing=1.0)
184 |         ).findings.relative_excess_ratio
185 |         heavy = detection.evaluate(
186 |             data, reg, now=NOW, config=DetectionConfig(relative_smoothing=25.0)
187 |         ).findings.relative_excess_ratio
188 |         self.assertLess(heavy, light)
189 |

191 | class AbsoluteDetectorCategoryScopingTest(unittest.TestCase):
192 |     """The absolute detector counts only dominant-category reports, so a
193 |     spike made of several unrelated illnesses does not fire it."""
194 | 
195 |     def test_mixed_category_volume_spike_does_not_fire_absolute(self) -> None:
196 |         reg = standard_registry()
197 |         # 70 within-window reports campus-wide -- above the raw-volume
198 |         # threshold of 1.5 * (0.02 * 2000) = 60 -- but split evenly
199 |         # between two unrelated categories, so the dominant-category
200 |         # count is only 35.
201 |         data = []
202 |         for b in ("B1", "B2", "B3", "B4", "B5"):
203 |             data += reports(b, 7, category=Category.RESPIRATORY)
204 |             data += reports(b, 7, category=Category.GASTROINTESTINAL)
205 |         result = detection.evaluate(data, reg, now=NOW)
206 | 
207 |         self.assertEqual(result.findings.count_within_window, 70)
208 |         self.assertFalse(result.absolute_detector_fired)
209 |         self.assertFalse(result.relative_detector_fired)
210 |         self.assertFalse(result.fired)

213 |         reg = standard_registry()

215 |         for b in ("B1", "B2", "B3", "B4", "B5"):
216 |             data += reports(b, 14, category=Category.RESPIRATORY)
217 |         result = detection.evaluate(data, reg, now=NOW)
218 |

220 |         self.assertTrue(result.absolute_detector_fired)

284 | class HysteresisTest(unittest.TestCase):
285 |     """Detection hysteresis (config.HYSTERESIS_MIN_FIRED_DAYS /
286 |     HYSTERESIS_RELEASE_THRESHOLD), threaded via HysteresisState across a
287 |     sequence of evaluate() calls.
288 | 
289 |     A campus-only registry isolates these to the absolute detector alone:
290 |     with a single scope, the relative detector has no peers and can never
291 |     fire (see DetectorEdgeCaseTest), so ``raw_fired`` here is exactly
292 |     ``absolute_detector_fired``, driven entirely by how many reports we
293 |     put in the window.
294 |     """
295 | 
296 |     def setUp(self) -> None:
297 |         self.reg = single_scope_registry(2000)  # expected = 0.02*2000 = 40
298 |         self.config = DetectionConfig(
299 |             background_rate=0.02,
300 |             absolute_excess_ratio=1.5,  # fires at >= 60
301 |             hysteresis_min_fired_days=3,
302 |             hysteresis_release_threshold=3,
303 |         )
304 | 
305 |     def _evaluate(self, count: int, state: HysteresisState | None):
306 |         data = reports("C", count, category=Category.RESPIRATORY, hours_ago=0, now=NOW)
307 |         return detection.evaluate(
308 |             data, self.reg, now=NOW, config=self.config, hysteresis_state=state
309 |         )
310 | 
311 |     def test_single_call_with_no_state_is_unaffected_by_hysteresis(self) -> None:
312 |         # 70 >= 60 -> raw fires; no prior state -> fired == raw_fired.
313 |         fired_result = self._evaluate(70, None)
314 |         self.assertTrue(fired_result.raw_fired)
315 |         self.assertEqual(fired_result.fired, fired_result.raw_fired)
316 | 
317 |         # 10 < 60 -> raw does not fire; still no prior state -> False.
318 |         quiet_result = self._evaluate(10, None)
319 |         self.assertFalse(quiet_result.raw_fired)
320 |         self.assertEqual(quiet_result.fired, quiet_result.raw_fired)
321 | 
322 |     def test_quiet_stretch_with_no_prior_activation_never_fires(self) -> None:
323 |         state = None
324 |         for _ in range(4):
325 |             result = self._evaluate(5, state)
326 |             self.assertFalse(result.fired)
327 |             self.assertFalse(result.hysteresis_state.active)
328 |             state = result.hysteresis_state
329 | 
330 |     def test_hold_continues_while_raw_still_fires(self) -> None:
331 |         state = None
332 |         result = self._evaluate(70, state)  # raw fires
333 |         self.assertTrue(result.fired)
334 |         self.assertEqual(result.hysteresis_state, HysteresisState(active=True, consecutive_days=1))
335 | 
336 |         state = result.hysteresis_state
337 |         result = self._evaluate(65, state)  # still raw-fires
338 |         self.assertTrue(result.fired)
339 |         self.assertEqual(result.hysteresis_state, HysteresisState(active=True, consecutive_days=2))
340 | 
341 |     def test_hold_survives_a_raw_dip_until_the_minimum_is_served(self) -> None:
342 |         # Day 0: raw fires (70 >= 60).
343 |         state = None
344 |         result = self._evaluate(70, state)
345 |         self.assertTrue(result.fired)
346 |         state = result.hysteresis_state
347 | 
348 |         # Day 1: raw fires again (65 >= 60); streak now 2.
349 |         result = self._evaluate(65, state)
350 |         self.assertTrue(result.fired)
351 |         self.assertEqual(result.hysteresis_state.consecutive_days, 2)
352 |         state = result.hysteresis_state
353 | 
354 |         # Day 2: raw does NOT fire (50 < 60), but the window is nowhere
355 |         # near the release threshold (3) and the minimum hold (3 days)
356 |         # has not yet been served (streak is only 2) -> HELD fired=True.
357 |         result = self._evaluate(50, state)
358 |         self.assertFalse(result.raw_fired)
359 |         self.assertTrue(result.fired, "hysteresis should have held fired=True")
360 |         self.assertEqual(result.hysteresis_state, HysteresisState(active=True, consecutive_days=3))
361 |         state = result.hysteresis_state
362 | 
363 |         # Day 3: raw still does not fire (45 < 60); the minimum (3) has
364 |         # now been served -> releases on schedule.
365 |         result = self._evaluate(45, state)
366 |         self.assertFalse(result.raw_fired)
367 |         self.assertFalse(result.fired, "hysteresis should have released on schedule")
368 |         self.assertEqual(result.hysteresis_state, HysteresisState(active=False, consecutive_days=0))
369 | 
370 |     def test_release_threshold_overrides_the_minimum_hold(self) -> None:
371 |         # Day 0: raw fires (70 >= 60); streak = 1, well under the minimum.
372 |         state = None
373 |         result = self._evaluate(70, state)
374 |         self.assertTrue(result.fired)
375 |         state = result.hysteresis_state
376 | 
377 |         # Day 1: the window has nearly emptied out (2 <= release threshold
378 |         # of 3) -- releases IMMEDIATELY, even though the minimum hold (3)
379 |         # was never served.
380 |         result = self._evaluate(2, state)
381 |         self.assertFalse(result.raw_fired)
382 |         self.assertFalse(result.fired, "a near-empty window should release early")
383 |         self.assertEqual(result.hysteresis_state, HysteresisState(active=False, consecutive_days=0))
384 | 
385 |     def test_a_fresh_raw_fire_restarts_the_streak_at_one(self) -> None:
386 |         state = HysteresisState(active=False, consecutive_days=0)
387 |         result = self._evaluate(70, state)
388 |         self.assertEqual(result.hysteresis_state, HysteresisState(active=True, consecutive_days=1))
389 |

392 |     unittest.main()

tests/test_disclosure.py

  1 | """Disclosure engine tests -- independent of the detection engine.

  3 | The disclosure engine is fed aggregate per-scope counts directly, exactly
  4 | as the detection engine would hand them over (rule 10).

 23 | class StatisticalThresholdTest(unittest.TestCase):
 24 |     def test_threshold_scales_up_with_population(self) -> None:
 25 |         expected = {12: 5, 18: 5, 40: 7, 100: 10, 500: 23, 2000: 45}
 26 |         for n, threshold in expected.items():
 27 |             self.assertEqual(disclosure.statistical_threshold(n), threshold, msg=f"n={n}")
 28 | 
 29 |     def test_floor_applies_to_small_populations(self) -> None:
 30 |         # ceil(sqrt(4)) == 2, but the floor is 5.
 31 |         self.assertEqual(disclosure.statistical_threshold(4), 5)
 32 | 
 33 |     def test_perfect_squares_are_exact(self) -> None:
 34 |         self.assertEqual(disclosure.statistical_threshold(10_000), 100)

 37 | class BothGatesAcrossPopulationsTest(unittest.TestCase):
 38 |     """n in {12, 18, 40, 100, 500, 2000}: the statistical threshold rises
 39 |     with n, while the privacy minimum rejects the two smallest scopes."""
 40 | 
 41 |     POPULATIONS = (12, 18, 40, 100, 500, 2000)
 42 |     STAT_THRESHOLD = {12: 5, 18: 5, 40: 7, 100: 10, 500: 23, 2000: 45}
 43 | 
 44 |     def test_qualifying_at_exactly_the_statistical_threshold(self) -> None:
 45 |         for n in self.POPULATIONS:
 46 |             q = self.STAT_THRESHOLD[n]
 47 |             result = disclosure.evaluate(single_scope_registry(n), {"C": q})
 48 |             ev = result.evaluations[0]
 49 |             with self.subTest(n=n):
 50 |                 self.assertEqual(ev.statistical_threshold, q)
 51 |                 self.assertTrue(ev.statistical_pass)
 52 |                 # Privacy minimum (20) rejects n=12 and n=18.
 53 |                 if n < 20:
 54 |                     self.assertFalse(ev.min_population_pass)
 55 |                     self.assertFalse(ev.privacy_pass)
 56 |                     self.assertFalse(ev.disclosure_eligible)
 57 |                     self.assertIsNone(result.disclosed_scope_id)
 58 |                     self.assertIn("below minimum privacy scope", ev.disclosure_reason)
 59 |                 else:
 60 |                     self.assertTrue(ev.min_population_pass)
 61 |                     self.assertTrue(ev.report_fraction_pass)
 62 |                     self.assertTrue(ev.privacy_pass)
 63 |                     self.assertTrue(ev.disclosure_eligible)
 64 |                     self.assertEqual(result.disclosed_scope_id, "C")
 65 | 
 66 |     def test_report_fraction_gate_rejects_a_dense_small_scope(self) -> None:
 67 |         # n=40, 25 qualifying reports: statistical gate passes (25 >= 7),
 68 |         # but 25/40 = 0.625 > MAX_REPORT_FRACTION (0.5).
 69 |         result = disclosure.evaluate(single_scope_registry(40), {"C": 25})
 70 |         ev = result.evaluations[0]
 71 |         self.assertTrue(ev.statistical_pass)
 72 |         self.assertTrue(ev.min_population_pass)
 73 |         self.assertFalse(ev.report_fraction_pass)
 74 |         self.assertFalse(ev.privacy_pass)
 75 |         self.assertFalse(ev.disclosure_eligible)
 76 |         self.assertIsNone(result.disclosed_scope_id)
 77 |         self.assertIn("report fraction", ev.disclosure_reason)
 78 | 
 79 |     def test_gates_pull_in_opposite_directions(self) -> None:
 80 |         # Same qualifying count (10). Small scope fails statistics-free but
 81 |         # privacy; large scope fails statistics. Only the middle passes.
 82 |         small = disclosure.evaluate(single_scope_registry(15), {"C": 10}).evaluations[0]
 83 |         mid = disclosure.evaluate(single_scope_registry(60), {"C": 10}).evaluations[0]
 84 |         large = disclosure.evaluate(single_scope_registry(400), {"C": 10}).evaluations[0]
 85 | 
 86 |         self.assertFalse(small.privacy_pass)      # n=15 < 20
 87 |         self.assertTrue(small.statistical_pass)   # 10 >= 5
 88 | 
 89 |         self.assertTrue(mid.disclosure_eligible)  # 10 >= 8 and privacy ok
 90 | 
 91 |         self.assertFalse(large.statistical_pass)  # 10 < 20
 92 |         self.assertTrue(large.privacy_pass)       # n=400, fraction tiny

113 | class PrivacyRefusalTest(unittest.TestCase):
114 |     """5 qualifying reports, floor n=18: statistical gate passes (5 >= 5)
115 |     but the privacy gate fails (18 < 20). A coarser scope is disclosed."""
116 |

242 | class ScopeStabilityTest(unittest.TestCase):
243 |     """config.SIBLING_SWITCH_MARGIN: once a scope has been disclosed,
244 |     evaluate() prefers to keep disclosing it (or a coarser ancestor of it)
245 |     across a SEQUENCE of calls, threaded via ``prior_disclosed_scope_id``.
246 |     A single call with no prior id is unaffected (all other disclosure
247 |     tests exercise exactly that default path).
248 |     """
249 | 
250 |     def setUp(self) -> None:
251 |         self.reg = standard_registry()  # S1, S2 are sibling suites under F1
252 | 
253 |     def test_stays_at_the_prior_scope_when_a_sibling_is_within_the_margin(self) -> None:
254 |         # S2's count (11) exceeds S1's (6) by only 5 -- not MORE than the
255 |         # margin (5) -- so disclosure must stay at S1.
256 |         counts = {"S1": 6, "S2": 11, "F1": 6, "B1": 6, "C": 6}
257 |         result = disclosure.evaluate(self.reg, counts, prior_disclosed_scope_id="S1")
258 | 
259 |         self.assertEqual(result.disclosed_scope_id, "S1")
260 |         s1 = next(e for e in result.evaluations if e.scope_id == "S1")
261 |         self.assertIn("kept disclosing the same scope", s1.disclosure_reason)
262 | 
263 |     def test_switches_only_once_a_sibling_clears_the_margin(self) -> None:
264 |         # S2's count (12) exceeds S1's (6) by 6 -- MORE than the margin
265 |         # (5) -- so disclosure is allowed to move.
266 |         counts = {"S1": 6, "S2": 12, "F1": 6, "B1": 6, "C": 6}
267 |         result = disclosure.evaluate(self.reg, counts, prior_disclosed_scope_id="S1")
268 | 
269 |         self.assertEqual(result.disclosed_scope_id, "S2")
270 |         s2 = next(e for e in result.evaluations if e.scope_id == "S2")
271 |         self.assertIn("exceeded the previously disclosed scope", s2.disclosure_reason)
272 |         self.assertIn("switch margin", s2.disclosure_reason)
273 | 
274 |     def test_never_silently_hops_between_siblings_across_a_sequence(self) -> None:
275 |         # Simulate three consecutive days. Day 1 names S1. Day 2, S2 briefly
276 |         # edges ahead but not past the margin -- must NOT switch. Day 3, S2
277 |         # pulls decisively ahead -- switching is now allowed.
278 |         day1 = disclosure.evaluate(
279 |             self.reg, {"S1": 6, "F1": 6, "B1": 6, "C": 6}, prior_disclosed_scope_id=None
280 |         )
281 |         self.assertEqual(day1.disclosed_scope_id, "S1")
282 | 
283 |         day2 = disclosure.evaluate(
284 |             self.reg,
285 |             {"S1": 6, "S2": 10, "F1": 6, "B1": 6, "C": 6},
286 |             prior_disclosed_scope_id=day1.disclosed_scope_id,
287 |         )
288 |         self.assertEqual(day2.disclosed_scope_id, "S1")
289 | 
290 |         day3 = disclosure.evaluate(
291 |             self.reg,
292 |             {"S1": 6, "S2": 12, "F1": 6, "B1": 6, "C": 6},
293 |             prior_disclosed_scope_id=day2.disclosed_scope_id,
294 |         )
295 |         self.assertEqual(day3.disclosed_scope_id, "S2")
296 | 
297 |     def test_falls_back_to_a_coarser_ancestor_when_the_prior_scope_drops_out(self) -> None:
298 |         # S1 no longer clears its own statistical gate; its ancestor F1
299 |         # does. A different, unrelated suite (S2) is ALSO eligible with a
300 |         # smaller count than F1 -- disclosure must still prefer F1 (S1's
301 |         # own ancestor) over hopping to the unrelated sibling S2.
302 |         counts = {"S1": 3, "S2": 8, "F1": 15, "B1": 15, "C": 15}
303 |         result = disclosure.evaluate(self.reg, counts, prior_disclosed_scope_id="S1")
304 | 
305 |         self.assertEqual(result.disclosed_scope_id, "F1")
306 |         f1 = next(e for e in result.evaluations if e.scope_id == "F1")
307 |         self.assertIn("no longer qualifies", f1.disclosure_reason)
308 |         self.assertIn("coarser ancestor", f1.disclosure_reason)
309 | 
310 |     def test_no_prior_scope_ignores_stability_entirely(self) -> None:
311 |         # Same counts as the "switches" test, but with no prior id: plain
312 |         # finest-eligible selection (rule 9) -- S1 and S2 are both SUITE
313 |         # level with the same declared population, so the qualifying count
314 |         # is irrelevant to the tie-break; S1 wins on scope id alone,
315 |         # exactly as every other disclosure test in this module exercises.
316 |         counts = {"S1": 6, "S2": 12, "F1": 6, "B1": 6, "C": 6}
317 |         result = disclosure.evaluate(self.reg, counts)
318 |         self.assertEqual(result.disclosed_scope_id, "S1")
319 | 
320 | 
321 | if __name__ == "__main__":
322 |     unittest.main()

 77 | class SummarizeSettingConditioningTest(unittest.TestCase):
 78 |     def setUp(self) -> None:
 79 |         self.outcomes = [
 80 |             # established, fired + disclosed
 81 |             _outcome(
 82 |                 1, established=True, total_infections=40,
 83 |                 first_fired_day=3, first_disclosed_day=7,
 84 |                 infections_at_first_disclosure=20, finest_disclosed_level=ScopeLevel.SUITE,
 85 |             ),
 86 |             _outcome(
 87 |                 2, established=True, total_infections=60,
 88 |                 first_fired_day=5, first_disclosed_day=9,
 89 |                 infections_at_first_disclosure=30, finest_disclosed_level=ScopeLevel.FLOOR,
 90 |             ),
 91 |             # established, detector never fired (a miss)
 92 |             _outcome(3, established=True, total_infections=15),
 93 |             # not established, one fired (false alarm), one silent
 94 |             _outcome(4, established=False, first_fired_day=2),
 95 |             _outcome(5, established=False),
 96 |         ]
 97 |         self.s = summarize_setting(self.outcomes, param_name="MIN_SCOPE_POPULATION", value=20)
 98 | 
 99 |     def test_counts(self) -> None:
100 |         self.assertEqual(self.s.n_established, 3)
101 |         self.assertEqual(self.s.n_non_established, 2)
102 |         self.assertEqual(self.s.n_fired, 2)
103 |         self.assertEqual(self.s.n_disclosed, 2)
104 | 
105 |     def test_total_infections_is_over_all_established(self) -> None:
106 |         self.assertAlmostEqual(self.s.mean_total_infections, (40 + 60 + 15) / 3)
107 | 
108 |     def test_delays_are_over_the_disclosed_subset_only(self) -> None:

111 |         self.assertAlmostEqual(self.s.mean_gap, ((7 - 3) + (9 - 5)) / 2)

122 |         # disclosed -> contributes its full total (15).

191 | class DegenerateReasonTest(unittest.TestCase):
192 |     POPS = (25, 75, 150)  # suite / floor / building=campus in the default structure
193 | 
194 |     def test_normal_config_is_not_degenerate(self) -> None:
195 |         self.assertIsNone(degenerate_reason_for(DisclosureConfig(), self.POPS))
196 | 
197 |     def test_min_population_above_whole_campus_is_degenerate(self) -> None:
198 |         reason = degenerate_reason_for(
199 |             DisclosureConfig(min_scope_population=200), self.POPS
200 |         )
201 |         self.assertIsNotNone(reason)
202 |         self.assertIn("200", reason)
203 | 
204 |     def test_tiny_report_fraction_is_degenerate(self) -> None:
205 |         # floor(25*0.08)=2, floor(75*0.08)=6 < ceil(sqrt(75))=9,
206 |         # floor(150*0.08)=12 < 13 -- no scope can clear both gates.
207 |         self.assertIsNotNone(
208 |             degenerate_reason_for(DisclosureConfig(max_report_fraction=0.08), self.POPS)
209 |         )

212 |         # floor n=75 admits up to floor(75*0.5)=37 reports and needs 25 -> OK.
213 |         self.assertIsNone(
214 |             degenerate_reason_for(DisclosureConfig(statistical_gate_floor=25), self.POPS)
215 |         )
216 | 
217 |     def test_adjacent_duplicate_rows_skips_degenerate_rows(self) -> None:
218 |         a = summarize_setting([], param_name="p", value=1, degenerate=True, degenerate_reason="x")
219 |         b = summarize_setting([], param_name="p", value=2, degenerate=True, degenerate_reason="x")
220 |         self.assertEqual(adjacent_duplicate_rows([a, b]), [])

223 | class RunOnceIntegrationTest(unittest.TestCase):
224 |     def test_disclosure_fields_are_consistent(self) -> None:
225 |         outcome = run_once(2, days=20)
226 |         if outcome.first_disclosed_day is None:
227 |             self.assertIsNone(outcome.infections_at_first_disclosure)
228 |             self.assertIsNone(outcome.finest_disclosed_level)
229 |         else:
230 |             self.assertIsNotNone(outcome.infections_at_first_disclosure)
231 |             self.assertIsInstance(outcome.finest_disclosed_level, ScopeLevel)
232 |             # disclosure cannot precede firing
233 |             self.assertLessEqual(outcome.first_fired_day, outcome.first_disclosed_day)

236 | class SweepIntegrationTest(unittest.TestCase):
237 |     def setUp(self) -> None:
238 |         self.results = run_disclosure_sweeps(seeds=list(range(1, 13)), days=18)
239 | 
240 |     def test_three_sweeps_each_with_the_right_row_count(self) -> None:
241 |         self.assertEqual(
242 |             set(self.results.sweeps),
243 |             {"MIN_SCOPE_POPULATION", "STATISTICAL_GATE_FLOOR", "MAX_REPORT_FRACTION"},
244 |         )
245 |         self.assertEqual(len(self.results.sweeps["MIN_SCOPE_POPULATION"]), 4)
246 |         self.assertEqual(len(self.results.sweeps["STATISTICAL_GATE_FLOOR"]), 5)
247 |         self.assertEqual(len(self.results.sweeps["MAX_REPORT_FRACTION"]), 3)
248 | 
249 |     def test_establishment_is_disclosure_independent(self) -> None:
250 |         # Every row of every sweep must reflect the same established count
251 |         # (establishment depends only on the simulated epidemic).
252 |         n_established = {
253 |             s.n_established
254 |             for summaries in self.results.sweeps.values()
255 |             for s in summaries
256 |         }
257 |         self.assertEqual(len(n_established), 1)
258 |         only = n_established.pop()
259 |         self.assertAlmostEqual(
260 |             1 - only / 12, self.results.fizzle_rate
261 |         )
262 | 
263 |     def test_false_alarm_and_fire_delay_are_detection_side_constants(self) -> None:
264 |         self.assertEqual(self.results.n_established + self.results.non_established, 12)
265 |         # control fire delay is defined whenever at least one established
266 |         # run fired
267 |         if self.results.n_fired:
268 |             self.assertIsNotNone(self.results.control_fire_delay)
269 | 
270 |     def test_impossible_min_population_row_is_marked_degenerate(self) -> None:
271 |         rows = self.results.sweeps["MIN_SCOPE_POPULATION"]
272 |         self.assertTrue(rows[-1].degenerate)  # value 200, campus is 150
273 |         self.assertFalse(rows[0].degenerate)  # value 20
274 | 
275 |     def test_tighter_gate_never_discloses_more_than_a_looser_one(self) -> None:
276 |         # Within the MIN_SCOPE_POPULATION sweep (monotone tightening),
277 |         # n_disclosed should be non-increasing.
278 |         counts = [s.n_disclosed for s in self.results.sweeps["MIN_SCOPE_POPULATION"]]
279 |         self.assertEqual(counts, sorted(counts, reverse=True))
280 | 
281 | 
282 | if __name__ == "__main__":
283 |     unittest.main()

tests/test_intake.py

  1 | """Intake submission-cap tests (rule 11).
  2 | 
  3 | Boundary coverage: exactly at the cap, one over, the reset conditions
  4 | (a fresh session_id, and the per-day counter rolling at midnight), the
  5 | session_id=None exemption, and that a rejection is surfaced (report kept
  6 | plus a reason) rather than silently dropped.
  7 | """
  8 | 
  9 | from __future__ import annotations
 10 | 
 11 | import unittest
 12 | from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
 13 | 
 14 | from microcluster.config import IntakeConfig
 15 | from microcluster.engine import analyze
 16 | from microcluster.intake import enforce_submission_caps
 17 | from microcluster.models import (
 18 |     Category,
 19 |     Onset,
 20 |     Report,
 21 |     Scope,
 22 |     ScopeLevel,
 23 |     ScopeRegistry,
 24 | )
 25 | 
 26 | _DAY0 = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
 27 | _CFG = IntakeConfig(session_submission_cap=3, daily_submission_cap=10)
 28 | 
 29 | 
 30 | def _report(session_id: str | None, *, when: datetime = _DAY0, loc: str = "S1") -> Report:
 31 |     return Report(
 32 |         category=Category.GENERAL,
 33 |         symptom="fever",
 34 |         onset=Onset.TODAY,
 35 |         location_id=loc,
 36 |         submitted_at=when,
 37 |         session_id=session_id,
 38 |     )
 39 |

 41 | class SessionCapTest(unittest.TestCase):
 42 |     def test_reports_up_to_the_cap_are_all_accepted(self) -> None:
 43 |         reports = [_report("s") for _ in range(_CFG.session_submission_cap)]
 44 |         result = enforce_submission_caps(reports, config=_CFG)
 45 |         self.assertEqual(len(result.accepted), 3)
 46 |         self.assertEqual(result.rejected, ())
 47 | 
 48 |     def test_one_over_the_cap_is_rejected_not_dropped(self) -> None:
 49 |         reports = [_report("s") for _ in range(_CFG.session_submission_cap + 1)]
 50 |         result = enforce_submission_caps(reports, config=_CFG)
 51 |         self.assertEqual(len(result.accepted), 3)
 52 |         self.assertEqual(result.n_rejected, 1)
 53 |         rej = result.rejected[0]
 54 |         self.assertIs(rej.report, reports[-1])          # the report is kept
 55 |         self.assertIn("session submission cap", rej.reason)  # with a reason
 56 | 
 57 |     def test_the_earliest_reports_win_when_a_session_is_over_cap(self) -> None:
 58 |         early = [_report("s", when=_DAY0 + timedelta(minutes=i)) for i in range(3)]
 59 |         late = _report("s", when=_DAY0 + timedelta(hours=5))
 60 |         result = enforce_submission_caps([*early, late], config=_CFG)
 61 |         self.assertEqual(list(result.accepted), early)
 62 |         self.assertEqual(result.rejected[0].report, late)
 63 | 
 64 |     def test_a_fresh_session_id_resets_the_allowance(self) -> None:
 65 |         reports = [_report("s1") for _ in range(4)] + [_report("s2") for _ in range(3)]
 66 |         result = enforce_submission_caps(reports, config=_CFG)
 67 |         # s1: 3 in, 1 out.  s2: a different key, all 3 in.
 68 |         self.assertEqual(len(result.accepted), 6)
 69 |         self.assertEqual(result.n_rejected, 1)
 70 | 
 71 |     def test_none_session_id_is_never_rate_limited(self) -> None:

 73 |         result = enforce_submission_caps(reports, config=_CFG)
 74 |         self.assertEqual(len(result.accepted), 50)
 75 |         self.assertEqual(result.rejected, ())
 76 | 
 77 |     def test_accepted_order_is_preserved(self) -> None:
 78 |         reports = [
 79 |             _report(None, loc="A"),
 80 |             _report("s", loc="B"),
 81 |             _report(None, loc="C"),
 82 |             _report("s", loc="D"),
 83 |         ]
 84 |         accepted = enforce_submission_caps(reports, config=_CFG).accepted
 85 |         self.assertEqual([r.location_id for r in accepted], ["A", "B", "C", "D"])

 90 |         cfg = IntakeConfig(session_submission_cap=100, daily_submission_cap=4)
 91 |         reports = [_report("s", when=_DAY0 + timedelta(minutes=i)) for i in range(6)]

 99 |         day1 = [_report("s", when=_DAY0 + timedelta(minutes=i)) for i in range(6)]

22 |     kwargs = dict(
23 |         category=Category.RESPIRATORY,
24 |         symptom="cough",
25 |         onset=Onset.TODAY,
26 |         location_id="X",
27 |         submitted_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=1),
28 |     )

33 | class ReportValidationTest(unittest.TestCase):

37 |         self.assertIn("not in the fixed checklist", str(ctx.exception))

42 |         with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
43 |             _report(category=Category.RESPIRATORY, symptom="diarrhea")

51 |             _report(onset="a while ago")  # type: ignore[arg-type]

54 |         for category, symptoms in CHECKLIST.items():

tests/test_motion_constraints.py

 1 | """CLAUDE.md UI CONSTRAINTS, "Motion" -- the durable version of the ad hoc
 2 | grep that used to just ban transition/animation outright.
 3 | 
 4 | Motion is now permitted narrowly (state-change and interaction feedback,
 5 | capped at 400ms, never looping; one decorative exception). What must still
 6 | be impossible -- gradients, shadows, glassmorphism/blur, and anything that
 7 | loops -- is checked here instead of by a blanket "no transition" grep, so
 8 | this suite has to be updated if the rule changes, not silently bypassed.
 9 | """
10 | 
11 | from __future__ import annotations
12 | 
13 | import re
14 | import unittest
15 | from pathlib import Path
16 | 
17 | WEB = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "web"
18 | 
19 | # still an absolute ban, with or without motion.
20 | _BANNED_RAW = (
21 |     r"linear-gradient",
22 |     r"radial-gradient",
23 |     r"conic-gradient",
24 |     r"box-shadow",
25 |     r"text-shadow",
26 |     r"backdrop-filter",
27 |     r"filter:\s*blur",
28 |     r"\binfinite\b",          # looping animation/animation-iteration-count
29 | )
30 | _BANNED_FONT = r"font-family:[^;]*(Inter|Geist|Space Grotesk)"
31 | 
32 | _FOCUS_RING_RULE = (
33 |     "a:focus-visible, button:focus-visible, input:focus-visible,\n"
34 |     "select:focus-visible, summary:focus-visible {\n"
35 |     "  outline: 2px solid var(--ink);\n"
36 |     "  outline-offset: 2px;\n"
37 |     "}"
38 | )
39 | 
40 | 
41 | class BannedPatternsTest(unittest.TestCase):
42 |     def test_css_has_no_banned_decoration_or_looping_motion(self) -> None:
43 |         css = (WEB / "style.css").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
44 |         for pattern in _BANNED_RAW:
45 |             self.assertIsNone(re.search(pattern, css, re.I), f"found banned pattern: {pattern}")
46 |         self.assertIsNone(re.search(_BANNED_FONT, css, re.I), "banned font-family")
47 | 
48 |     def test_html_pages_have_no_banned_decoration(self) -> None:
49 |         for name in ("index.html", "privacy.html", "terms.html"):
50 |             html = (WEB / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
51 |             for pattern in _BANNED_RAW:
52 |                 self.assertIsNone(re.search(pattern, html, re.I), f"{name}: {pattern}")
53 | 
54 |     def test_no_em_dash_in_ui_copy_or_code(self) -> None:
55 |         for path in list(WEB.glob("*.html")) + list(WEB.glob("*.css")) + list(WEB.glob("*.js")):
56 |             self.assertNotIn("—", path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), path.name)
57 | 
58 | 
59 | class MotionCapTest(unittest.TestCase):
60 |     def test_every_transition_and_animation_duration_is_capped_at_400ms(self) -> None:
61 |         css = (WEB / "style.css").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
62 |         durations = [int(m) for m in re.findall(r"(\d{2,4})ms", css)]
63 |         # sanity check the test itself is looking at the motion section at all
64 |         self.assertGreaterEqual(len(durations), 5, "expected the new motion rules to be present")
65 |         for ms in durations:
66 |             self.assertLessEqual(ms, 400, f"{ms}ms exceeds the 400ms cap")
67 | 
68 |     def test_reduced_motion_neutralises_every_new_transition_and_animation(self) -> None:
69 |         css = (WEB / "style.css").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
70 |         match = re.search(
71 |             r"@media \(prefers-reduced-motion:\s*reduce\)\s*\{(.*?)\n\}", css, re.S
72 |         )
73 |         self.assertIsNotNone(match, "no prefers-reduced-motion: reduce block found")
74 |         block = match.group(1)
75 |         self.assertIn("transition: none", block)
76 |         self.assertIn("animation: none", block)
77 |         # the pulse ring and the flashed gate row must be fully suppressed,
78 |         # not just left with an un-eased snap
79 |         self.assertIn(".agent.pulse::before", block)
80 |         self.assertIn("tr.verdict-changed", block)
81 | 
82 | 
83 | class FocusRingUntouchedTest(unittest.TestCase):
84 |     def test_focus_visible_ring_is_byte_identical(self) -> None:
85 |         css = (WEB / "style.css").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
86 |         self.assertIn(_FOCUS_RING_RULE, css, "the focus-visible ring rule was changed")
87 | 
88 | 
89 | if __name__ == "__main__":
90 |     unittest.main()

 94 | class DelayRecordingTest(unittest.TestCase):
 95 |     def test_first_fired_and_first_disclosed_are_recorded_and_ordered(self) -> None:
 96 |         config = SimulationConfig(
 97 |             suite_transmission_probability=1.0,
 98 |             reporting_probability_min=1.0,
 99 |             reporting_probability_max=1.0,
100 |             background_noise_daily_rate=0.0,
101 |         )
102 |         result = run_with_detection(seed=1, days=20, config=config)
103 | 
104 |         fired_day = next(r.day for r in result.daily_records if r.detection_fired)
105 |         disclosed_day = next(
106 |             r.day for r in result.daily_records if r.disclosed_scope_id is not None
107 |         )
108 |         last = result.daily_records[-1]
109 | 
110 |         # The running fields on the final record must match what actually
111 |         # happened, and disclosure can never precede firing.
112 |         self.assertEqual(last.first_fired_day, fired_day)
113 |         self.assertEqual(last.first_disclosed_day, disclosed_day)
114 |         self.assertLessEqual(fired_day, disclosed_day)
115 | 
116 |     def test_before_either_happens_both_fields_are_none(self) -> None:
117 |         result = run_with_detection(seed=1, days=1)
118 |         first = result.daily_records[0]
119 |         if not first.detection_fired:
120 |             self.assertIsNone(first.first_fired_day)
121 |         if first.disclosed_scope_id is None:
122 |             self.assertIsNone(first.first_disclosed_day)
123 |

125 |         config = SimulationConfig(
126 |             reporting_probability_min=0.0,
127 |             reporting_probability_max=0.0,
128 |             background_noise_daily_rate=0.0,
129 |         )
130 |         result = run_with_detection(seed=1, days=20, config=config)
131 |         last = result.daily_records[-1]
132 |         self.assertIsNone(last.first_fired_day)

135 | 
136 | class ScopeStabilityAcrossARunTest(unittest.TestCase):
137 |     """config.SIBLING_SWITCH_MARGIN, exercised over a full simulated run
138 |     rather than a single evaluate() call."""
139 | 
140 |     def test_distinct_disclosed_scopes_stay_small_across_the_default_seeds(self) -> None:
141 |         # The default structure has 6 suites, 2 floors, 1 building, 1
142 |         # campus -- 10 possible scopes. Without stability, a wandering
143 |         # outbreak can (and, before this change, did) name most of them
144 |         # over a 30-day run. With it, each run should settle on very few.
145 |         for seed in DEFAULT_SMOKE_SEEDS:
146 |             with self.subTest(seed=seed):
147 |                 result = run_with_detection(seed=seed, days=30)
148 |                 distinct = result.distinct_disclosed_scopes()
149 |                 self.assertLessEqual(
150 |                     len(distinct), 3, f"seed {seed} disclosed too many scopes: {distinct}"
151 |                 )
152 | 
153 |     def test_a_named_scope_persists_across_consecutive_disclosing_days(self) -> None:
154 |         # Once something is disclosed, most consecutive disclosing days
155 |         # should repeat it rather than naming something new each time.
156 |         found_a_run_with_multiple_disclosures = False
157 |         for seed in DEFAULT_SMOKE_SEEDS:
158 |             result = run_with_detection(seed=seed, days=30)
159 |             sequence = [r.disclosed_scope_id for r in result.daily_records if r.disclosed_scope_id]
160 |             if len(sequence) < 2:
161 |                 continue
162 |             found_a_run_with_multiple_disclosures = True
163 |             changes = sum(1 for a, b in zip(sequence, sequence[1:]) if a != b)
164 |             self.assertLess(
165 |                 changes, len(sequence), f"seed {seed}: every disclosing day changed scope"
166 |             )
167 |         self.assertTrue(found_a_run_with_multiple_disclosures)
168 | 
169 | 
170 | class TwoPhaseEquivalenceTest(unittest.TestCase):
171 |     """run_with_detection == simulate_and_report + analyze_report_stream.
172 |     The split exists so an experiment can simulate a seed once and replay
173 |     the analysis under many configs; it must not change the result."""
174 | 
175 |     def test_split_matches_the_combined_call(self) -> None:
176 |         combined = run_with_detection(seed=3, days=25)
177 | 
178 |         simulated = simulate_and_report(seed=3, days=25)
179 |         replayed = analyze_report_stream(simulated)
180 | 
181 |         self.assertEqual(combined.daily_records, replayed)
182 |         self.assertEqual(
183 |             [s.counts for s in combined.simulation.history],
184 |             [s.counts for s in simulated.simulation.history],
185 |         )
186 | 
187 |     def test_one_simulation_replayed_under_two_configs(self) -> None:
188 |         from microcluster.config import DisclosureConfig
189 | 
190 |         simulated = simulate_and_report(seed=4, days=25)
191 |         loose = analyze_report_stream(
192 |             simulated, disclosure_config=DisclosureConfig(min_scope_population=10)
193 |         )
194 |         strict = analyze_report_stream(
195 |             simulated, disclosure_config=DisclosureConfig(min_scope_population=200)
196 |         )
197 |         # Same epidemic, different disclosure gate -> the strict one names
198 |         # nothing, the loose one may.
199 |         self.assertTrue(all(r.disclosed_scope_id is None for r in strict))
200 |         self.assertEqual(len(loose), len(strict))
201 |

207 |         )
